     1→# GitHub Repo Onboarding — Dependency Scanner
     2→
     3→**Issue**: [#7 — Be able to connect to a GitHub repo and detect dependencies it has](https://github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/issues/7)
     4→
     5→**Date**: 2026-03-14
     6→
     7→## Overview
     8→
     9→Add a "Quick Onboard" feature that lets users point Changelogue at a GitHub repository, automatically detect its dependencies using an LLM, and selectively create tracked projects/sources for those dependencies.
    10→
    11→## Decisions
    12→
    13→| Decision | Choice | Rationale |
    14→|----------|--------|-----------|
    15→| Ecosystem scope | Multi-ecosystem via LLM | Gemini parses any manifest format without per-ecosystem parsers |
    16→| Repo access | GitHub API | Lighter than cloning; fetches only needed files |
    17→| Project mapping | User picks per dependency | Flexible — user chooses existing project or creates new |
    18→| LLM integration | Direct Gemini `genai.GenerateContent` call | Single-turn structured output — ADK-Go runner/session overhead is unnecessary |
    19→| UX entry point | Dedicated `/onboard` page | Clear onboarding flow, separate from project management |
    20→| Backend pattern | Async River job | Handles large repos; matches existing architecture |
    21→
    22→## Architecture
    23→
    24→### Data Flow
    25→
    26→```
    27→User enters repo URL
    28→  → POST /api/v1/onboard/scan
    29→    → River job: scan_dependencies
    30→      → GitHub API: fetch repo tree (using GITHUB_TOKEN from env if set)
    31→      → GitHub API: fetch dependency file contents
    32→      → Gemini genai.GenerateContent: parse dependencies (structured JSON output)
    33→      → Store results in onboard_scans table
    34→      → pg NOTIFY on release_events channel with scan_complete event
    35→    → Frontend polls GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id} for results
    36→  → User selects dependencies to track
    37→  → POST /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}/apply
    38→    → In a single DB transaction: create projects + sources for selections
    39→    → Trigger first poll for each source via IngestionService
    40→```
    41→
    42→### New Database Table
    43→
    44→```sql
    45→CREATE TABLE onboard_scans (
    46→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    47→    repo_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    48→    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    49→    results JSONB,
    50→    error TEXT,
    51→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    52→    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    53→    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ
    54→);
    55→```
    56→
    57→`results` schema (JSONB array):
    58→```json
    59→[
    60→  {
    61→    "name": "gorilla/mux",
    62→    "version": "v1.8.1",
    63→    "ecosystem": "go",
    64→    "upstream_repo": "github.com/gorilla/mux",
    65→    "provider": "github"
    66→  }
    67→]
    68→```
    69→
    70→### New API Endpoints
    71→
    72→**`POST /api/v1/onboard/scan`**
    73→- Request: `{ "repo_url": "https://github.com/org/repo" }` (or `"org/repo"`)
    74→- Validates: repo URL format, checks no active scan for same repo exists (status = pending/processing)
    75→- Response: `{ "data": { "id": "uuid", "status": "pending", "repo_url": "org/repo" } }`
    76→
    77→**`GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}`**
    78→- Response: `{ "data": { "id": "uuid", "status": "completed", "repo_url": "...", "results": [...], "completed_at": "..." } }`
    79→
    80→**`POST /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}/apply`**
    81→- Request:
    82→```json
    83→{
    84→  "selections": [
    85→    {
    86→      "dep_name": "gorilla/mux",
    87→      "upstream_repo": "github.com/gorilla/mux",
    88→      "provider": "github",
    89→      "project_id": "existing-uuid-or-null",
    90→      "new_project_name": "gorilla-mux"
    91→    }
    92→  ]
    93→}
    94→```
    95→- Validation: exactly one of `project_id` or `new_project_name` must be set per selection. If `new_project_name` conflicts with an existing project name, return 409 with the conflicting name.
    96→- All project/source creation happens in a **single database transaction** — if any creation fails, the entire batch rolls back.
    97→- After successful commit, triggers first poll for each created source via `IngestionService.PollSource()`.
    98→- Response: `{ "data": { "created_projects": [...], "created_sources": [...], "skipped": [...] } }`
    99→- `skipped` includes entries where source already exists (UNIQUE constraint on provider+repository).
   100→
   101→### OnboardStore Interface
   102→
   103→```go
   104→type OnboardStore interface {
   105→    CreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)
   106→    GetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)
   107→    UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id string, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error
   108→    ApplyOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, scanID string, selections []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error)
   109→}
   110→```
   111→
   112→`ApplyOnboardScan` operates within a single transaction.
   113→
   114→### New River Job
   115→
   116→Added to existing `internal/queue/jobs.go`:
   117→
   118→```go
   119→type ScanDependenciesJobArgs struct {
   120→    ScanID string `json:"scan_id"`
   121→}
   122→
   123→func (ScanDependenciesJobArgs) Kind() string { return "scan_dependencies" }
   124→```
   125→
   126→Worker steps:
   127→1. Load scan record from DB to get `repo_url`
   128→2. Update scan status to `processing`, set `started_at`
   129→3. Parse repo owner/name from `repo_url`
   130→4. Fetch repo tree via `GET /repos/{owner}/{repo}/git/trees/{default_branch}?recursive=1` (uses `GITHUB_TOKEN` from env for auth, same pattern as `internal/ingestion/github.go`)
   131→5. Filter tree entries for known dependency file patterns (go.mod, package.json, requirements.txt, Cargo.toml, pyproject.toml, Gemfile, pom.xml, build.gradle, etc.)
   132→6. Fetch contents of matched files via `GET /repos/{owner}/{repo}/contents/{path}` (same `GITHUB_TOKEN` auth)
   133→7. Call Gemini via direct `genai.GenerateContent` with file contents (see LLM section below)
   134→8. Parse structured JSON response, store in `onboard_scans.results`
   135→9. Update status to `completed`, set `completed_at`
   136→10. Send `pg_notify('release_events', ...)` with scan_complete event
   137→
   138→### GitHub Authentication
   139→
   140→Uses the same `GITHUB_TOKEN` environment variable as the existing `GitHubSource` in `internal/ingestion/github.go`. All GitHub API requests (tree, contents) set `Authorization: Bearer $GITHUB_TOKEN` when the env var is present. No per-scan token support — uses server-wide token.
   141→
   142→### LLM Integration — Direct Gemini Call
   143→
   144→Uses `genai.GenerateContent` directly (not ADK-Go runner/session). This is a single-turn structured output extraction — ADK-Go's agent orchestration adds unnecessary overhead.
   145→
   146→Reuses model configuration from `internal/agent/model.go` for client setup and model selection.
   147→
   148→**Prompt**:
   149→```
   150→You are a dependency extraction agent. Given the contents of dependency/manifest
   151→files from a software project, extract all dependencies.
   152→
   153→For each dependency, return:
   154→- name: the package/library name
   155→- version: the version constraint or pinned version
   156→- ecosystem: one of "go", "npm", "pypi", "cargo", "rubygems", "maven", "gradle", "docker", "other"
   157→- upstream_repo: your best guess at the canonical GitHub repository URL (e.g., "github.com/gorilla/mux")
   158→- provider: the Changelogue provider to use for release tracking. Use "github" for repos
   159→  with GitHub releases, "dockerhub" for Docker images, "gitlab" for GitLab repos,
   160→  "ecr_public" for AWS ECR images. When unsure, default to "github".
   161→
   162→Return ONLY a JSON array. No explanations.
   163→```
   164→
   165→### SSE / Real-Time Updates
   166→
   167→Reuses the existing `release_events` PostgreSQL NOTIFY channel. The scan worker sends a notification with event type `scan_complete` and the scan ID as payload. The frontend on the onboard page polls `GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}` on an interval (2s) while status is `pending` or `processing` — simpler than wiring up SSE for a one-off wait.
   168→
   169→## Frontend
   170→
   171→### New Page: `/onboard`
   172→
   173→**Sidebar entry**: "Quick Onboard" — placed after "Dashboard" and before "Projects" in the sidebar.
   174→
   175→**Step 1 — Input**: Text field for GitHub repo URL + "Scan" button.
   176→
   177→**Step 2 — Scanning**: Loading state with spinner. Polls `GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}` every 2 seconds until status changes.
   178→
   179→**Step 3 — Results**: Table with columns:
   180→- Checkbox (select/deselect, all selected by default)
   181→- Dependency name
   182→- Version (detected)
   183→- Ecosystem badge (Go, npm, Python, etc.)
   184→- Upstream source (GitHub repo URL)
   185→- Project assignment: dropdown — "Create new project" (default, uses dep name) or select existing project
   186→
   187→**Step 4 — Apply**: "Track Selected" button. Shows success summary with links to created projects. Shows skipped items (duplicates) with explanation.
   188→
   189→### Frontend API Client Additions
   190→
   191→```typescript
   192→export const onboard = {
   193→  scan: (repoUrl: string) =>
   194→    request<ApiResponse<OnboardScan>>("/onboard/scan", {
   195→      method: "POST",
   196→      body: JSON.stringify({ repo_url: repoUrl }),
   197→    }),
   198→  getScan: (id: string) =>
   199→    request<ApiResponse<OnboardScan>>(`/onboard/scans/${id}`),
   200→  apply: (id: string, selections: OnboardSelection[]) =>
   201→    request<ApiResponse<OnboardApplyResult>>(`/onboard/scans/${id}/apply`, {
   202→      method: "POST",
   203→      body: JSON.stringify({ selections }),
   204→    }),
   205→};
   206→```
   207→
   208→## Error Handling
   209→
   210→| Scenario | Behavior |
   211→|----------|----------|
   212→| Private repo without token | Scan fails: "GitHub API returned 404. Set GITHUB_TOKEN for private repos." |
   213→| No dependency files found | LLM returns empty array → UI shows "No dependencies detected" |
   214→| LLM returns malformed JSON | River retries (max 3 attempts); if all fail, status = `failed` with error |
   215→| Duplicate source (provider+repo) | Apply endpoint skips, includes in `skipped` array with reason |
   216→| Duplicate active scan (same repo) | POST /scan returns 409 with existing scan ID |
   217→| GitHub rate limit (429) | Worker retries with River's backoff; surfaces error after max retries |
   218→| Large repo tree | Only fetches files matching dependency file name patterns |
   219→| Apply validation: both/neither project_id and new_project_name | Returns 400 with field-level error |
   220→| Apply: new_project_name conflicts with existing | Returns 409 with conflicting name |
   221→| Apply: partial failure | Entire batch rolls back (single transaction) |
   222→
   223→## Testing
   224→
   225→- **Unit tests**: Scan worker with mocked GitHub API + mocked Gemini client
   226→- **Unit tests**: Apply endpoint — creates projects/sources correctly, handles duplicates, validates selections
   227→- **Unit tests**: Apply endpoint — transaction rollback on partial failure
   228→- **Unit tests**: OnboardStore — CreateOnboardScan, GetOnboardScan, UpdateOnboardScanStatus, ApplyOnboardScan
   229→- **Integration test**: End-to-end scan of a known public repo
   230→- **Frontend**: Manual testing of the onboard flow
   231→
   232→## Files to Create/Modify
   233→
   234→### New Files
   235→- `internal/onboard/scanner.go` — GitHub API file fetcher + tree walker
   236→- `internal/onboard/gemini.go` — Direct Gemini call for dependency extraction
   237→- `internal/onboard/worker.go` — River job worker
   238→- `internal/api/onboard.go` — API handlers (scan, get scan, apply) + OnboardStore interface
   239→- `web/app/onboard/page.tsx` — Onboarding page
   240→
   241→### Modified Files
   242→- `internal/db/migrations.go` — Add `onboard_scans` table
   243→- `internal/api/server.go` — Register new routes
   244→- `internal/api/pgstore.go` — Add OnboardStore implementation methods
   245→- `internal/queue/jobs.go` — Add `ScanDependenciesJobArgs`
   246→- `cmd/server/main.go` — Register scan worker with River
   247→- `web/lib/api/client.ts` — Add onboard API functions
   248→- `web/lib/api/types.ts` — Add OnboardScan, OnboardSelection, OnboardApplyResult types
   249→- `web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx` — Add "Quick Onboard" entry after Dashboard
   250→

## COMPREHENSIVE CODE PATTERNS REFERENCE

Here are all the exact code patterns from the ReleaseBeacon/Changelogue codebase organized by layer:

### 1. QUEUE & JOBS (`internal/queue/jobs.go`) — Lines 1-27

**River JobArgs Pattern:**

```go
package queue

import "github.com/riverqueue/river"

// Your River job args struct must:
// - Include json struct tags for all fields
// - Implement Kind() string method returning unique job type name
// - Include `var _ river.JobArgs = YourJobArgs{}`

type NotifyJobArgs struct {
	ReleaseID string `json:"release_id"`
	SourceID  string `json:"source_id"`
}

func (NotifyJobArgs) Kind() string { return "notify_release" }
var _ river.JobArgs = NotifyJobArgs{}

// Second example (agent):
type AgentJobArgs struct {
	AgentRunID string `json:"agent_run_id"`
	ProjectID  string `json:"project_id"`
	Version    string `json:"version"`
}

func (AgentJobArgs) Kind() string { return "agent_run" }
var _ river.JobArgs = AgentJobArgs{}
```

**For ScanDependenciesJobArgs, follow this exact pattern:**
- Add struct with string fields tagged with `json:"field_name"`
- Implement `Kind()` returning a unique string like `"scan_dependencies"`
- Add var assertion line

### 2. DATABASE MIGRATIONS (`internal/db/migrations.go`) — Lines 12-177

**Migration Pattern:**

```go
const schema = `
-- Table with standard columns (UUID PK, timestamps)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS onboard_scans (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    -- Your columns here
    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Index pattern
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_onboard_scans_project_id ON onboard_scans(project_id);
`
```

**RunMigrations function (lines 180-223):**
- Calls `rivermigrate.Migrate()` first for River schema
- Then `pool.Exec(ctx, schema)` for your schema
- Wrap errors with context: `fmt.Errorf("your migrations: %w", err)`
- All migrations are idempotent (use IF NOT EXISTS)
- ALTER TABLE statements for schema updates are separated from initial CREATE

---

### 3. API ROUTE REGISTRATION (`internal/api/server.go`) — Lines 32-137

**Route Registration Pattern:**

```go
// Line 1: Create handler instance (injectable dependencies)
handler := NewYourHandler(deps.YourStore, deps.OtherDep)

// Line 2: Register routes with middleware chain
mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/your-path/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(handler.Get)))
mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/your-path", chain(http.HandlerFunc(handler.Create)))
mux.Handle("PATCH /api/v1/your-path/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(handler.Update)))
mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/your-path/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(handler.Delete)))
```

**Dependencies struct (lines 12-30):**
- Each Store interface is added as a field in Dependencies
- All stores must be added to Dependencies struct AND to RegisterRoutes call in main.go (lines 120-138)

---

### 4. PGSTORE PATTERNS (`internal/api/pgstore.go`) — Lines 18-100 (struct) + 554-602 (methods)

**PgStore Struct:**

```go
type PgStore struct {
	pool  *pgxpool.Pool
	river *river.Client[pgx.Tx]
}

func NewPgStore(pool *pgxpool.Pool, riverClient *river.Client[pgx.Tx]) *PgStore {
	return &PgStore{pool: pool, river: riverClient}
}

func (s *PgStore) SetRiverClient(rc *river.Client[pgx.Tx]) {
	s.river = rc
}
```

**Create Method Pattern (lines 554-561):**

```go
func (s *PgStore) CreateSubscription(ctx context.Context, sub *models.Subscription) error {
	return s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
		`INSERT INTO subscriptions (channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, version_filter)
		 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)
		 RETURNING id, created_at`,
		sub.ChannelID, sub.Type, sub.SourceID, sub.ProjectID, sub.VersionFilter,
	).Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.CreatedAt)
}
```

**Get Method Pattern (lines 591-602):**

```go
func (s *PgStore) GetSubscription(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Subscription, error) {
	var sub models.Subscription
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
		`SELECT id, channel_id, type, source_id, project_id, COALESCE(version_filter,''), created_at
		 FROM subscriptions WHERE id = $1`, id,
	).Scan(&sub.ID, &sub.ChannelID, &sub.Type, &sub.SourceID,
		&sub.ProjectID, &sub.VersionFilter, &sub.CreatedAt)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &sub, nil
}
```

**Complex Get with JOINs (lines 494-524):**
- Use LEFT JOINs for optional relationships
- Use LATERAL for correlated subqueries
- Use COALESCE for nullable fields with defaults
- Scan into model struct fields in same order as SELECT

type TodosStore interface {
	ListTodos(ctx context.Context, status string, page, perPage int, aggregated bool) ([]models.Todo, int, error)
	GetTodo(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Todo, error)
	AcknowledgeTodo(ctx context.Context, id string, cascade bool) error
	ResolveTodo(ctx context.Context, id string, cascade bool) error
	ReopenTodo(ctx context.Context, id string) error
}

**Key patterns:**
- All methods take `ctx context.Context` as first parameter
- ID lookups return `(*Model, error)` or error
- List methods return `([]Model, totalCount, error)`
- Action methods that don't return data return just `error`
- Include query parameters (page, perPage, filters)

```go
type AgentWorker struct {
	river.WorkerDefaults[queue.AgentJobArgs]
	orchestrator *Orchestrator
	store        OrchestratorStore
}

func NewAgentWorker(orchestrator *Orchestrator, store OrchestratorStore) *AgentWorker {
	return &AgentWorker{
		orchestrator: orchestrator,
		store:        store,
	}
}

// Timeout override (line 36):
func (w *AgentWorker) Timeout(_ *river.Job[queue.AgentJobArgs]) time.Duration {
	return 5 * time.Minute
}

// Work method (line 44):
func (w *AgentWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.AgentJobArgs]) error {
	slog.Info("agent worker picked up job",
		"job_id", job.ID,
		"agent_run_id", job.Args.AgentRunID,
		"attempt", job.Attempt,
	)
	// ... implementation
	return nil
}
```

**Key patterns:**
- Embed `river.WorkerDefaults[YourJobArgs]`
- Implement `Timeout()` method for custom timeout
- Implement `Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[YourJobArgs]) error`
- Log with slog.Info/Error with structured key-value pairs
- Return `river.JobSnooze(duration)` to defer job retry

### 7. LLM MODEL CONFIGURATION (`internal/agent/model.go`) — Lines 14-49

```go
type LLMConfig struct {
	Provider      string // "gemini" or "openai"
	Model         string // e.g. "gemini-2.5-flash", "gpt-5.2"
	GoogleAPIKey  string
	OpenAIAPIKey  string
	OpenAIBaseURL string // defaults to https://api.openai.com/v1
}

func NewLLMModel(ctx context.Context, cfg LLMConfig, wsConfig *oaimodel.WebSearch) (model.LLM, error) {
	switch cfg.Provider {
	case "gemini", "":
		if cfg.GoogleAPIKey == "" {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("GOOGLE_API_KEY is required for gemini provider")
		}
		return gemini.NewModel(ctx, cfg.Model, &genai.ClientConfig{
			APIKey: cfg.GoogleAPIKey,
		})

	case "openai":
		return oaimodel.NewModel(ctx, cfg.Model, oaimodel.Config{
			APIKey:  cfg.OpenAIAPIKey,
			BaseURL: cfg.OpenAIBaseURL,
		}, wsConfig)

	default:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown LLM provider: %q (supported: gemini, openai)", cfg.Provider)
	}
}
```

### 8. GITHUB API CALL PATTERN (`internal/ingestion/github.go`) — Lines 44-100

```go
func (s *GitHubSource) FetchNewReleases(ctx context.Context) ([]IngestionResult, error) {
	url := fmt.Sprintf("%s/repos/%s/releases?per_page=30", s.baseURL, s.repository)

	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, url, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
	}
	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json")

	if token := os.Getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN"); token != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
	}

	resp, err := s.client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("fetch releases: %w", err)
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected status: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	var releases []ghRelease
	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&releases); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode releases: %w", err)
	}

	results := make([]IngestionResult, 0, len(releases))
	for _, rel := range releases {
		if rel.Draft {
			continue
		}
		if rel.TagName == "" {
			continue
		}

		ts, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, rel.PublishedAt)

		prerelease := "false"
		if rel.Prerelease {
			prerelease = "true"
		}

		results = append(results, IngestionResult{
			Repository: s.repository,
			RawVersion: rel.TagName,
			Changelog:  rel.Body,
			Metadata: map[string]string{
				"release_url": rel.HTMLURL,
				"prerelease":  prerelease,
			},
			Timestamp: ts,
		})
	}
	return results, nil
}
```

**Key patterns:**
- Use `http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, ...)`
- Set Accept and Authorization headers
- Check `resp.StatusCode` before decoding
- Use `json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode()`
- Filter/validate data in loop before accumulating results
- Use `time.Parse(time.RFC3339, ...)` for timestamps

### 9. WORKER REGISTRATION (`cmd/server/main.go`) — Lines 58-95

```go
// Create store first (with nil river client initially)
pgStore := api.NewPgStore(pool, nil)

// Register workers
workers := river.NewWorkers()
notifyWorker := routing.NewNotifyWorker(pgStore, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
river.AddWorker(workers, notifyWorker)

// Optional: create agent worker if LLM configured
llmConfig := agentpkg.LLMConfig{
	Provider:      envOr("LLM_PROVIDER", "gemini"),
	Model:         envOr("LLM_MODEL", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
	GoogleAPIKey:  os.Getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
	OpenAIAPIKey:  os.Getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	OpenAIBaseURL: os.Getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL"),
}
agentOrchestrator, err := agentpkg.NewOrchestrator(pgStore, llmConfig, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
if err != nil {
	slog.Warn("agent orchestrator not available — agent jobs will not be processed", "err", err)
} else {
	agentWorker := agentpkg.NewAgentWorker(agentOrchestrator, pgStore)
	river.AddWorker(workers, agentWorker)
	slog.Info("agent worker registered")
}

// Create river client with registered workers
riverClient, err := queue.NewRiverClient(pool, workers)
// ...

// Set river client on store AFTER creating river client
pgStore.SetRiverClient(riverClient)

// Register all stores in Dependencies struct (lines 120-138)
api.RegisterRoutes(mux, api.Dependencies{
	DB:                    pool,
	ProjectsStore:         pgStore,
	ReleasesStore:         pgStore,
	SubscriptionsStore:    pgStore,
	SourcesStore:          pgStore,
	ChannelsStore:         pgStore,
	ContextSourcesStore:   pgStore,
	SemanticReleasesStore: pgStore,
	AgentStore:            pgStore,
	TodosStore:            pgStore,  // Add here when you create it
	PublicURL:             os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"),
	KeyStore:              pgStore,
	HealthChecker:         pgStore,
	Broadcaster:           broadcaster,
	NoAuth:                noAuth,
	IngestionService:      svc,
	HTTPClient:            http.DefaultClient,
})
```

### 10. SIDEBAR NAVIGATION (`web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx`) — Lines 18-111

```tsx
const navItems = [
  { href: "/", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { href: "/projects", label: "Projects", icon: FolderKanban },
  { href: "/todo", label: "Todo", icon: ListTodo },
  { href: "/releases", label: "Releases", icon: Package },
  { href: "/channels", label: "Channels", icon: Megaphone },
  { href: "/subscriptions", label: "Subscriptions", icon: Bell },
];

export function Sidebar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  return (
    <nav className="flex-1 pt-2">
      {navItems.map((item) => {
        const isActive =
          item.href === "/"
            ? pathname === "/"
            : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
        return (
          <Link
            key={item.href}
            href={item.href}
            className={cn(
              "flex items-center gap-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition-colors duration-150",
              expanded ? "pl-4 pr-3" : "justify-center px-0",
              isActive
                ? "text-white"
                : "text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-white"
            )}
          >
            <item.icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
            {expanded && <span>{item.label}</span>}
          </Link>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

**To add a new page:** Add entry to `navItems` array with `{ href: "/path", label: "Label", icon: IconComponent }`

### 11. API CLIENT PATTERN (`web/lib/api/client.ts`) — Lines 28-250

**Core request function (lines 28-39):**

```typescript
async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    ...init,
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
  }
  if (res.status === 204) return null as T;
  return res.json();
}
```

**API module pattern (lines 43-60 example):**

```typescript
export const projects = {
  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Project[]>>(`/projects?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
  get: (id: string) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`),
  create: (input: ProjectInput) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Project>>("/projects", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    }),
  update: (id: string, input: ProjectInput) =>
    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`, {
      method: "PUT",
      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    }),
  delete: (id: string) =>
    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/projects/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
};
```

**For new endpoints:**
- Create new export const object with method functions
- Each function returns `request<TypeResponse>(path, options)`
- Always wrap response type with `ApiResponse<T>`

### 12. TYPESCRIPT TYPES (`web/lib/api/types.ts`) — Lines 1-275

**Response envelope (lines 5-20):**

```typescript
export interface ApiResponse<T> {
  data: T;
  meta?: ApiMeta;
  error?: ApiError;
}

export interface ApiMeta {
  page: number;
  per_page: number;
  total: number;
}

export interface ApiError {
  code: string;
  message: string;
}
```

**Domain model pattern (lines 24-46 example):**

```typescript
export interface Project {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  agent_prompt?: string;
  agent_rules?: AgentRules;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface ProjectInput {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  agent_prompt?: string;
  agent_rules?: AgentRules;
}
```

**Key patterns:**
- All IDs are `string` (UUIDs)
- All timestamps are ISO strings: `string`
- Use lowercase snake_case for JSON field names
- Create separate `Input` types for mutations
- Use `?` for optional fields
- Timestamps always: `created_at`, `updated_at`

### 13. MODEL STRUCT (`internal/models/release.go`) — Lines 1-27

```go
package models

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"time"
)

type Release struct {
	ID         string          `json:"id"`
	SourceID   string          `json:"source_id"`
	Version    string          `json:"version"`
	RawData    json.RawMessage `json:"raw_data,omitempty"`
	ReleasedAt *time.Time      `json:"released_at,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt  time.Time       `json:"created_at"`

	// Enriched fields from JOINed sources/projects tables.
	ProjectID   string `json:"project_id,omitempty"`
	ProjectName string `json:"project_name,omitempty"`
	Provider    string `json:"provider,omitempty"`
	Repository  string `json:"repository,omitempty"`
	Excluded    bool   `json:"excluded"`

	// Enriched fields from LEFT JOINed semantic_releases table.
	SemanticReleaseID      string `json:"semantic_release_id,omitempty"`
	SemanticReleaseStatus  string `json:"semantic_release_status,omitempty"`
	SemanticReleaseUrgency string `json:"semantic_release_urgency,omitempty"`
}
```

**Key patterns:**
- All IDs are `string` (UUID)
- Timestamps use `time.Time` for Go, or `string` for frontend (ISO format)
- Use `json.RawMessage` for flexible JSON fields
- Use pointers for nullable fields: `*time.Time`
- Use `omitempty` for optional JSON fields
- Comments explain data origin (enriched fields)

    48→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    49→);
    50→
    51→-- Source-level releases (detected from polling sources)
    52→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS releases (
    53→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    54→    source_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    55→    version VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    56→    raw_data JSONB,
    57→    released_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    58→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    59→    UNIQUE(source_id, version)
    60→);
    61→
    62→-- Project-level semantic releases (AI-generated, correlating source releases)
    63→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS semantic_releases (
    64→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    65→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    66→    version VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    67→    report JSONB,
    68→    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    69→    error TEXT,
    70→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    71→    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    72→    UNIQUE(project_id, version)
    73→);
    74→
    75→-- Join table: which source releases compose a semantic release
    76→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS semantic_release_sources (
    77→    semantic_release_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    78→    release_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    79→    PRIMARY KEY (semantic_release_id, release_id)
    80→);
    81→
    82→-- Notification channels (standalone)
    83→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_channels (
    84→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    85→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    86→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    87→    config JSONB NOT NULL,
    88→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    89→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    90→);
    91→
    92→-- Subscriptions: two types (source releases and semantic releases)
    93→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subscriptions (
    94→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    95→    channel_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES notification_channels(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    96→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release')),
    97→    source_id UUID REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    98→    project_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    99→    version_filter TEXT,
   100→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   101→    CHECK (
   102→        (type = 'source_release'   AND source_id  IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
   103→        (type = 'semantic_release' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id  IS NULL)
   104→    )
   105→);
   106→
   107→-- Agent runs (scoped to project)
   108→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_runs (
   109→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   110→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   111→    semantic_release_id UUID REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
   112→    trigger VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
   113→    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
   114→    prompt_used TEXT,
   115→    error TEXT,
   116→    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   117→    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   118→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
   119→);
   120→
   121→ALTER TABLE agent_runs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version VARCHAR(100);
   122→
   123→-- API authentication keys
   124→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
   125→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   126→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
   127→    key_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
   128→    key_prefix VARCHAR(12) NOT NULL,
   129→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   130→    last_used_at TIMESTAMPTZ
   131→);
   132→
   133→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on new releases
   134→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_release_created() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   135→BEGIN
   136→    PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   137→    RETURN NEW;
   138→END;
   139→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   140→
   141→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS release_created_trigger ON releases;
   142→CREATE TRIGGER release_created_trigger
   143→    AFTER INSERT ON releases
   144→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_release_created();
   145→
   146→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on semantic release completion
   147→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   148→BEGIN
   149→    IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND (OLD IS NULL OR OLD.status != 'completed') THEN
   150→        PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'semantic_release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   151→    END IF;
   152→    RETURN NEW;
   153→END;
   154→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   155→
   156→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS semantic_release_trigger ON semantic_releases;
   157→CREATE TRIGGER semantic_release_trigger
   158→    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON semantic_releases
   159→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release();
   160→
   161→-- Release TODOs (acknowledge/resolve tracking)
   162→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS release_todos (
   163→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   164→    release_id UUID REFERENCES releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   165→    semantic_release_id UUID REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   166→    status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
   167→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   168→    acknowledged_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   169→    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   170→    CHECK (
   171→        (release_id IS NOT NULL AND semantic_release_id IS NULL) OR
   172→        (release_id IS NULL AND semantic_release_id IS NOT NULL)
   173→    )
   174→);
   175→CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_release_todos_release_id ON release_todos(release_id) WHERE release_id IS NOT NULL;
   176→CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_release_todos_semantic_release_id ON release_todos(semantic_release_id) WHERE semantic_release_id IS NOT NULL;
   177→`
   178→
   179→// RunMigrations applies River's schema and the application schema. Idempotent — safe to call on every startup.
   180→func RunMigrations(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool) error {
   181→	migrator, err := rivermigrate.New(riverpgxv5.New(pool), nil)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		return fmt.Errorf("create river migrator: %w", err)
   184→	}
   185→	if _, err := migrator.Migrate(ctx, rivermigrate.DirectionUp, nil); err != nil {
   186→		return fmt.Errorf("river migrations: %w", err)
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, schema); err != nil {
   190→		return fmt.Errorf("app migrations: %w", err)
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	// Migrate existing subscription type values: source→source_release, project→semantic_release.
   194→	// Drop old CHECK constraints first (both the named type check and the unnamed
   195→	// composite check referencing old values), then UPDATE data, then re-add constraints.
   196→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `
   197→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS subscriptions_type_check;
   198→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS subscriptions_check;
   199→		UPDATE subscriptions SET type = 'source_release' WHERE type = 'source';
   200→		UPDATE subscriptions SET type = 'semantic_release' WHERE type = 'project';
   201→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions ADD CONSTRAINT subscriptions_type_check CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release'));
   202→		ALTER TABLE subscriptions ADD CONSTRAINT subscriptions_check CHECK (
   203→			(type = 'source_release'   AND source_id IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
   204→			(type = 'semantic_release' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id IS NULL)
   205→		);
   206→	`); err != nil {
   207→		return fmt.Errorf("subscription type migration: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `
   211→		ALTER TABLE sources ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version_filter_include TEXT;
   212→		ALTER TABLE sources ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version_filter_exclude TEXT;
   213→	`); err != nil {
   214→		return fmt.Errorf("source version filter migration: %w", err)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `
   218→		ALTER TABLE sources ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS exclude_prereleases BOOLEAN DEFAULT false;
   219→	`); err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("source exclude_prereleases migration: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→	return nil
   223→}
   224→

    11→// Dependencies holds all external dependencies required for registering API routes.
    12→type Dependencies struct {
    13→	DB                    *pgxpool.Pool
    14→	ProjectsStore         ProjectsStore
    15→	ReleasesStore         ReleasesStore
    16→	SubscriptionsStore    SubscriptionsStore
    17→	SourcesStore          SourcesStore

    31→
    32→// RegisterRoutes registers all API v1 routes on the given ServeMux.
    33→func RegisterRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, deps Dependencies) {
    34→	// Public chain: no auth required.
    35→	publicChain := Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery)
    36→
    37→	// Authenticated chain: includes auth unless NoAuth is set.
    38→	chain := publicChain
    39→	if !deps.NoAuth {
    40→		chain = Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery, RateLimit(10, 20), Auth(deps.KeyStore))
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Projects (CRUD)
    44→	projects := NewProjectsHandler(deps.ProjectsStore)
    45→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.List)))
    46→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Create)))
    47→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Get)))
    48→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Update)))
    49→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Delete)))
    50→
    51→	// Sources (nested under projects)
    52→	sources := NewSourcesHandler(deps.SourcesStore, deps.IngestionService, deps.HTTPClient)
    53→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.List)))
    54→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Create)))
    55→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Get)))
    56→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Update)))
    57→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Delete)))
    58→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/sources/{id}/poll", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.FetchReleases)))
    59→
    60→	// Context Sources (nested under projects)
    61→	contextSources := NewContextSourcesHandler(deps.ContextSourcesStore)
    62→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.List)))
    63→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Create)))
    64→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Get)))
    65→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Update)))
    66→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Delete)))
    67→
    68→	// Releases (read-only, nested under sources and projects)

    74→

    82→
    83→	// Subscriptions (CRUD)

   101→

   118→

   131→

   135→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/stats", chain(http.HandlerFunc(health.Stats)))
   136→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/stats/trend", chain(http.HandlerFunc(health.Trend)))
   137→}

     1→package main
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"log/slog"
     6→	"net/http"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"os/signal"
     9→	"time"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	agentpkg "github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/agent"
    13→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/api"
    14→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/db"
    15→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/ingestion"
    16→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    17→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/routing"
    18→)
    19→
    20→func main() {
    21→	// Configure log level from LOG_LEVEL env (debug, info, warn, error).
    22→	logLevel := new(slog.LevelVar) // defaults to Info
    23→	switch os.Getenv("LOG_LEVEL") {
    24→	case "debug":
    25→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelDebug)
    26→	case "warn":
    27→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelWarn)
    28→	case "error":
    29→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelError)
    30→	}
    31→	slog.SetDefault(slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(os.Stderr, &slog.HandlerOptions{Level: logLevel})))
    32→
    33→	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt)
    34→	defer stop()
    35→
    36→	dbURL := envOr("DATABASE_URL", "postgres://localhost:5432/changelogue?sslmode=disable")
    37→	addr := envOr("LISTEN_ADDR", ":8080")
    38→	noAuth := os.Getenv("NO_AUTH") == "true"
    39→
    40→	// Database
    41→	pool, err := db.NewPool(ctx, dbURL)
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		slog.Error("database connection failed", "err", err)
    44→		os.Exit(1)
    45→	}
    46→	defer pool.Close()
    47→
    48→	if err := db.RunMigrations(ctx, pool); err != nil {
    49→		slog.Error("migrations failed", "err", err)
    50→		os.Exit(1)
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	// API & routing store (created before River so we can register workers)
    54→	// The river client is set to nil initially; TriggerAgentRun (which needs it)
    55→	// won't be called until the server is up and the client is set below.
    56→	pgStore := api.NewPgStore(pool, nil)
    57→
    58→	// Register River workers
    59→	workers := river.NewWorkers()
    60→	notifyWorker := routing.NewNotifyWorker(pgStore, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
    61→	river.AddWorker(workers, notifyWorker)
    62→
    63→	// Agent worker: requires an LLM API key. If unavailable, agent jobs
    64→	// will remain in the queue until the key is configured and the server
    65→	// is restarted.
    66→	llmProvider := envOr("LLM_PROVIDER", "gemini")
    67→	llmModelDefault := "gemini-2.5-flash"
    68→	if llmProvider == "openai" {
    69→		llmModelDefault = "gpt-5.2"
    70→	}
    71→	llmConfig := agentpkg.LLMConfig{
    72→		Provider:      llmProvider,
    73→		Model:         envOr("LLM_MODEL", llmModelDefault),
    74→		GoogleAPIKey:  os.Getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
    75→		OpenAIAPIKey:  os.Getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
    76→		OpenAIBaseURL: os.Getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL"),
    77→	}
    78→	agentOrchestrator, err := agentpkg.NewOrchestrator(pgStore, llmConfig, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
    79→	if err != nil {
    80→		slog.Warn("agent orchestrator not available — agent jobs will not be processed", "err", err)
    81→	} else {
    82→		agentWorker := agentpkg.NewAgentWorker(agentOrchestrator, pgStore)
    83→		river.AddWorker(workers, agentWorker)
    84→		slog.Info("agent worker registered")
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	riverClient, err := queue.NewRiverClient(pool, workers)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		slog.Error("river client failed", "err", err)
    90→		os.Exit(1)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// Now that the river client exists, set it on the existing pgStore so that
    94→	// all holders (including NotifyWorker) can enqueue agent jobs.
    95→	pgStore.SetRiverClient(riverClient)
    96→
    97→	if err := riverClient.Start(ctx); err != nil {
    98→		slog.Error("river start failed", "err", err)
    99→		os.Exit(1)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Ingestion layer
   103→	ingestionStore := ingestion.NewPgStore(pool, riverClient)
   104→	svc := ingestion.NewService(ingestionStore)
   105→
   106→	loader := ingestion.NewSourceLoader(pool, http.DefaultClient)
   107→	orch := ingestion.NewOrchestrator(svc, loader, pool, 5*time.Minute)
   108→
   109→	broadcaster := api.NewBroadcaster()
   110→
   111→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
   112→
   113→	// Serve frontend static export if available
   114→	if info, err := os.Stat("web/out"); err == nil && info.IsDir() {
   115→		api.RegisterFrontend(mux, "web/out")
   116→		slog.Info("serving frontend from web/out")
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Register all API v1 routes
   120→	api.RegisterRoutes(mux, api.Dependencies{
   121→		DB:                    pool,
   122→		ProjectsStore:         pgStore,
   123→		ReleasesStore:         pgStore,
   124→		SubscriptionsStore:    pgStore,
   125→		SourcesStore:          pgStore,
   126→		ChannelsStore:         pgStore,
   127→		ContextSourcesStore:   pgStore,
   128→		SemanticReleasesStore: pgStore,
   129→		AgentStore:            pgStore,
   130→		TodosStore:            pgStore,
   131→		PublicURL:             os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"),
   132→		KeyStore:              pgStore,
   133→		HealthChecker:         pgStore,
   134→		Broadcaster:           broadcaster,
   135→		NoAuth:                noAuth,
   136→		IngestionService:      svc,
   137→		HTTPClient:            http.DefaultClient,
   138→	})
   139→
   140→	srv := &http.Server{Addr: addr, Handler: api.CORS(mux)}
   141→
   142→	// Start polling in background
   143→	go orch.Run(ctx)
   144→
   145→	// Start PostgreSQL LISTEN/NOTIFY → SSE bridge
   146→	go api.ListenForNotifications(ctx, pool, broadcaster)
   147→
   148→	// Start HTTP server
   149→	go func() {
   150→		slog.Info("server starting", "addr", srv.Addr)
   151→		if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != http.ErrServerClosed {
   152→			slog.Error("server error", "err", err)
   153→		}
   154→	}()
   155→
   156→	<-ctx.Done()
   157→	slog.Info("shutting down")
   158→
   159→	shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   160→	defer cancel()
   161→	riverClient.Stop(shutdownCtx)
   162→	srv.Shutdown(shutdownCtx)
   163→}
   164→
   165→func envOr(key, fallback string) string {
   166→	if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
   167→		return v
   168→	}
   169→	return fallback
   170→}
   171→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"

     6→
     7→	"google.golang.org/adk/model"
     8→	"google.golang.org/adk/model/gemini"
     9→	"google.golang.org/genai"
    10→
    11→	oaimodel "github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/agent/openai"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// LLMConfig holds the configuration for creating an LLM model.
    15→type LLMConfig struct {
    16→	Provider string // "gemini" or "openai"
    17→	Model    string // e.g. "gemini-2.5-flash", "gpt-5.2"
    18→
    19→	// Gemini
    20→	GoogleAPIKey string
    21→
    22→	// OpenAI
    23→	OpenAIAPIKey  string
    24→	OpenAIBaseURL string // defaults to https://api.openai.com/v1
    25→}
    26→
    27→// NewLLMModel creates a model.LLM based on the configured provider.
    28→// wsConfig is optional and only applies to the OpenAI provider; it enables
    29→// the Responses API built-in web_search tool on the created model.
    30→func NewLLMModel(ctx context.Context, cfg LLMConfig, wsConfig *oaimodel.WebSearch) (model.LLM, error) {
    31→	switch cfg.Provider {
    32→	case "gemini", "":
    33→		if cfg.GoogleAPIKey == "" {
    34→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("GOOGLE_API_KEY is required for gemini provider")
    35→		}
    36→		return gemini.NewModel(ctx, cfg.Model, &genai.ClientConfig{
    37→			APIKey: cfg.GoogleAPIKey,
    38→		})
    39→
    40→	case "openai":
    41→		return oaimodel.NewModel(ctx, cfg.Model, oaimodel.Config{
    42→			APIKey:  cfg.OpenAIAPIKey,
    43→			BaseURL: cfg.OpenAIBaseURL,
    44→		}, wsConfig)
    45→
    46→	default:
    47→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown LLM provider: %q (supported: gemini, openai)", cfg.Provider)
    48→	}
    49→}
    50→

    10→// TodosStore defines the data access interface for TODO operations.
    11→type TodosStore interface {
    12→	ListTodos(ctx context.Context, status string, page, perPage int, aggregated bool) ([]models.Todo, int, error)
    13→	GetTodo(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Todo, error)
    14→	AcknowledgeTodo(ctx context.Context, id string, cascade bool) error
    15→	ResolveTodo(ctx context.Context, id string, cascade bool) error
    16→	ReopenTodo(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    17→}
    18→
    19→// TodosHandler handles HTTP requests for release TODOs.
    20→type TodosHandler struct {
    21→	store     TodosStore
    22→	publicURL string
    23→}
    24→
    25→// NewTodosHandler creates a new TodosHandler.
    26→func NewTodosHandler(store TodosStore, publicURL string) *TodosHandler {
    27→	return &TodosHandler{store: store, publicURL: publicURL}
    28→}
    29→
    30→// List returns a paginated list of TODOs, optionally filtered by status.
    31→func (h *TodosHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    32→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    33→	status := r.URL.Query().Get("status")
    34→	aggregated := r.URL.Query().Get("aggregated") == "true"
    35→
    36→	todos, total, err := h.store.ListTodos(r.Context(), status, page, perPage, aggregated)
    37→	if err != nil {
    38→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", err.Error())
    39→		return
    40→	}
    41→	if todos == nil {
    42→		todos = []models.Todo{}
    43→	}
    44→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, todos, page, perPage, total)
    45→}
    46→
    47→// Get returns a single TODO by ID.
    48→func (h *TodosHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    49→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    50→	todo, err := h.store.GetTodo(r.Context(), id)

     1→"use client";

     3→import { useState } from "react";
     4→import Link from "next/link";
     5→import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
     6→import {
     7→  LayoutDashboard,
     8→  ListTodo,
     9→  FolderKanban,
    10→  Package,
    11→  Bell,
    12→  Megaphone,
    13→  PanelLeftOpen,
    14→  PanelLeftClose,
    15→} from "lucide-react";
    16→import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
    17→
    18→const navItems = [
    19→  { href: "/", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
    20→  { href: "/projects", label: "Projects", icon: FolderKanban },
    21→  { href: "/todo", label: "Todo", icon: ListTodo },
    22→  { href: "/releases", label: "Releases", icon: Package },
    23→  { href: "/channels", label: "Channels", icon: Megaphone },
    24→  { href: "/subscriptions", label: "Subscriptions", icon: Bell },
    25→];
    26→
    27→export function Sidebar() {
    28→  const pathname = usePathname();
    29→  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
    30→
    31→  return (
    32→    <aside
    33→      className={cn(
    34→        "flex shrink-0 flex-col transition-[width] duration-200",
    35→        expanded ? "w-[200px]" : "w-[52px]"
    36→      )}
    37→      style={{ backgroundColor: "#16181c" }}
    38→    >
    39→      {/* Logo + toggle */}
    40→      <div className="flex h-12 items-center px-3">
    41→        {expanded ? (
    42→          <>
    43→            <img src="/logo.svg" alt="" className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0" />
    44→            <Link
    45→              href="/"
    46→              className="ml-2 text-[16px] italic text-white"
    47→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
    48→            >
    49→              Changelogue
    50→            </Link>
    51→            <button
    52→              onClick={() => setExpanded(false)}
    53→              className="ml-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    54→              title="Collapse sidebar"
    55→            >
    56→              <PanelLeftClose className="h-4 w-4" />
    57→            </button>
    58→          </>
    59→        ) : (
    60→          <button
    61→            onClick={() => setExpanded(true)}
    62→            className="mx-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    63→            title="Expand sidebar"
    64→          >
    65→            <PanelLeftOpen className="h-4 w-4" />
    66→          </button>
    67→        )}
    68→      </div>
    69→
    70→      {/* Nav */}
    71→      <nav className="flex-1 pt-2">
    72→        {navItems.map((item) => {
    73→          const isActive =
    74→            item.href === "/"
    75→              ? pathname === "/"
    76→              : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
    77→          return (
    78→            <Link
    79→              key={item.href}
    80→              href={item.href}
    81→              title={expanded ? undefined : item.label}
    82→              className={cn(
    83→                "flex items-center gap-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition-colors duration-150",
    84→                expanded ? "pl-4 pr-3" : "justify-center px-0",
    85→                isActive
    86→                  ? "text-white"
    87→                  : "text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-white"
    88→              )}
    89→              style={
    90→                isActive
    91→                  ? {
    92→                      borderLeft: "3px solid #e8601a",
    93→                      backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
    94→                      paddingLeft: expanded ? "13px" : undefined,
    95→                    }
    96→                  : { borderLeft: "3px solid transparent" }
    97→              }
    98→            >
    99→              <item.icon className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
   100→              {expanded && (
   101→                <span style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
   102→                  {item.label}
   103→                </span>
   104→              )}
   105→            </Link>
   106→          );
   107→        })}
   108→      </nav>
   109→    </aside>
   110→  );
   111→}
   112→

     2→import type {
     3→  ApiResponse,

    25→
    26→const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
    27→
    28→async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    29→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    30→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    31→    ...init,
    32→  });
    33→  if (!res.ok) {
    34→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    35→    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
    36→  }
    37→  if (res.status === 204) return null as T;
    38→  return res.json();
    39→}
    40→
    41→// --- Projects ---

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"net/http"
     7→	"strings"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// ProjectsStore defines the persistence operations for projects.
    13→type ProjectsStore interface {
    14→	ListProjects(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.Project, int, error)
    15→	CreateProject(ctx context.Context, p *models.Project) error
    16→	GetProject(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Project, error)
    17→	UpdateProject(ctx context.Context, id string, p *models.Project) error
    18→	DeleteProject(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    19→}
    20→
    21→// ProjectsHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /projects resource.
    22→type ProjectsHandler struct {
    23→	store ProjectsStore
    24→}
    25→
    26→// NewProjectsHandler returns a new ProjectsHandler.
    27→func NewProjectsHandler(store ProjectsStore) *ProjectsHandler {
    28→	return &ProjectsHandler{store: store}
    29→}
    30→
    31→// List handles GET /projects — returns a paginated list of projects.
    32→func (h *ProjectsHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    33→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    34→	projects, total, err := h.store.ListProjects(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list projects")
    37→		return
    38→	}
    39→	// Return empty array, not null, when no results.
    40→	if projects == nil {
    41→		projects = []models.Project{}
    42→	}
    43→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, projects, page, perPage, total)
    44→}
    45→
    46→// Create handles POST /projects — creates a new project.
    47→func (h *ProjectsHandler) Create(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    48→	var p models.Project
    49→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &p); err != nil {
    50→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid JSON body")
    51→		return
    52→	}
    53→	p.Name = strings.TrimSpace(p.Name)
    54→	if p.Name == "" {
    55→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "Name is required")
    56→		return
    57→	}
    58→	// Default agent_rules to empty object if not provided.
    59→	if p.AgentRules == nil {
    60→		p.AgentRules = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
    61→	}
    62→	if err := h.store.CreateProject(r.Context(), &p); err != nil {
    63→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to create project")
    64→		return
    65→	}
    66→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, p)
    67→}
    68→
    69→// Get handles GET /projects/{id} — returns a single project.
    70→func (h *ProjectsHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    71→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    72→	if id == "" {
    73→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Invalid project ID")
    74→		return
    75→	}
    76→	p, err := h.store.GetProject(r.Context(), id)
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Project not found")
    79→		return
    80→	}

{\n\t\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{\n\t\t\t\"content\":  \"eyJuYW1lIjogIm15YXBwIn0=\", // base64 '{\"name\": \"myapp\"}'\n\t\t\t\"encoding\": \"base64\",\n\t\t})\n\t})\n\n\tsrv := err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"FetchDependencyFiles: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(files) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 2 dependency files, got %d\", len(files))\n\t}\n\t// Verify go.mod content was `internal/onboard/scanner.go`:\n\n```go\npackage onboard\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/base64\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// dependencyFilePatterns lists filenames that indicate dependency manifests.\nvar dependencyFilePatterns = []string{\n\t\"go.mod\",\n\t\"go.sum\",\n\t\"package.json\",\n\t\"package-lock.json\",\n\t\"yarn.lock\",\n\t\"pnpm-lock.yaml\",\n\t\"requirements.txt\",\n\t\"Pipfile\",\n\t\"pyproject.toml\",\n\t\"setup.py\",\n\t\"Cargo.toml\",\n\t\"Cargo.lock\",\n\t\"Gemfile\",\n\t\"Gemfile.lock\",\n\t\"pom.xml\",\n\t\"build.gradle\",\n\t\"build.gradle.kts\",\n\t\"composer.json\",\n\t\"mix.exs\",\n\t\"pubspec.yaml\",\n\t\"Dockerfile\",\n}\n\n// DependencyFile holds the path and decoded content of a dependency file.\ntype DependencyFile struct {\n\tPath    string\n\tContent string\n}\n\n// Scanner fetches dependency files from a GitHub repository.\ntype Scanner struct {\n\tclient  *http.Client\n\tbaseURL string\n\ttoken   string\n}\n\n// NewScanner creates a Scanner. If token is empty, it reads GITHUB_TOKEN from env.\nfunc NewScanner(client *http.Client, baseURL, token string) *Scanner {\n\tif token == \"\" {\n\t\ttoken = os.Getenv(\"GITHUB_TOKEN\")\n\t}\n\tif baseURL == \"\" {\n\t\tbaseURL = \"https://api.github.com\"\n\t}\n\treturn &Scanner{client: client, baseURL: baseURL, token: token}\n}\n\n// ParseRepoURL extracts owner and repo from various GitHub URL formats.\nfunc ParseRepoURL(raw string) (owner, repo string, err error) {\n\traw = strings.TrimSpace(raw)\n\tif raw == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"empty repo URL\")\n\t}\n\t// Strip common prefixes\n\traw = strings.TrimPrefix(raw, \"https://\")\n\traw = strings.TrimPrefix(raw, \"http://\")\n\traw = strings.TrimPrefix(raw, \"github.com/\")\n\traw = strings.TrimSuffix(raw, \".git\")\n\traw = strings.TrimSuffix(raw, \"/\")\n\n\tparts := strings.SplitN(raw, \"/\", 3)\n\tif len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == \"\" || parts[1] == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"invalid repo URL: expected 'owner/repo', got %q\", raw)\n\t}\n\treturn parts[0], parts[1], nil\n}\n\n// FetchDependencyFiles fetches the repo tree, identifies dependency files, and returns their contents.\nfunc (s *Scanner) FetchDependencyFiles(ctx context.Context, owner, repo string) ([]DependencyFile, error) {\n\tbranch, err := s.getDefaultBranch(ctx, owner, repo)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"get default branch: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttree, err := s.getTree(ctx, owner, repo, branch)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"get tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Filter to dependency files\n\tvar depPaths []string\n\tfor _, entry := range tree {\n\t\tif entry.Type != \"blob\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tbasename := entry.Path\n\t\tif idx := strings.LastIndex(entry.Path, \"/\"); idx >= 0 {\n\t\t\tbasename = entry.Path[idx+1:]\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, pat := range dependencyFilePatterns {\n\t\t\tif strings.EqualFold(basename, pat) {\n\t\t\t\tdepPaths = append(depPaths, entry.Path)\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Fetch contents\n\tvar files []DependencyFile\n\tfor _, path := range depPaths {\n\t\tcontent, err := s.getFileContent(ctx, owner, repo, path)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t// Log but continue — some files may be too large\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfiles = append(files, DependencyFile{Path: path, Content: content})\n\t}\n\treturn files, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Scanner) doRequest(ctx context.Context, url string, target any) error {\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, url, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Accept\", \"application/vnd.github+json\")\n\tif s.token != \"\" {\n\t\treq.Header.Set(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer \"+s.token)\n\t}\n\n\tresp, err := s.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"execute request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"GitHub API returned %d for %s\", resp.StatusCode, url)\n\t}\n\treturn json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(target)\n}\n\ntype treeEntry struct {\n\tPath string `json:\"path\"`\n\tType string `json:\"type\"`\n}\n\nfunc (s *Scanner) getDefaultBranch(ctx context.Context, owner, repo string) (string, error) {\n\tvar result struct {\n\t\tDefaultBranch string `json:\"default_branch\"`\n\t}\n\turl := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/repos/%s/%s\", s.baseURL, owner, repo)\n\tif err := s.doRequest(ctx, url, &result); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif result.DefaultBranch == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"main\", nil\n\t}\n\treturn result.DefaultBranch, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Scanner) getTree(ctx context.Context, owner, repo, branch string) ([]treeEntry, error) {\n\tvar result struct {\n\t\tTree []treeEntry `json:\"tree\"`\n\t}\n\turl := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/repos/%s/%s/git/trees/%s?recursive=1\", s.baseURL, owner, repo, branch)\n\tif err := s.doRequest(ctx, url, &result); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn result.Tree, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Scanner) getFileContent(ctx context.Context, owner, repo, path string) (string, error) {\n\tvar result struct {\n\t\tContent  string `json:\"content\"`\n\t\tEncoding string `json:\"encoding\"`\n\t}\n\turl := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/repos/%s/%s/contents/%s\", s.baseURL, owner, repo, path)\n\tif err := s.doRequest(ctx, url, &result); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif result.Encoding == \"base64\" {\n\t\t// GitHub base64 content may contain newlines\n\t\tcleaned := strings.ReplaceAll(result.Content, \"\\n\", \"\")\n\t\tdecoded, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(cleaned)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"decode base64: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn string(decoded), nil\n\t}\n\treturn result.Content, nil\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `go test ./internal/onboard/... -v`\nExpected: PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add internal/onboard/scanner.go `internal/onboard/gemini_test.go`:\n\n```go\npackage onboard\n\nimport (\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\nfunc TestParseDependencies(t *testing.T) {\n\traw := `[\n\t\t{\"name\": \"gorilla/mux\", \"version\": \"v1.8.1\", \"ecosystem\": \"go\", \"upstream_repo\": \"github.com/gorilla/mux\", \"provider\": \"github\"},\n\t\t{\"name\": \"react\", \"version\": \"^18.0.0\", \"ecosystem\": \"npm\", \"upstream_repo\": \"github.com/facebook/react\", \"provider\": \"github\"}\n\t]`\n\n\tdeps, err := ParseDependencies([]byte(raw))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ParseDependencies: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(deps) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 2 deps, got %d\", len(deps))\n\t}\n\tif deps[0].Name != \"gorilla/mux\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"deps[0].Name = %q, want %q\", deps[0].Name, \"gorilla/mux\")\n\t}\n\tif deps[1].Ecosystem != \"npm\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"deps[1].Ecosystem = %q, want %q\", deps[1].Ecosystem, \"npm\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseDependencies_ExtractsJSON(t *testing.T) {\n\t// LLM sometimes wraps JSON in markdown code fences\n\traw := \"```json\\n[{\\\"name\\\": \\\"test\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"1.0\\\", \\\"ecosystem\\\": \\\"go\\\", \\\"upstream_repo\\\": \\\"github.com/test/test\\\", \\\"provider\\\": \\\"github\\\"}]\\n```\"\n\n\tdeps, err := ParseDependencies([]byte(raw))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ParseDependencies: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(deps) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 1 dep, got %d\", len(deps))\n\t}\n\tif deps[0].Name != \"test\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"deps[0].Name = %q, want %q\", deps[0].Name, \"test\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseDependencies_EmptyArray(t *testing.T) {\n\tdeps, err := ParseDependencies([]byte(\"[]\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"ParseDependencies: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(deps) != 0 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 0 deps, got %d\", len(deps))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestBuildPrompt(t *testing.T) {\n\tfiles := []DependencyFile{\n\t\t{Path: \"go.mod\", Content: \"module example.com\"},\n\t}\n\tprompt := BuildExtractionPrompt(files)\n\tif prompt == \"\" {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"prompt is empty\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsString(prompt, \"go.mod\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"prompt should contain file path\")\n\t}\n\tif !containsString(prompt, \"module example.com\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"prompt should contain file content\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc containsString(s, sub string) bool {\n\treturn len(s) >= len(sub) && (s == sub || len(s) > 0 && containsSubstring(s, sub))\n}\n\nfunc containsSubstring(s, sub string) bool {\n\tfor i := 0; i <= len(s)-len(sub); i++ {\n\t\tif s[i:i+len(sub)] == sub {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\nRun: `go test ./internal/onboard/... -v -run TestParseDependencies`\nExpected: FAIL\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the extractor**\n\nCreate `internal/onboard/gemini.go`:\n\n```go\npackage onboard\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n\t\"google.golang.org/genai\"\n)\n\nconst extractionPrompt = `You are a dependency extraction agent. Given the contents of dependency/manifest files from a software project, extract all direct dependencies (not dev dependencies or build tools).\n\nFor each dependency, return:\n- name: the package/library name\n- version: the version constraint or pinned version\n- ecosystem: one of \"go\", \"npm\", \"pypi\", \"cargo\", \"rubygems\", \"maven\", \"gradle\", \"docker\", \"other\"\n- upstream_repo: your best guess at the canonical GitHub repository URL (e.g., \"github.com/gorilla/mux\"). Use the format \"github.com/owner/repo\" without https://.\n- provider: the Changelogue provider to use for release tracking. Use \"github\" for repos with GitHub releases, \"dockerhub\" for Docker images, \"gitlab\" for GitLab repos, \"ecr_public\" for AWS ECR images. When unsure, default to \"github\".\n\nReturn ONLY a JSON array. No explanations, no markdown formatting.`\n\n// DependencyExtractor calls Gemini to extract dependencies from file contents.\ntype DependencyExtractor struct {\n\tclient *genai.Client\n\tmodel  string\n}\n\n// NewDependencyExtractor creates a new extractor. apiKey and model are required.\nfunc NewDependencyExtractor(ctx context.Context, apiKey, model string) (*DependencyExtractor, error) {\n\tif apiKey == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY is required for dependency extraction\")\n\t}\n\tif model == \"\" {\n\t\tmodel = \"gemini-2.5-flash\"\n\t}\n\tclient, err := genai.NewClient(ctx, &genai.ClientConfig{\n\t\tAPIKey: apiKey,\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create genai client: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &DependencyExtractor{client: client, model: model}, nil\n}\n\n// Extract sends dependency file contents to Gemini and returns parsed dependencies.\nfunc (e *DependencyExtractor) Extract(ctx context.Context, files []DependencyFile) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {\n\tif len(files) == 0 {\n\t\treturn []models.ScannedDependency{}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tprompt := BuildExtractionPrompt(files)\n\n\tresult, err := e.client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, e.model, genai.Text(prompt), nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"gemini generate: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(result.Candidates) == 0 || len(result.Candidates[0].Content.Parts) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"gemini returned no content\")\n\t}\n\n\ttext := result.Candidates[0].Content.Parts[0].Text\n\treturn ParseDependencies([]byte(text))\n}\n\n// BuildExtractionPrompt constructs the prompt with file contents.\nfunc BuildExtractionPrompt(files []DependencyFile) string {\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.WriteString(extractionPrompt)\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\\n--- FILES ---\\n\")\n\tfor _, f := range files {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(&b, \"\\n### %s\\n```\\n%s\\n```\\n\", f.Path, f.Content)\n\t}\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// ParseDependencies parses the LLM response into ScannedDependency structs.\n// Handles raw JSON arrays and markdown-fenced JSON.\nfunc ParseDependencies(raw []byte) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {\n\ttext := strings.TrimSpace(string(raw))\n\n\t// Strip markdown code fences if present\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(text, \"```\") {\n\t\tlines := strings.Split(text, \"\\n\")\n\t\t// Remove first and last lines (the fence markers)\n\t\tif len(lines) >= 2 {\n\t\t\tlines = lines[1 : len(lines)-1]\n\t\t\t// Remove trailing fence if still present\n\t\t\tif len(lines) > 0 && strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]), \"```\") {\n\t\t\t\tlines = lines[:len(lines)-1]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttext = strings.Join(lines, \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar deps []models.ScannedDependency\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(text), &deps); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"parse dependencies JSON: %w\\nraw: %s\", err, text)\n\t}\n\treturn deps, nil\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `go test ./internal/onboard/... -v`\nExpected: PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add internal/onboard/gemini.go internal/onboard/gemini_test.go\ngit commit -m \"feat(onboard): implement Gemini dependency extractor with tests\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Chunk 4: Store Layer (PgStore Methods)\n\n### Task 6: Add OnboardStore interface and API handler scaffold\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `internal/api/onboard.go`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create the handler file with store interface**\n\nCreate `internal/api/onboard.go`:\n\n```go\npackage api\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n)\n\n// OnboardSelection represents a user's choice for one dependency.\ntype OnboardSelection struct {\n\tDepName        string `json:\"dep_name\"`\n\tUpstreamRepo   string `json:\"upstream_repo\"`\n\tProvider       string `json:\"provider\"`\n\tProjectID      string `json:\"project_id,omitempty\"`\n\tNewProjectName string `json:\"new_project_name,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// OnboardApplyResult holds the outcome of applying scan selections.\ntype OnboardApplyResult struct {\n\tCreatedProjects []models.Project `json:\"created_projects\"`\n\tCreatedSources  []models.Source  `json:\"created_sources\"`\n\tSkipped         []string         `json:\"skipped\"`\n}\n\n// OnboardStore defines the data access interface for onboarding operations.\ntype OnboardStore interface {\n\tCreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tGetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tUpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error\n\tActiveScanForRepo(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tApplyOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, scanID string, selections []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error)\n}\n\n// OnboardHandler handles HTTP requests for the /onboard resource.\ntype OnboardHandler struct {\n\tstore  OnboardStore\n\triver  RiverInserter\n}\n\n// RiverInserter is the minimal interface for enqueuing River jobs.\ntype RiverInserter interface {\n\tInsert(ctx context.Context, args interface{ Kind() string }, opts *interface{}) (*interface{}, error)\n}\n\n// NewOnboardHandler returns a new OnboardHandler.\nfunc NewOnboardHandler(store OnboardStore) *OnboardHandler {\n\treturn &OnboardHandler{store: store}\n}\n\n// Scan handles POST /api/v1/onboard/scan — starts a new dependency scan.\nfunc (h *OnboardHandler) Scan(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tvar req struct {\n\t\tRepoURL string `json:\"repo_url\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := DecodeJSON(r, &req); err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid JSON body\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\treq.RepoURL = strings.TrimSpace(req.RepoURL)\n\tif req.RepoURL == \"\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"repo_url is required\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Check for active scan\n\texisting, _ := h.store.ActiveScanForRepo(r.Context(), req.RepoURL)\n\tif existing != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusConflict, \"conflict\", \"A scan is already in progress for this repo\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tscan, err := h.store.CreateOnboardScan(r.Context(), req.RepoURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"Failed to create scan\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, scan)\n}\n\n// GetScan handles GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id} — returns scan status and results.\nfunc (h *OnboardHandler) GetScan(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tid := r.PathValue(\"id\")\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid scan ID\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tscan, err := h.store.GetOnboardScan(r.Context(), id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif err == pgx.ErrNoRows {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, \"not_found\", \"Scan not found\")\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"Failed to get scan\")\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, scan)\n}\n\n// Apply handles POST /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}/apply — creates projects/sources from selections.\nfunc (h *OnboardHandler) Apply(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tid := r.PathValue(\"id\")\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid scan ID\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tvar req struct {\n\t\tSelections []OnboardSelection `json:\"selections\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := DecodeJSON(r, &req); err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid JSON body\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif len(req.Selections) == 0 {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"At least one selection is required\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Validate each selection\n\tfor i, sel := range req.Selections {\n\t\thasProjectID := sel.ProjectID != \"\"\n\t\thasNewName := sel.NewProjectName != \"\"\n\t\tif hasProjectID == hasNewName {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"validation_error\",\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"selection[%d]: exactly one of project_id or new_project_name must be set\", i))\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tresult, err := h.store.ApplyOnboardScan(r.Context(), id, req.Selections)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"unique constraint\") || strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"duplicate key\") {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusConflict, \"conflict\", err.Error())\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"Failed to apply selections\")\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, result)\n}\n```\n\nNote: The `RiverInserter` interface above is a placeholder — the scan endpoint enqueues jobs via the store's `CreateOnboardScan` which writes both the scan record and enqueues the River job in the same transaction. We'll wire this up in the PgStore implementation.
