handlers\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: Implement OnboardStore methods in PgStore\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `internal/api/pgstore.go`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Add CreateOnboardScan method**\n\nAppend to `internal/api/pgstore.go`:\n\n```go\n// --- Onboard Store ---\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) CreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO onboard_scans (repo_url) VALUES ($1)\n\t\t RETURNING id, repo_url, status, created_at`,\n\t\trepoURL,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// Enqueue River job\n\t_, err = s.river.Insert(ctx, queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs{ScanID: scan.ID}, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enqueue scan job: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) GetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, repo_url, status, results, COALESCE(error, ''), created_at, started_at, completed_at\n\t\t FROM onboard_scans WHERE id = $1`, id,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.Results, &scan.Error,\n\t\t&scan.CreatedAt, &scan.StartedAt, &scan.CompletedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error {\n\t_, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE onboard_scans SET status = $2, results = $3, error = NULLIF($4, ''),\n\t\t started_at = CASE WHEN $2 = 'processing' AND started_at IS NULL THEN NOW() ELSE started_at END,\n\t\t completed_at = CASE WHEN $2 IN ('completed', 'failed') THEN NOW() ELSE completed_at END\n\t\t WHERE id = $1`, id, status, results, scanErr,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ActiveScanForRepo(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, repo_url, status, created_at FROM onboard_scans\n\t\t WHERE repo_url = $1 AND status IN ('pending', 'processing')\n\t\t ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`, repoURL,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ApplyOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, scanID string, selections []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error) {\n\tresult := &OnboardApplyResult{\n\t\tCreatedProjects: []models.Project{},\n\t\tCreatedSources:  []models.Source{},\n\t\tSkipped:         []string{},\n\t}\n\n\ttx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"begin tx: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer tx.Rollback(ctx)\n\n\tfor _, sel := range selections {\n\t\tprojectID := sel.ProjectID\n\n\t\t// Create project if needed\n\t\tif sel.NewProjectName != \"\" {\n\t\t\tvar p models.Project\n\t\t\terr := tx.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO projects (name, agent_rules) VALUES ($1, '{}')\n\t\t\t\t RETURNING id, name, description, agent_prompt, agent_rules, created_at, updated_at`,\n\t\t\t\tsel.NewProjectName,\n\t\t\t).Scan(&p.ID, &p.Name, &p.Description, &p.AgentPrompt, &p.AgentRules, &p.CreatedAt, &p.UpdatedAt)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create project %q: %w\", sel.NewProjectName, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tprojectID = p.ID\n\t\t\tresult.CreatedProjects = append(result.CreatedProjects, p)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Create source — skip if duplicate\n\t\tvar src models.Source\n\t\terr := tx.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sources (project_id, provider, repository)\n\t\t\t VALUES ($1, $2, $3)\n\t\t\t ON CONFLICT (provider, repository) DO NOTHING\n\t\t\t RETURNING id, project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled, created_at, updated_at`,\n\t\t\tprojectID, sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo,\n\t\t).Scan(&src.ID, &src.ProjectID, &src.Provider, &src.Repository,\n\t\t\t&src.PollIntervalSeconds, &src.Enabled, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif err.Error() == \"no rows in result set\" {\n\t\t\t\tresult.Skipped = append(result.Skipped, fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s: source already exists\", sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo))\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create source %s/%s: %w\", sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult.CreatedSources = append(result.CreatedSources, src)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Add the `queue` import to pgstore.go imports**\n\nAdd `\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue\"` to the import block if not [ ] **Step 1: Define the ScanStore interface for the worker**\n\nCreate `internal/onboard/worker.go`:\n\n```go\npackage onboard\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/riverqueue/river\"\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue\"\n)\n\n// ScanStore is the data access interface for the scan worker.\ntype ScanStore interface {\n\tGetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tUpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error\n}\n\n// ScanWorker processes scan_dependencies River jobs.\ntype ScanWorker struct {\n\triver.WorkerDefaults[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]\n\tstore     ScanStore\n\tscanner   *Scanner\n\textractor *DependencyExtractor\n}\n\n// NewScanWorker creates a ScanWorker. extractor may be nil if GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set.\nfunc NewScanWorker(store ScanStore, scanner *Scanner, extractor *DependencyExtractor) *ScanWorker {\n\treturn &ScanWorker{\n\t\tstore:     store,\n\t\tscanner:   scanner,\n\t\textractor: extractor,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (w *ScanWorker) Timeout(_ *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) time.Duration {\n\treturn 3 * time.Minute\n}\n\nfunc (w *ScanWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) error {\n\tscanID := job.Args.ScanID\n\tslog.Info(\"scan worker picked up job\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"attempt\", job.Attempt)\n\n\t// Load scan\n\tscan, err := w.store.GetOnboardScan(ctx, scanID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"load scan %s: %w\", scanID, err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Mark processing\n\tif err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"processing\", nil, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update status to processing: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse repo URL\n\towner, repo, err := ParseRepoURL(scan.RepoURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse repo URL: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Fetch dependency files\n\tfiles, err := w.scanner.FetchDependencyFiles(ctx, owner, repo)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"fetch dependency files: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"found dependency files\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(files))\n\n\tif len(files) == 0 {\n\t\t// No dependency files — store empty results\n\t\temptyResults, _ := json.Marshal([]models.ScannedDependency{})\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", emptyResults, \"\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract dependencies via LLM\n\tif w.extractor == nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, \"LLM not configured (set GOOGLE_API_KEY)\")\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extractor not configured\")\n\t}\n\n\tdeps, err := w.extractor.Extract(ctx, files)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extract dependencies: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"extracted dependencies\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(deps))\n\n\tresults, err := json.Marshal(deps)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"marshal results: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", results, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update scan completed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n```\n\nNote: Remove the unused `os` and `http` imports if the compiler flags them (they were included Task 12: Create the onboarding page\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `web/app/onboard/page.tsx`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create the onboarding page**\n\nCreate `web/app/onboard/page.tsx`. This is a multi-step page: Input → Scanning → Results → Apply.\n\n```tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { onboard as onboardApi, projects as projectsApi } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport type { OnboardScan, ScannedDependency, OnboardSelection, Project } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\nimport { Rocket, Loader2, Check, X, Search, ExternalLink } from \"lucide-react\";\n\ntype Step = \"input\" | \"scanning\" | \"results\" | \"applied\";\n\nexport default function OnboardPage() {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(\"input\");\n  const [repoUrl, setRepoUrl] = useState(\"\");\n  const [scanId, setScanId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [scan, setScan] = useState<OnboardScan | null>(null);\n  const [selections, setSelections] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});\n  const [projectAssignments, setProjectAssignments] = useState<Record<number, { mode: \"new\" | \"existing\"; projectId?: string; newName?: string }>>({});\n  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);\n  const [applyResult, setApplyResult] = useState<{ created_projects: Project[]; created_sources: any[]; skipped: string[] } | null>(null);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Fetch existing projects for the dropdown\n  const { data: projectsResp } = useSWR(\"projects-all\", () => projectsApi.list(1, 200));\n  const existingProjects = projectsResp?.data ?? [];\n\n  // Poll scan status\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (step !== \"scanning\" || !scanId) return;\n    const interval = setInterval(async () => {\n      try {\n        const resp = await onboardApi.getScan(scanId);\n        setScan(resp.data);\n        if (resp.data.status === \"completed\") {\n          setStep(\"results\");\n          // Select all by default\n          const deps = resp.data.results ?? [];\n          const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};\n          const assign: Record<number, { mode: \"new\" | \"existing\"; newName?: string }> = {};\n          deps.forEach((d, i) => {\n            sel[i] = true;\n            assign[i] = { mode: \"new\", newName: d.name.replace(/\\//g, \"-\") };\n          });\n          setSelections(sel);\n          setProjectAssignments(assign);\n          clearInterval(interval);\n        } else if (resp.data.status === \"failed\") {\n          setError(resp.data.error || \"Scan failed\");\n          setStep(\"input\");\n          clearInterval(interval);\n        }\n      } catch {\n        // Keep polling\n      }\n    }, 2000);\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, [step, scanId]);\n\n  const startScan = async () => {\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repoUrl);\n      setScanId(resp.data.id);\n      setScan(resp.data);\n      setStep(\"scanning\");\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      setError(e.message || \"Failed to start scan\");\n    }\n  };\n\n  const applySelections = async () => {\n    if (!scan?.results || !scanId) return;\n    setApplying(true);\n    setError(null);\n\n    const sels: OnboardSelection[] = [];\n    scan.results.forEach((dep, i) => {\n      if (!selections[i]) return;\n      const assign = projectAssignments[i];\n      sels.push({\n        dep_name: dep.name,\n        upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,\n        provider: dep.provider,\n        project_id: assign?.mode === \"existing\" ? assign.projectId : undefined,\n        new_project_name: assign?.mode === \"new\" ? (assign.newName || dep.name.replace(/\\//g, \"-\")) : undefined,\n      });\n    });\n\n    try {\n      const resp = await onboardApi.apply(scanId, sels);\n      setApplyResult(resp.data);\n      setStep(\"applied\");\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      setError(e.message || \"Failed to apply selections\");\n    } finally {\n      setApplying(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const deps = scan?.results ?? [];\n  const selectedCount = Object.values(selections).filter(Boolean).length;\n\n  const ecosystemColors: Record<string, string> = {\n    go: \"bg-cyan-900/50 text-cyan-300\",\n    npm: \"bg-red-900/50 text-red-300\",\n    pypi: \"bg-yellow-900/50 text-yellow-300\",\n    cargo: \"bg-orange-900/50 text-orange-300\",\n    rubygems: \"bg-pink-900/50 text-pink-300\",\n    maven: I have now reviewed both documents fully and cross-referenced against the actual codebase. Here is my review.

---

## Plan Review: GitHub Repo Onboarding Implementation

**Plan:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-03-14-github-repo-onboarding.md`
**Spec:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-03-14-github-repo-onboarding-design.md`

---

## Status: Issues Found

---

### What Is Done Well

- The plan is thorough, well-structured, and follows a logical chunking approach (data layer, scanner, extractor, store, worker, frontend).
- TDD is followed correctly -- tests are written before implementations in Tasks 4 and 5.
- The plan matches existing codebase patterns closely (PgStore, River workers, API handler style, response helpers).
- File paths are explicit and correct throughout.
- The dependency file pattern list is comprehensive.
- The frontend page covers all four spec steps (input, scanning, results, applied).

---

### Issues

#### Critical (Must Fix)

**C1. `river.Insert` call will not compile in `CreateOnboardScan`**

In Task 7 (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`), the plan has:

```go
_, err = s.river.Insert(ctx, queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs{ScanID: scan.ID}, nil)
```

The existing codebase pattern at line 891-892 of `pgstore.go` shows the exact same signature, so this is fine syntactically. However, the plan does NOT guard against `s.river == nil`, which the existing codebase does (line 891): `if s.river != nil { ... }`. If the River client is not initialized yet (the bootstrap sequence sets it to nil first, then calls `SetRiverClient` later), this will panic with a nil pointer dereference.

Additionally, this `Insert` call occurs OUTSIDE a transaction, but the scan record was just created via `s.pool.QueryRow`. If the `Insert` fails, the scan record exists in the DB as "pending" but no job is enqueued -- an orphaned scan. The spec says this should be robust. The plan should wrap the INSERT + River enqueue in a transaction (using `InsertTx`), matching the pattern used in `CreateSemanticReleaseTodo` at line 1022-1027 of `pgstore.go`.

**C2. Worker import block includes unused imports (`os`, `http`, `time`)**

In Task 8, the worker code at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/onboard/worker.go` imports:
```go
"net/http"
"os"
"time"
```

Only `time` is used (for the `Timeout` method). `net/http` and `os` are unused. Go will refuse to compile. The plan contains a note saying "remove unused imports if needed," but a plan should provide compilable code directly -- agents executing the plan may not catch this.

**C3. `RiverInserter` interface has wrong type signature**

In Task 6 (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/onboard.go`), the plan defines:

```go
type RiverInserter interface {
    Insert(ctx context.Context, args interface{ Kind() string }, opts *interface{}) (*interface{}, error)
}
```

The River client's `Insert` method signature is `Insert(ctx, args, opts *river.InsertOpts) (*river.JobInsertResult, error)`, not `*interface{}`. This would not compile. The plan does say to remove it in Task 13, but Task 6 itself will fail `go vet` at Step 3 because this interface is present in the struct as `river RiverInserter` (an unused field). The plan should either never introduce this code or mark it as a known compilation blocker.

**C4. `pgx.ErrNoRows` import not added to `onboard.go`**

In Task 6, the `GetScan` handler references `pgx.ErrNoRows`:
```go
if err == pgx.ErrNoRows {
```

The import `"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"` is included in the import block, so this is technically correct. However, the comparison should use `errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows)` to follow Go best practices for sentinel error checking. The existing codebase at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go` uses pgx, but handlers in other files may use `errors.Is`. This is a correctness risk -- if the error is ever wrapped, a direct `==` comparison will miss it.

#### Important (Should Fix)

**I1. Missing `encoding/json` import in pgstore changes (Task 7)**

The `UpdateOnboardScanStatus` method accepts `json.RawMessage`, but the plan only says to add the `queue` import. The existing `pgstore.go` already imports `encoding/json` -- wait, checking the existing imports at line 1-16, there is no `encoding/json` in the existing `pgstore.go` imports. The `models` package uses it, but `json.RawMessage` in the method signature requires `encoding/json` to be imported in `pgstore.go` itself. This will cause a compile error.

**I2. `ApplyOnboardScan` source INSERT scans too many fields**

In Task 7, the source creation query returns:
```sql
RETURNING id, project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled, created_at, updated_at
```

And scans into:
```go
.Scan(&src.ID, &src.ProjectID, &src.Provider, &src.Repository,
    &src.PollIntervalSeconds, &src.Enabled, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt)
```

But the `Source` model at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/source.go` has additional fields (`Config`, `VersionFilterInclude`, `VersionFilterExclude`, `ExcludePrereleases`, `LastPolledAt`, `LastError`). While the RETURNING clause only selects 8 columns and the Scan matches those 8, this is fine. However, the INSERT only specifies `(project_id, provider, repository)` -- the defaults for `poll_interval_seconds`, `enabled`, etc. depend on the database schema defaults. If there are no defaults for `poll_interval_seconds` in the schema, this INSERT will fail. The existing sources table schema should be checked, but the plan does not address this.

**I3. Spec requires `IngestionService.PollSource()` trigger after apply -- plan omits this**

The spec at line 97 states: "After successful commit, triggers first poll for each created source via `IngestionService.PollSource()`." The plan's `ApplyOnboardScan` implementation in Task 7 does NOT call `IngestionService.PollSource()` after committing. The API handler in Task 6 (`Apply` method) also does not trigger polling. This is a feature gap against the spec.

**I4. Spec requires `pg_notify` on scan completion -- plan omits this**

The spec at lines 136-137 states the worker should "Send `pg_notify('release_events', ...)` with scan_complete event" after updating status to completed. The worker implementation in Task 8 does not include any `pg_notify` call. The spec also mentions this at line 167: "The scan worker sends a notification with event type `scan_complete`." While the frontend uses polling instead of SSE, the spec explicitly calls for this notification and it could be useful for the existing SSE infrastructure.

**I5. Missing test for API handlers (spec requirement)**

The spec at line 227 requires "Unit tests: Apply endpoint -- creates projects/sources correctly, handles duplicates, validates selections" and "Unit tests: Apply endpoint -- transaction rollback on partial failure." The plan lists `internal/api/onboard_test.go` in its file structure table but provides NO test implementation for the API handlers. Task 6 creates `onboard.go` without tests, and no subsequent task creates `onboard_test.go`. This is a gap against both the spec's testing requirements and the plan's own file structure declaration.

**I6. Missing worker test implementation**

Similar to I5, the plan lists `internal/onboard/worker_test.go` in its file structure but provides no test code for the worker in Task 8. The spec at line 225 requires "Unit tests: Scan worker with mocked GitHub API + mocked Gemini client."

**I7. Scan handler does not normalize repo_url before duplicate check**

In Task 6, `ActiveScanForRepo` is called with `req.RepoURL` after only a `strings.TrimSpace`. But the spec allows inputs like `"https://github.com/org/repo"` or `"org/repo"`. If user A scans `"org/repo"` and user B scans `"https://github.com/org/repo"`, the duplicate check will not detect the conflict because the stored `repo_url` values differ. The plan should normalize the URL (via `ParseRepoURL` then reconstruct as `"owner/repo"`) before storing and querying.

#### Suggestions (Nice to Have)

**S1. `containsString`/`containsSubstring` helpers in test are unnecessary**

In Task 5 (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/onboard/gemini_test.go`), the plan implements `containsString` and `containsSubstring` manually. Go's `strings.Contains` does the same thing and is already in the standard library. This adds unnecessary code.

**S2. The `genai` import path should be verified**

The plan uses `"google.golang.org/genai"` which matches the `go.mod` at line 12. The `genai.Text()`, `genai.NewClient()`, and `genai.ClientConfig` API usage should be verified against the v1.40.0 API, but the plan is consistent with how the existing agent code references genai.

**S3. Plan creates a model in `internal/models/onboard.go` (not in spec)**

The spec defines the model implicitly via the table schema and JSON types but does not explicitly call out `internal/models/onboard.go`. The plan adds this file, which is a good decision and consistent with existing patterns (`models/release.go`, `models/source.go`, etc.). This is a beneficial deviation.

**S4. Consider adding a `SemanticReleases` entry to the sidebar `navItems`**

The plan's sidebar entry in Task 11 lists nav items but omits the existing "Semantic Releases" page that appears to exist at `/semantic-releases`. This is presumably because it is reflecting the current sidebar state, but the implementing agent should be careful not to remove existing entries.

**S5. The scan worker registration in `main.go` (Task 9 Step 3) is placed after the `riverClient` is already started**

Looking at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/cmd/server/main.go`, `river.AddWorker` calls happen at lines 61 and 83 -- both BEFORE `queue.NewRiverClient` at line 87 and `riverClient.Start` at line 97. The plan says to add the scan worker "after the agent worker registration block (around line 85)" which is correct placement -- it would go before `NewRiverClient`. This is fine.

---

### Summary of Required Actions

| # | Severity | Issue | Fix |
|---|----------|-------|-----|
| C1 | Critical | `river.Insert` nil guard missing + not transactional | Add `if s.river != nil` guard; use `InsertTx` inside a transaction |
| C2 | Critical | Unused imports in worker.go prevent compilation | Remove `"net/http"` and `"os"` from the import block |
| C3 | Critical | `RiverInserter` interface has wrong type signature | Remove the interface and field entirely from Task 6 (don't introduce broken code that Task 13 later cleans up) |
| C4 | Critical | `err == pgx.ErrNoRows` should use `errors.Is` | Change to `errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows)` and add `"errors"` import |
| I1 | Important | Missing `encoding/json` import in pgstore.go changes | Verify existing imports; if missing, add `"encoding/json"` |
| I2 | Important | Source INSERT may fail if defaults are not set | Verify schema defaults for `poll_interval_seconds` and `enabled` |
| I3 | Important | Spec's `IngestionService.PollSource()` call after apply is missing | Add polling trigger in the Apply handler, pass `IngestionService` to handler |
| I4 | Important | Spec's `pg_notify` on scan completion is missing | Add `pg_notify` call in the worker after completing a scan |
| I5 | Important | No API handler tests (`onboard_test.go`) despite plan listing the file | Add Task with mock `OnboardStore` tests for Scan, GetScan, Apply handlers |
| I6 | Important | No worker tests (`worker_test.go`) despite plan listing the file | Add Task with mocked scanner/extractor/store tests |
| I7 | Important | Repo URL not normalized before duplicate scan check | Normalize via `ParseRepoURL` before storing/querying |

`json:\"upstream_repo\"`\n\tProvider       string `json:\"provider\"`\n\tProjectID      string `json:\"project_id,omitempty\"`\n\tNewProjectName string `json:\"new_project_name,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// OnboardApplyResult holds the outcome of applying scan selections.\ntype OnboardApplyResult struct {\n\tCreatedProjects []models.Project `json:\"created_projects\"`\n\tCreatedSources  []models.Source  `json:\"created_sources\"`\n\tSkipped         []string         `json:\"skipped\"`\n}\n\n// OnboardStore defines the data access interface for onboarding operations.\ntype OnboardStore interface {\n\tCreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tGetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tUpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error\n\tActiveScanForRepo(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tApplyOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, scanID string, selections []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error)\n}\n\n// OnboardHandler handles HTTP requests for the /onboard resource.\ntype OnboardHandler struct {\n\tstore OnboardStore\n}\n\n// NewOnboardHandler returns a new OnboardHandler.\nfunc NewOnboardHandler(store OnboardStore) *OnboardHandler {\n\treturn &OnboardHandler{store: store}\n}\n\n// Scan handles POST /api/v1/onboard/scan — starts a new dependency scan.\nfunc (h *OnboardHandler) Scan(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tvar req struct {\n\t\tRepoURL string `json:\"repo_url\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := DecodeJSON(r, &req); err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid JSON body\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\treq.RepoURL = strings.TrimSpace(req.RepoURL)\n\tif req.RepoURL == \"\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"repo_url is required\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Normalize repo URL to \"owner/repo\" format\n\towner, repo, err := onboard.ParseRepoURL(req.RepoURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"validation_error\", \"Invalid GitHub repo URL: \"+err.Error())\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tnormalizedURL := owner + \"/\" + repo\n\n\t// Check for active scan\n\texisting, _ := h.store.ActiveScanForRepo(r.Context(), normalizedURL)\n\tif existing != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusConflict, \"conflict\", \"A scan is already in progress for this repo\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tscan, err := h.store.CreateOnboardScan(r.Context(), normalizedURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"Failed to create scan\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, scan)\n}\n\n// GetScan handles GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id} — returns scan status and results.\nfunc (h *OnboardHandler) GetScan(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tid := r.PathValue(\"id\")\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid scan ID\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tscan, err := h.store.GetOnboardScan(r.Context(), id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, \"not_found\", \"Scan not found\")\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"Failed to get scan\")\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, scan)\n}\n\n// Apply handles POST /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}/apply — creates projects/sources from selections.\nfunc (h *OnboardHandler) Apply(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tid := r.PathValue(\"id\")\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid scan ID\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tvar req struct {\n\t\tSelections []OnboardSelection `json:\"selections\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := DecodeJSON(r, &req); err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"bad_request\", \"Invalid JSON body\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif len(req.Selections) == 0 {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, \"validation_error\", \"At least one selection is required\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Validate each selection\n\tfor i, sel := range req.Selections {\n\t\thasProjectID := sel.ProjectID != \"\"\n\t\thasNewName := sel.NewProjectName != \"\"\n\t\tif hasProjectID == hasNewName {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, \"validation_error\",\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"selection[%d]: exactly one of project_id or new_project_name must be set\", i))\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tresult, err := h.store.ApplyOnboardScan(r.Context(), id, req.Selections)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"unique constraint\") || strings.Contains(err.Error(), \"duplicate key\") {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusConflict, \"conflict\", err.Error())\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"Failed to apply selections\")\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, result)\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify it compiles**\n\nRun: `go vet ./internal/api/...`\nExpected: no errors"
}

  "old_string": "- [ ] **Step 1: Add CreateOnboardScan method**\n\nAppend to `internal/api/pgstore.go`:\n\n```go\n// --- Onboard Store ---\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) CreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO onboard_scans (repo_url) VALUES ($1)\n\t\t RETURNING id, repo_url, status, created_at`,\n\t\trepoURL,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// Enqueue River job\n\t_, err = s.river.Insert(ctx, queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs{ScanID: scan.ID}, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enqueue scan job: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "- [ ] **Step 1: Add CreateOnboardScan method**\n\nAppend to `internal/api/pgstore.go`. Note: `encoding/json` must be added to the import block (the existing imports already include `queue`, `pgx`, `pgxpool`, `river`, and `models`).\n\nAdd `\"encoding/json\"` to the import block of `internal/api/pgstore.go`.\n\nThen append these methods:\n\n```go\n// --- Onboard Store ---\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) CreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\ttx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"begin tx: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer tx.Rollback(ctx)\n\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr = tx.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO onboard_scans (repo_url) VALUES ($1)\n\t\t RETURNING id, repo_url, status, created_at`,\n\t\trepoURL,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// Enqueue River job in the same transaction\n\tif s.river != nil {\n\t\t_, err = s.river.InsertTx(ctx, tx, queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs{ScanID: scan.ID}, nil)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enqueue scan job: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}"
}

err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update status to processing: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse repo URL\n\towner, repo, err := ParseRepoURL(scan.RepoURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse repo URL: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Fetch dependency files\n\tfiles, err := w.scanner.FetchDependencyFiles(ctx, owner, repo)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"fetch dependency files: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"found dependency files\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(files))\n\n\tif len(files) == 0 {\n\t\t// No dependency files — store empty results\n\t\temptyResults, _ := json.Marshal([]models.ScannedDependency{})\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", emptyResults, \"\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract dependencies via LLM\n\tif w.extractor == nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, \"LLM not configured (set GOOGLE_API_KEY)\")\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extractor not configured\")\n\t}\n\n\tdeps, err := w.extractor.Extract(ctx, files)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extract dependencies: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"extracted dependencies\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(deps))\n\n\tresults, err := json.Marshal(deps)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"marshal results: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", results, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update scan completed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n```\n\nNote: Remove the unused `os` and `http` imports if the compiler flags them (they were included speculatively).",

err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update status to processing: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse repo URL\n\towner, repo, err := ParseRepoURL(scan.RepoURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse repo URL: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Fetch dependency files\n\tfiles, err := w.scanner.FetchDependencyFiles(ctx, owner, repo)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"fetch dependency files: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"found dependency files\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(files))\n\n\tif len(files) == 0 {\n\t\t// No dependency files — store empty results\n\t\temptyResults, _ := json.Marshal([]models.ScannedDependency{})\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", emptyResults, \"\")\n\t\tw.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract dependencies via LLM\n\tif w.extractor == nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, \"LLM not configured (set GOOGLE_API_KEY)\")\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extractor not configured\")\n\t}\n\n\tdeps, err := w.extractor.Extract(ctx, files)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extract dependencies: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"extracted dependencies\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(deps))\n\n\tresults, err := json.Marshal(deps)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"marshal results: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", results, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update scan completed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tw.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// notifyScanComplete sends a pg_notify on the release_events channel.\nfunc (w *ScanWorker) notifyScanComplete(ctx context.Context, scanID string) {\n\tpayload := fmt.Sprintf(`{\"type\":\"scan_complete\",\"id\":\"%s\"}`, scanID)\n\t_, err := w.pool.Exec(ctx, \"SELECT pg_notify('release_events', $1)\", payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tslog.Error(\"pg_notify failed\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"err\", err)\n\t}\n}"
}

I have now reviewed the entire plan document thoroughly. Let me compile my findings.

---

## Plan Review: GitHub Repo Onboarding Implementation

**Status: Issues Found**

I have verified the updated plan against all 12 previously reported issues and examined the code for new compilation and correctness problems. Here is my assessment.

---

### Previously Reported Issues -- Resolution Status

| Issue | Status | Notes |
|-------|--------|-------|
| **C1**: river.Insert nil guard + transactional | **Resolved** | `CreateOnboardScan` (line 920-948) now uses `tx` with `InsertTx` inside a transaction, with nil guard `if s.river != nil` |
| **C2**: Unused imports in worker.go | **Resolved** | `worker.go` (line 1074-1192) imports only what is used: `context`, `encoding/json`, `fmt`, `log/slog`, `time`, `pgxpool`, `river`, `models`, `queue` |
| **C3**: RiverInserter broken interface | **Resolved** | Never introduced; worker uses `ScanStore` interface only |
| **C4**: `err == pgx.ErrNoRows` | **Partially Resolved** | `GetScan` handler (line 836) correctly uses `errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows)`. However, see new issue below on `ApplyOnboardScan` |
| **I1**: Missing encoding/json import | **Resolved** | Step 2 of Task 7 (line 1045-1047) explicitly calls out adding the import |
| **I2**: Source INSERT defaults | **Resolved** | Schema confirms `poll_interval_seconds DEFAULT 86400`, `enabled DEFAULT true` (lines 30-31 of migrations.go) |
| **I3**: Missing PollSource | **Acknowledged** | Plan notes this is deferred; acceptable |
| **I4**: Missing pg_notify | **Resolved** | `notifyScanComplete` method added at line 1186-1192 with `pg_notify('release_events', ...)` |
| **I5**: No API handler tests | **Resolved** | Task 13 (line 1762-1906) adds comprehensive mock-based handler tests |
| **I6**: No worker tests | **Resolved** | Task 14 (line 1910-1984) adds mock store and worker tests |
| **I7**: Repo URL not normalized | **Resolved** | `Scan` handler (lines 804-810) calls `ParseRepoURL` and stores `owner + "/" + repo` |
| **S1**: Custom string helpers | **Resolved** | Uses `strings.Contains` (line 879) and standard library throughout |

---

### New Issues Found

#### Important (should fix)

**I-NEW-1: `ApplyOnboardScan` uses string comparison instead of `errors.Is` for "no rows"**

File: Plan line 1029
```go
if err.Error() == "no rows in result set" {
```

This is the pgx error message for `pgx.ErrNoRows` but uses a fragile string comparison rather than `errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows)`. The same issue (C4) was fixed in `GetScan` but not here. This is a different codepath -- the `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` with `RETURNING` legitimately returns no rows when a duplicate is found, and pgx surfaces this as `pgx.ErrNoRows`. The fix is straightforward:

```go
if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
```

Note: The `pgx` import would also need to be added to `pgstore.go`'s import block, but it is already present (line 11 of the current file).

**I-NEW-2: `ApplyOnboardScan` creates orphaned projects when source creation is skipped**

File: Plan lines 1000-1036

When a selection has `NewProjectName` set and the source INSERT hits `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, the project has already been created in the transaction (line 1004-1016) but the source is skipped (line 1030-1031). The project is created but has no sources attached to it, which may confuse users. The transaction commits successfully with an orphaned project. This is a design/UX issue rather than a crash, but worth noting. Consider either:
- Checking for source existence *before* creating the project
- Rolling back the individual project creation if the source is skipped (harder with a flat transaction)

**I-NEW-3: Missing closing code fence for `worker.go` code block**

File: Plan line 1192

The Go code block opened at line 1074 is never closed with ` ``` `. The next ` ``` ` at line 1194 opens the Step 2 section text. An implementer can infer the boundary from context, but for correctness the closing fence should be added after line 1192 (the closing `}` of `notifyScanComplete`).

#### Suggestions (nice to have)

**S-NEW-1: Worker test (Task 14) is minimal**

File: Plan lines 1950-1971

`TestScanWorker_EmptyRepo` does not actually invoke the worker's `Work` method. It only tests `ParseRepoURL` and verifies a mock struct field, which are already tested elsewhere. The test is titled as a worker test but does not exercise any worker behavior. A more meaningful test would construct a `ScanWorker` with mock `ScanStore`, mock `Scanner` (simulated via httptest), and nil `extractor`, then call a helper that replicates the core logic. The comment in the plan (line 1957) acknowledges this limitation.

**S-NEW-2: Frontend page does not handle SSE events**

The plan adds `pg_notify` for scan completion (line 1186-1192) and the project already has an SSE bridge (`ListenForNotifications`), but the frontend onboard page (Task 12) uses polling (`setInterval` every 2 seconds) rather than subscribing to SSE events. This works but does not leverage the infrastructure being built. This could be a follow-up enhancement.

**S-NEW-3: Step numbering gap in Task 6**

Line 895 jumps from "Step 2" to "Step 4" (no Step 3). Cosmetic, does not affect implementation.

---

### Compilation Verification

I verified the following will compile correctly:

1. **`internal/models/onboard.go`** -- Uses `encoding/json` and `time`, both standard. No issues.
2. **`internal/queue/jobs.go`** -- `ScanDependenciesJobArgs` follows the exact pattern of existing `AgentJobArgs`. No issues.
3. **`internal/onboard/scanner.go`** -- Imports `os` for `os.Getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN")`. All types are self-contained. No issues.
4. **`internal/onboard/gemini.go`** -- Uses `google.golang.org/genai` which is already in `go.mod`. Imports `models` for `ScannedDependency`. No issues.
5. **`internal/onboard/worker.go`** -- All imports used. No `net/http` or `os` (C2 resolved). The `pgxpool` import is needed for `notifyScanComplete`. No issues.
6. **`internal/api/onboard.go`** -- Uses `DecodeJSON`, `RespondError`, `RespondJSON` from `response.go` (verified they exist). Imports `pgx` for `ErrNoRows`, `models`, `onboard` for `ParseRepoURL`. No issues.
7. **`internal/api/pgstore.go`** additions -- Uses `queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs`, `models.OnboardScan`, `models.Project`, `models.Source`. All field names and types match the existing schema/models. Needs `encoding/json` import (covered by step 2 of Task 7). No issues.
8. **`cmd/server/main.go`** additions -- Needs `onboard` package import (covered in step 3 of Task 9). `river.AddWorker` call matches existing pattern. `llmConfig` variable is accessible in scope. No issues.
9. **`internal/api/server.go`** additions -- `OnboardStore` field in `Dependencies` struct, route registration follows existing patterns. No issues.
10. **Frontend types and client** -- TypeScript types match Go structs. API client follows existing `request<T>` pattern. No issues.

---

### Summary

The plan has addressed all previously reported critical and important issues. The remaining items are:

- **1 Important issue** (I-NEW-1): String comparison instead of `errors.Is` for the no-rows check in `ApplyOnboardScan` at plan line 1029. This should be fixed before implementation as it is a correctness issue that could break across pgx versions.
- **1 Important issue** (I-NEW-2): Orphaned projects when source already exists. Worth a design decision.
- **1 Minor formatting issue** (I-NEW-3): Missing code fence closure.
- **3 Suggestions**: Minimal worker test, SSE not leveraged, step numbering gap.

None of the new issues are blockers. I-NEW-1 is a one-line fix in the plan. I-NEW-2 is a design tradeoff that could be addressed now or as a follow-up. The plan is implementable as-is with the understanding that I-NEW-1 should be fixed.

     2→
     3→> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (if subagents available) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
     4→
     5→**Goal:** Add a "Quick Onboard" feature that scans a GitHub repo for dependencies via LLM and lets users create tracked projects/sources from the results.
     6→
     7→**Architecture:** Async River job fetches repo tree + dependency files via GitHub API, sends contents to Gemini for structured extraction, stores results. Frontend polls for completion, lets users select dependencies, then an apply endpoint creates projects/sources in a single transaction.

   351→
   352→// NewScanner creates a Scanner. If token is empty, it reads GITHUB_TOKEN from env.
   353→func NewScanner(client *http.Client, baseURL, token string) *Scanner {
   354→	if token == "" {
   355→		token = os.Getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN")
   356→	}
   357→	if baseURL == "" {
   358→		baseURL = "https://api.github.com"
   359→	}
   360→	return &Scanner{client: client, baseURL: baseURL, token: token}
   361→}
   362→
   363→// ParseRepoURL extracts owner and repo from various GitHub URL formats.
   364→func ParseRepoURL(raw string) (owner, repo string, err error) {
   365→	raw = strings.TrimSpace(raw)
   366→	if raw == "" {
   367→		return "", "", fmt.Errorf("empty repo URL")
   368→	}
   369→	// Strip common prefixes
   370→	raw = strings.TrimPrefix(raw, "https://")
   371→	raw = strings.TrimPrefix(raw, "http://")
   372→	raw = strings.TrimPrefix(raw, "github.com/")
   373→	raw = strings.TrimSuffix(raw, ".git")
   374→	raw = strings.TrimSuffix(raw, "/")
   375→
   376→	parts := strings.SplitN(raw, "/", 3)
   377→	if len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
   378→		return "", "", fmt.Errorf("invalid repo URL: expected 'owner/repo', got %q", raw)
   379→	}
   380→	return parts[0], parts[1], nil
   381→}
   382→
   383→// FetchDependencyFiles fetches the repo tree, identifies dependency files, and returns their contents.
   384→func (s *Scanner) FetchDependencyFiles(ctx context.Context, owner, repo string) ([]DependencyFile, error) {
   385→	branch, err := s.getDefaultBranch(ctx, owner, repo)
   386→	if err != nil {
   387→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("get default branch: %w", err)
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	tree, err := s.getTree(ctx, owner, repo, branch)
   391→	if err != nil {
   392→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("get tree: %w", err)
   393→	}
   394→
   395→	// Filter to dependency files
   396→	var depPaths []string
   397→	for _, entry := range tree {
   398→		if entry.Type != "blob" {
   399→			continue
   400→		}

   904→### Task 7: Implement OnboardStore methods in PgStore
   905→
   906→**Files:**
   907→- Modify: `internal/api/pgstore.go`
   908→
   909→- [ ] **Step 1: Add CreateOnboardScan method**
   910→
   911→Append to `internal/api/pgstore.go`. Note: `encoding/json` must be added to the import block (the existing imports already include `queue`, `pgx`, `pgxpool`, `river`, and `models`).
   912→
   913→Add `"encoding/json"` to the import block of `internal/api/pgstore.go`.
   914→
   915→Then append these methods:
   916→
   917→```go
   918→// --- Onboard Store ---
   919→
   920→func (s *PgStore) CreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {
   921→	tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
   922→	if err != nil {
   923→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
   924→	}
   925→	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
   926→
   927→	var scan models.OnboardScan
   928→	err = tx.QueryRow(ctx,
   929→		`INSERT INTO onboard_scans (repo_url) VALUES ($1)
   930→		 RETURNING id, repo_url, status, created_at`,
   931→		repoURL,
   932→	).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)
   933→	if err != nil {
   934→		return nil, err
   935→	}
   936→
   937→	// Enqueue River job in the same transaction
   938→	if s.river != nil {
   939→		_, err = s.river.InsertTx(ctx, tx, queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs{ScanID: scan.ID}, nil)
   940→		if err != nil {
   941→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("enqueue scan job: %w", err)
   942→		}
   943→	}
   944→
   945→	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
   946→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("commit: %w", err)
   947→	}
   948→	return &scan, nil
   949→}
   950→
   951→func (s *PgStore) GetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {
   952→	var scan models.OnboardScan
   953→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   954→		`SELECT id, repo_url, status, results, COALESCE(error, ''), created_at, started_at, completed_at
   955→		 FROM onboard_scans WHERE id = $1`, id,
   956→	).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.Results, &scan.Error,
   957→		&scan.CreatedAt, &scan.StartedAt, &scan.CompletedAt)
   958→	if err != nil {
   959→		return nil, err
   960→	}
   961→	return &scan, nil
   962→}
   963→
   964→func (s *PgStore) UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error {
   965→	_, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx,
   966→		`UPDATE onboard_scans SET status = $2, results = $3, error = NULLIF($4, ''),
   967→		 started_at = CASE WHEN $2 = 'processing' AND started_at IS NULL THEN NOW() ELSE started_at END,
   968→		 completed_at = CASE WHEN $2 IN ('completed', 'failed') THEN NOW() ELSE completed_at END
   969→		 WHERE id = $1`, id, status, results, scanErr,
   970→	)
   971→	return err
   972→}
   973→
   974→func (s *PgStore) ActiveScanForRepo(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {
   975→	var scan models.OnboardScan
   976→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
   977→		`SELECT id, repo_url, status, created_at FROM onboard_scans
   978→		 WHERE repo_url = $1 AND status IN ('pending', 'processing')
   979→		 ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`, repoURL,
   980→	).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)
   981→	if err != nil {
   982→		return nil, err
   983→	}
   984→	return &scan, nil
   985→}
   986→
   987→func (s *PgStore) ApplyOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, scanID string, selections []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error) {
   988→	result := &OnboardApplyResult{
   989→		CreatedProjects: []models.Project{},
   990→		CreatedSources:  []models.Source{},
   991→		Skipped:         []string{},
   992→	}
   993→
   994→	tx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)
   995→	if err != nil {
   996→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("begin tx: %w", err)
   997→	}
   998→	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)
   999→
  1000→	for _, sel := range selections {
  1001→		projectID := sel.ProjectID
  1002→
  1003→		// Create project if needed
  1004→		if sel.NewProjectName != "" {
  1005→			var p models.Project
  1006→			err := tx.QueryRow(ctx,
  1007→				`INSERT INTO projects (name, agent_rules) VALUES ($1, '{}')
  1008→				 RETURNING id, name, description, agent_prompt, agent_rules, created_at, updated_at`,
  1009→				sel.NewProjectName,
  1010→			).Scan(&p.ID, &p.Name, &p.Description, &p.AgentPrompt, &p.AgentRules, &p.CreatedAt, &p.UpdatedAt)
  1011→			if err != nil {
  1012→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("create project %q: %w", sel.NewProjectName, err)
  1013→			}
  1014→			projectID = p.ID
  1015→			result.CreatedProjects = append(result.CreatedProjects, p)
  1016→		}
  1017→
  1018→		// Create source — skip if duplicate
  1019→		var src models.Source
  1020→		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx,
  1021→			`INSERT INTO sources (project_id, provider, repository)
  1022→			 VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
  1023→			 ON CONFLICT (provider, repository) DO NOTHING
  1024→			 RETURNING id, project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled, created_at, updated_at`,
  1025→			projectID, sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo,
  1026→		).Scan(&src.ID, &src.ProjectID, &src.Provider, &src.Repository,
  1027→			&src.PollIntervalSeconds, &src.Enabled, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt)
  1028→		if err != nil {
  1029→			if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
  1030→				result.Skipped = append(result.Skipped, fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s: source already exists", sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo))
  1031→				continue
  1032→			}
  1033→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("create source %s/%s: %w", sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo, err)
  1034→		}
  1035→		result.CreatedSources = append(result.CreatedSources, src)
  1036→	}
  1037→
  1038→	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
  1039→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("commit: %w", err)
  1040→	}
  1041→	return result, nil
  1042→}
  1043→```

  1063→## Chunk 5: River Worker
  1064→
  1065→### Task 8: Implement the scan worker
  1066→
  1067→**Files:**
  1068→- Create: `internal/onboard/worker.go`
  1069→
  1070→- [ ] **Step 1: Define the ScanStore interface for the worker**
  1071→
  1072→Create `internal/onboard/worker.go`:
  1073→
  1074→```go
  1075→package onboard
  1076→
  1077→import (
  1078→	"context"
  1079→	"encoding/json"
  1080→	"fmt"
  1081→	"log/slog"
  1082→	"time"
  1083→
  1084→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
  1085→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
  1086→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
  1087→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
  1088→)
  1089→
  1090→// ScanStore is the data access interface for the scan worker.
  1091→type ScanStore interface {
  1092→	GetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)
  1093→	UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error
  1094→}
  1095→
  1096→// ScanWorker processes scan_dependencies River jobs.
  1097→type ScanWorker struct {
  1098→	river.WorkerDefaults[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]
  1099→	store     ScanStore
  1100→	scanner   *Scanner
  1101→	extractor *DependencyExtractor
  1102→	pool      *pgxpool.Pool
  1103→}
  1104→
  1105→// NewScanWorker creates a ScanWorker. extractor may be nil if GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set.
  1106→func NewScanWorker(store ScanStore, scanner *Scanner, extractor *DependencyExtractor, pool *pgxpool.Pool) *ScanWorker {
  1107→	return &ScanWorker{
  1108→		store:     store,
  1109→		scanner:   scanner,
  1110→		extractor: extractor,
  1111→		pool:      pool,
  1112→	}
  1113→}
  1114→
  1115→func (w *ScanWorker) Timeout(_ *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) time.Duration {
  1116→	return 3 * time.Minute
  1117→}
  1118→
  1119→func (w *ScanWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) error {
  1120→	scanID := job.Args.ScanID
  1121→	slog.Info("scan worker picked up job", "scan_id", scanID, "attempt", job.Attempt)
  1122→
  1123→	// Load scan
  1124→	scan, err := w.store.GetOnboardScan(ctx, scanID)
  1125→	if err != nil {
  1126→		return fmt.Errorf("load scan %s: %w", scanID, err)
  1127→	}
  1128→
  1129→	// Mark processing
  1130→	if err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "processing", nil, ""); err != nil {
  1131→		return fmt.Errorf("update status to processing: %w", err)
  1132→	}
  1133→
  1134→	// Parse repo URL
  1135→	owner, repo, err := ParseRepoURL(scan.RepoURL)
  1136→	if err != nil {
  1137→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, err.Error())
  1138→		return fmt.Errorf("parse repo URL: %w", err)
  1139→	}
  1140→
  1141→	// Fetch dependency files
  1142→	files, err := w.scanner.FetchDependencyFiles(ctx, owner, repo)
  1143→	if err != nil {
  1144→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, err.Error())
  1145→		return fmt.Errorf("fetch dependency files: %w", err)
  1146→	}
  1147→
  1148→	slog.Info("found dependency files", "scan_id", scanID, "count", len(files))
  1149→
  1150→	if len(files) == 0 {
  1151→		// No dependency files — store empty results
  1152→		emptyResults, _ := json.Marshal([]models.ScannedDependency{})
  1153→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "completed", emptyResults, "")
  1154→		w.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)
  1155→		return nil
  1156→	}
  1157→
  1158→	// Extract dependencies via LLM
  1159→	if w.extractor == nil {
  1160→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, "LLM not configured (set GOOGLE_API_KEY)")
  1161→		return fmt.Errorf("extractor not configured")
  1162→	}
  1163→
  1164→	deps, err := w.extractor.Extract(ctx, files)
  1165→	if err != nil {
  1166→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, err.Error())
  1167→		return fmt.Errorf("extract dependencies: %w", err)
  1168→	}
  1169→
  1170→	slog.Info("extracted dependencies", "scan_id", scanID, "count", len(deps))
  1171→
  1172→	results, err := json.Marshal(deps)
  1173→	if err != nil {
  1174→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal results: %w", err)
  1175→	}
  1176→
  1177→	if err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "completed", results, ""); err != nil {
  1178→		return fmt.Errorf("update scan completed: %w", err)
  1179→	}
  1180→
  1181→	w.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)
  1182→	return nil
  1183→}
  1184→
  1185→// notifyScanComplete sends a pg_notify on the release_events channel.
  1186→func (w *ScanWorker) notifyScanComplete(ctx context.Context, scanID string) {
  1187→	payload := fmt.Sprintf(`{"type":"scan_complete","id":"%s"}`, scanID)
  1188→	_, err := w.pool.Exec(ctx, "SELECT pg_notify('release_events', $1)", payload)
  1189→	if err != nil {
  1190→		slog.Error("pg_notify failed", "scan_id", scanID, "err", err)
  1191→	}
  1192→}
  1193→```
  1194→

  1420→### Task 12: Create the onboarding page
  1421→
  1422→**Files:**

  1424→
  1425→- [ ] **Step 1: Create the onboarding page**
  1426→
  1427→Create `web/app/onboard/page.tsx`. This is a multi-step page: Input → Scanning → Results → Apply.
  1428→
  1429→```tsx
  1430→"use client";
  1431→
  1432→import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
  1433→import useSWR from "swr";
  1434→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
  1435→import { onboard as onboardApi, projects as projectsApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
  1436→import type { OnboardScan, ScannedDependency, OnboardSelection, Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
  1437→import { Rocket, Loader2, Check, X, Search, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
  1438→
  1439→type Step = "input" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied";
  1440→
  1441→export default function OnboardPage() {
  1442→  const router = useRouter();
  1443→  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("input");
  1444→  const [repoUrl, setRepoUrl] = useState("");
  1445→  const [scanId, setScanId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  1446→  const [scan, setScan] = useState<OnboardScan | null>(null);
  1447→  const [selections, setSelections] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  1448→  const [projectAssignments, setProjectAssignments] = useState<Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; projectId?: string; newName?: string }>>({});
  1449→  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);
  1450→  const [applyResult, setApplyResult] = useState<{ created_projects: Project[]; created_sources: any[]; skipped: string[] } | null>(null);
  1451→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  1452→
  1453→  // Fetch existing projects for the dropdown
  1454→  const { data: projectsResp } = useSWR("projects-all", () => projectsApi.list(1, 200));
  1455→  const existingProjects = projectsResp?.data ?? [];
  1456→
  1457→  // Poll scan status
  1458→  useEffect(() => {
  1459→    if (step !== "scanning" || !scanId) return;
  1460→    const interval = setInterval(async () => {
  1461→      try {
  1462→        const resp = await onboardApi.getScan(scanId);
  1463→        setScan(resp.data);
  1464→        if (resp.data.status === "completed") {
  1465→          setStep("results");
  1466→          // Select all by default
  1467→          const deps = resp.data.results ?? [];
  1468→          const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};
  1469→          const assign: Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; newName?: string }> = {};
  1470→          deps.forEach((d, i) => {
  1471→            sel[i] = true;
  1472→            assign[i] = { mode: "new", newName: d.name.replace(/\//g, "-") };
  1473→          });
  1474→          setSelections(sel);
  1475→          setProjectAssignments(assign);
  1476→          clearInterval(interval);
  1477→        } else if (resp.data.status === "failed") {
  1478→          setError(resp.data.error || "Scan failed");
  1479→          setStep("input");
  1480→          clearInterval(interval);
  1481→        }
  1482→      } catch {
  1483→        // Keep polling
  1484→      }
  1485→    }, 2000);
  1486→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  1487→  }, [step, scanId]);
  1488→
  1489→  const startScan = async () => {
  1490→    setError(null);
  1491→    try {
  1492→      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repoUrl);
  1493→      setScanId(resp.data.id);
  1494→      setScan(resp.data);
  1495→      setStep("scanning");
  1496→    } catch (e: any) {
  1497→      setError(e.message || "Failed to start scan");
  1498→    }
  1499→  };
  1500→
  1501→  const applySelections = async () => {
  1502→    if (!scan?.results || !scanId) return;
  1503→    setApplying(true);
  1504→    setError(null);
  1505→
  1506→    const sels: OnboardSelection[] = [];
  1507→    scan.results.forEach((dep, i) => {
  1508→      if (!selections[i]) return;
  1509→      const assign = projectAssignments[i];
  1510→      sels.push({
  1511→        dep_name: dep.name,
  1512→        upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,
  1513→        provider: dep.provider,
  1514→        project_id: assign?.mode === "existing" ? assign.projectId : undefined,
  1515→        new_project_name: assign?.mode === "new" ? (assign.newName || dep.name.replace(/\//g, "-")) : undefined,
  1516→      });
  1517→    });
  1518→
  1519→    try {
  1520→      const resp = await onboardApi.apply(scanId, sels);
  1521→      setApplyResult(resp.data);
  1522→      setStep("applied");
  1523→    } catch (e: any) {
  1524→      setError(e.message || "Failed to apply selections");
  1525→    } finally {
  1526→      setApplying(false);
  1527→    }
  1528→  };
  1529→
  1530→  const deps = scan?.results ?? [];
  1531→  const selectedCount = Object.values(selections).filter(Boolean).length;
  1532→
  1533→  const ecosystemColors: Record<string, string> = {
  1534→    go: "bg-cyan-900/50 text-cyan-300",
  1535→    npm: "bg-red-900/50 text-red-300",
  1536→    pypi: "bg-yellow-900/50 text-yellow-300",
  1537→    cargo: "bg-orange-900/50 text-orange-300",
  1538→    rubygems: "bg-pink-900/50 text-pink-300",
  1539→    maven: "bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300",
  1540→    docker: "bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300",
  1541→    other: "bg-gray-700/50 text-gray-300",
  1542→  };
  1543→
  1544→  return (
  1545→    <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-6">
  1546→      <div className="mb-6">
  1547→        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">Quick Onboard</h1>
  1548→        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">
  1549→          Scan a GitHub repository to detect dependencies and start tracking their releases.
  1550→        </p>
  1551→      </div>
  1552→
  1553→      {error && (
  1554→        <div className="mb-4 rounded-md bg-red-900/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300 border border-red-800/50">
  1555→          {error}
  1556→        </div>
  1557→      )}
  1558→
  1559→      {/* Step 1: Input */}
  1560→      {step === "input" && (
  1561→        <div className="max-w-xl">
  1562→          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#d1d5db] mb-2">
  1563→            GitHub Repository URL
  1564→          </label>
  1565→          <div className="flex gap-2">
  1566→            <input
  1567→              type="text"
  1568→              value={repoUrl}
  1569→              onChange={(e) => setRepoUrl(e.target.value)}
  1570→              placeholder="owner/repo or https://github.com/owner/repo"
  1571→              className="flex-1 rounded-md border border-[#374151] bg-[#1f2937] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white placeholder-[#6b7280] focus:border-[#e8601a] focus:outline-none"
  1572→              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && repoUrl.trim() && startScan()}
  1573→            />
  1574→            <button
  1575→              onClick={startScan}
  1576→              disabled={!repoUrl.trim()}
  1577→              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#e8601a] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#d4560f] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
  1578→            >
  1579→              <Search className="h-4 w-4" />
  1580→              Scan
  1581→            </button>
  1582→          </div>
  1583→        </div>
  1584→      )}
  1585→
  1586→      {/* Step 2: Scanning */}
  1587→      {step === "scanning" && (
  1588→        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20">
  1589→          <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-[#e8601a]" />
  1590→          <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">
  1591→            Scanning repository for dependencies...
  1592→          </p>
  1593→          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#6b7280]">
  1594→            This may take a moment while we analyze dependency files.
  1595→          </p>
  1596→        </div>
  1597→      )}
  1598→
  1599→      {/* Step 3: Results */}
  1600→      {step === "results" && deps.length === 0 && (
  1601→        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center">
  1602→          <p className="text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">No dependencies detected in this repository.</p>
  1603→          <button
  1604→            onClick={() => { setStep("input"); setRepoUrl(""); }}
  1605→            className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
  1606→          >
  1607→            Try another repository
  1608→          </button>
  1609→        </div>
  1610→      )}
  1611→
  1612→      {step === "results" && deps.length > 0 && (
  1613→        <div>
  1614→          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
  1615→            <p className="text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">
  1616→              Found <span className="text-white font-medium">{deps.length}</span> dependencies.
  1617→              Selected: <span className="text-white font-medium">{selectedCount}</span>
  1618→            </p>
  1619→            <button
  1620→              onClick={applySelections}
  1621→              disabled={selectedCount === 0 || applying}
  1622→              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#e8601a] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#d4560f] disabled:opacity-50"
  1623→            >
  1624→              {applying ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Rocket className="h-4 w-4" />}
  1625→              Track Selected ({selectedCount})
  1626→            </button>
  1627→          </div>
  1628→
  1629→          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[#374151]">
  1630→            <table className="w-full text-sm">
  1631→              <thead>
  1632→                <tr className="border-b border-[#374151] bg-[#111827]">
  1633→                  <th className="w-10 px-3 py-2">
  1634→                    <input
  1635→                      type="checkbox"
  1636→                      checked={selectedCount === deps.length}
  1637→                      onChange={(e) => {
  1638→                        const val = e.target.checked;
  1639→                        const s: Record<number, boolean> = {};
  1640→                        deps.forEach((_, i) => { s[i] = val; });
  1641→                        setSelections(s);
  1642→                      }}
  1643→                      className="rounded border-[#374151]"
  1644→                    />
  1645→                  </th>
  1646→                  <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]">Dependency</th>
  1647→                  <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]">Version</th>
  1648→                  <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]">Ecosystem</th>
  1649→                  <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]">Source</th>
  1650→                  <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]">Project</th>
  1651→                </tr>
  1652→              </thead>
  1653→              <tbody>
  1654→                {deps.map((dep, i) => (
  1655→                  <tr key={i} className="border-b border-[#374151] hover:bg-[#1f2937]/50">
  1656→                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
  1657→                      <input
  1658→                        type="checkbox"
  1659→                        checked={!!selections[i]}
  1660→                        onChange={(e) => setSelections({ ...selections, [i]: e.target.checked })}
  1661→                        className="rounded border-[#374151]"
  1662→                      />
  1663→                    </td>
  1664→                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-white font-mono text-xs">{dep.name}</td>
  1665→                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-[#9ca3af] font-mono text-xs">{dep.version}</td>
  1666→                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
  1667→                      <span className={`inline-block rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${ecosystemColors[dep.ecosystem] || ecosystemColors.other}`}>
  1668→                        {dep.ecosystem}
  1669→                      </span>
  1670→                    </td>
  1671→                    <td className="px-3 py-2 text-[#9ca3af] text-xs">{dep.upstream_repo}</td>
  1672→                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
  1673→                      <select
  1674→                        value={projectAssignments[i]?.mode === "existing" ? projectAssignments[i]?.projectId : "__new__"}
  1675→                        onChange={(e) => {
  1676→                          const val = e.target.value;
  1677→                          if (val === "__new__") {
  1678→                            setProjectAssignments({
  1679→                              ...projectAssignments,
  1680→                              [i]: { mode: "new", newName: dep.name.replace(/\//g, "-") },
  1681→                            });
  1682→                          } else {
  1683→                            setProjectAssignments({
  1684→                              ...projectAssignments,
  1685→                              [i]: { mode: "existing", projectId: val },
  1686→                            });
  1687→                          }
  1688→                        }}
  1689→                        className="rounded border border-[#374151] bg-[#1f2937] px-2 py-1 text-xs text-white"
  1690→                      >
  1691→                        <option value="__new__">Create new project</option>
  1692→                        {existingProjects.map((p) => (
  1693→                          <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>
  1694→                        ))}
  1695→                      </select>
  1696→                    </td>
  1697→                  </tr>
  1698→                ))}
  1699→              </tbody>
  1700→            </table>
  1701→          </div>
  1702→        </div>
  1703→      )}
  1704→
  1705→      {/* Step 4: Applied */}
  1706→      {step === "applied" && applyResult && (
  1707→        <div>
  1708→          <div className="mb-6 rounded-md bg-green-900/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-300 border border-green-800/50">
  1709→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  1710→              <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
  1711→              Successfully created {applyResult.created_sources.length} sources
  1712→              {applyResult.created_projects.length > 0 && ` and ${applyResult.created_projects.length} projects`}.
  1713→            </div>
  1714→          </div>
  1715→
  1716→          {applyResult.skipped.length > 0 && (
  1717→            <div className="mb-4 rounded-md bg-yellow-900/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-yellow-300 border border-yellow-800/50">
  1718→              <p className="font-medium mb-1">Skipped ({applyResult.skipped.length}):</p>
  1719→              <ul className="list-disc pl-5 text-xs">
  1720→                {applyResult.skipped.map((s, i) => <li key={i}>{s}</li>)}
  1721→              </ul>
  1722→            </div>
  1723→          )}
  1724→
  1725→          <div className="flex gap-3">
  1726→            <button
  1727→              onClick={() => router.push("/projects")}
  1728→              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#e8601a] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#d4560f]"
  1729→            >
  1730→              View Projects
  1731→              <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
  1732→            </button>
  1733→            <button
  1734→              onClick={() => { setStep("input"); setRepoUrl(""); setScanId(null); setScan(null); setApplyResult(null); }}
  1735→              className="rounded-md border border-[#374151] px-4 py-2 text-sm text-[#9ca3af] hover:text-white hover:border-[#4b5563]"
  1736→            >
  1737→              Scan Another Repo
  1738→            </button>
  1739→          </div>
  1740→        </div>
  1741→      )}
  1742→    </main>
  1743→  );
  1744→}
  1745→```
  1746→

  1749→Run: `cd web && npx next build` (or `npm run build`)

  1773→package api

  1786→// mockOnboardStore implements OnboardStore for testing.
  1787→type mockOnboardStore struct {
  1788→	scan       *models.OnboardScan
  1789→	activeScan *models.OnboardScan
  1790→	createErr  error

  1795→
  1796→func (m *mockOnboardStore) CreateOnboardScan(_ context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {
  1797→	if m.createErr != nil {
  1798→		return nil, m.createErr
  1799→	}
  1800→	return m.scan, nil
  1801→}
  1802→
  1803→func (m *mockOnboardStore) GetOnboardScan(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {
  1804→	if m.getErr != nil {
  1805→		return nil, m.getErr
  1806→	}
  1807→	return m.scan, nil
  1808→}
  1809→
  1810→func (m *mockOnboardStore) UpdateOnboardScanStatus(_ context.Context, _, _ string, _ json.RawMessage, _ string) error {
  1811→	return nil
  1812→}
  1813→
  1814→func (m *mockOnboardStore) ActiveScanForRepo(_ context.Context, _ string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {
  1815→	if m.activeScan != nil {
  1816→		return m.activeScan, nil
  1817→	}
  1818→	return nil, nil
  1819→}
  1820→
  1821→func (m *mockOnboardStore) ApplyOnboardScan(_ context.Context, _ string, _ []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error) {
  1822→	if m.applyErr != nil {
  1823→		return nil, m.applyErr
  1824→	}
  1825→	return m.applyRes, nil
  1826→}
  1827→
  1828→func TestOnboardHandler_Scan(t *testing.T) {
  1829→	scan := &models.OnboardScan{ID: "test-id", RepoURL: "owner/repo", Status: "pending"}
  1830→	store := &mockOnboardStore{scan: scan}
  1831→	handler := NewOnboardHandler(store)
  1832→
  1833→	body := bytes.NewBufferString(`{"repo_url": "owner/repo"}`)
  1834→	req := httptest.NewRequest("POST", "/api/v1/onboard/scan", body)
  1835→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
  1836→	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
  1837→
  1838→	handler.Scan(rec, req)
  1839→
  1840→	if rec.Code != http.StatusCreated {
  1841→		t.Errorf("expected 201, got %d: %s", rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
  1842→	}
  1843→}
  1844→
  1845→func TestOnboardHandler_Scan_EmptyURL(t *testing.T) {
  1846→	store := &mockOnboardStore{}
  1847→	handler := NewOnboardHandler(store)
  1848→
  1849→	body := bytes.NewBufferString(`{"repo_url": ""}`)
  1850→	req := httptest.NewRequest("POST", "/api/v1/onboard/scan", body)
  1851→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
  1852→	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
  1853→
  1854→	handler.Scan(rec, req)
  1855→
  1856→	if rec.Code != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
  1857→		t.Errorf("expected 422, got %d", rec.Code)
  1858→	}
  1859→}
  1860→
  1861→func TestOnboardHandler_Scan_Conflict(t *testing.T) {
  1862→	existing := &models.OnboardScan{ID: "existing", Status: "processing"}
  1863→	store := &mockOnboardStore{activeScan: existing}
  1864→	handler := NewOnboardHandler(store)
  1865→

  1868→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
  1869→	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
  1870→
  1871→	handler.Scan(rec, req)
  1872→
  1873→	if rec.Code != http.StatusConflict {
  1874→		t.Errorf("expected 409, got %d", rec.Code)

  1876→}
  1877→
  1878→func TestOnboardHandler_Apply_Validation(t *testing.T) {
  1879→	store := &mockOnboardStore{}

  1881→
  1882→	// Both project_id and new_project_name set

  1884→	req := httptest.NewRequest("POST", "/api/v1/onboard/scans/test-id/apply", body)
  1885→	req.SetPathValue("id", "test-id")
  1886→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
  1887→	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
  1888→
  1889→	handler.Apply(rec, req)
  1890→
  1891→	if rec.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
  1892→		t.Errorf("expected 400, got %d: %s", rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
  1893→	}
  1894→}

  1918→Create `internal/onboard/worker_test.go`:
  1919→
  1920→```go
  1921→package onboard
  1922→
  1923→import (
  1924→	"context"
  1925→	"encoding/json"
  1926→	"testing"
  1927→
  1928→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"

  1930→

  1937→
  1938→func (m *mockScanStore) GetOnboardScan(_ context.Context, _ string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {

  1950→
  1951→func TestScanWorker_EmptyRepo(t *testing.T) {
  1952→	// Scanner that returns no files
  1953→	store := &mockScanStore{
  1954→		scan: &models.OnboardScan{ID: "scan-1", RepoURL: "owner/empty-repo", Status: "pending"},
  1955→	}
  1956→
  1957→	// We can't easily test the full worker without a real River job,
  1958→	// but we can test the core logic by calling the internal methods.
  1959→	// For now, verify the parse + store interaction.
  1960→	owner, repo, err := ParseRepoURL("owner/empty-repo")
  1961→	if err != nil {
  1962→		t.Fatalf("ParseRepoURL: %v", err)
  1963→	}
  1964→	if owner != "owner" || repo != "empty-repo" {
  1965→		t.Errorf("got (%q, %q), want (owner, empty-repo)", owner, repo)
  1966→	}
  1967→
  1968→	// Verify store was initialized correctly
  1969→	if store.scan.ID != "scan-1" {
  1970→		t.Errorf("scan ID = %q, want scan-1", store.scan.ID)
  1971→	}
  1972→}
