```
     1→package db
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
     8→	"github.com/riverqueue/river/riverdriver/riverpgxv5"
     9→	"github.com/riverqueue/river/rivermigrate"
    10→)
    11→
    12→const schema = `
    13→-- Tracked software projects (the central entity)
    14→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
    15→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    16→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    17→    description TEXT,
    18→    agent_prompt TEXT,
    19→    agent_rules JSONB,
    20→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    21→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    22→);
    23→
    24→-- Configured ingestion sources (polling-based: GitHub, Docker Hub)
    25→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sources (
    26→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    27→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    28→    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    29→    repository VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    30→    poll_interval_seconds INT DEFAULT 86400,
    31→    enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    32→    config JSONB,
    33→    last_polled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    34→    last_error TEXT,
    35→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    36→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    37→    UNIQUE(provider, repository)
    38→);
    39→
    40→-- Context sources (read-only references for agent research)
    41→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS context_sources (
    42→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    43→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    44→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    45→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    46→    config JSONB NOT NULL,
    47→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    48→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    49→);
    50→
    51→-- Source-level releases (detected from polling sources)
    52→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS releases (
    53→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    54→    source_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    55→    version VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    56→    raw_data JSONB,
    57→    released_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    58→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    59→    UNIQUE(source_id, version)
    60→);
    61→
    62→-- Project-level semantic releases (AI-generated, correlating source releases)
    63→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS semantic_releases (
    64→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    65→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    66→    version VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    67→    report JSONB,
    68→    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    69→    error TEXT,
    70→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    71→    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    72→    UNIQUE(project_id, version)
    73→);
    74→
    75→-- Join table: which source releases compose a semantic release
    76→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS semantic_release_sources (
    77→    semantic_release_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    78→    release_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    79→    PRIMARY KEY (semantic_release_id, release_id)
    80→);
    81→
    82→-- Notification channels (standalone)
    83→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notification_channels (
    84→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    85→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    86→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    87→    config JSONB NOT NULL,
    88→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    89→    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
    90→);
    91→
    92→-- Subscriptions: two types (source releases and semantic releases)
    93→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subscriptions (
    94→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    95→    channel_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES notification_channels(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    96→    type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('source_release', 'semantic_release')),
    97→    source_id UUID REFERENCES sources(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    98→    project_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    99→    version_filter TEXT,
   100→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   101→    CHECK (
   102→        (type = 'source_release'   AND source_id  IS NOT NULL AND project_id IS NULL) OR
   103→        (type = 'semantic_release' AND project_id IS NOT NULL AND source_id  IS NULL)
   104→    )
   105→);
   106→
   107→-- Agent runs (scoped to project)
   108→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_runs (
   109→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   110→    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   111→    semantic_release_id UUID REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
   112→    trigger VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
   113→    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
   114→    prompt_used TEXT,
   115→    error TEXT,
   116→    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   117→    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   118→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
   119→);
   120→
   121→ALTER TABLE agent_runs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version VARCHAR(100);
   122→
   123→-- API authentication keys
   124→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
   125→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   126→    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
   127→    key_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
   128→    key_prefix VARCHAR(12) NOT NULL,
   129→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   130→    last_used_at TIMESTAMPTZ
   131→);
   132→
   133→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on new releases
   134→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_release_created() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   135→BEGIN
   136→    PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   137→    RETURN NEW;
   138→END;
   139→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   140→
   141→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS release_created_trigger ON releases;
   142→CREATE TRIGGER release_created_trigger
   143→    AFTER INSERT ON releases
   144→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_release_created();
   145→
   146→-- Trigger for SSE: notify on semantic release completion
   147→CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release() RETURNS trigger AS $$
   148→BEGIN
   149→    IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND (OLD IS NULL OR OLD.status != 'completed') THEN
   150→        PERFORM pg_notify('release_events', json_build_object('type', 'semantic_release', 'id', NEW.id)::text);
   151→    END IF;
   152→    RETURN NEW;
   153→END;
   154→$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
   155→
   156→DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS semantic_release_trigger ON semantic_releases;
   157→CREATE TRIGGER semantic_release_trigger
   158→    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON semantic_releases
   159→    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_semantic_release();
   160→
   161→-- Release TODOs (acknowledge/resolve tracking)
   162→CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS release_todos (
   163→    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
   164→    release_id UUID REFERENCES releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   165→    semantic_release_id UUID REFERENCES semantic_releases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   166→    status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
   167→    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
   168→    acknowledged_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   169→    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
   170→    CHECK (
   171→        (release_id IS NOT NULL AND semantic_release_id IS NULL) OR
   172→        (release_id IS NULL AND semantic_release_id IS NOT NULL)
   173→    )
   174→);
   175→CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_release_todos_release_id ON release_todos(release_id) WHERE release_id IS NOT NULL;
   176→CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_release_todos_semantic_release_id ON release_todos(semantic_release_id) WHERE semantic_release_id IS NOT NULL;
   177→`
   178→
   179→// RunMigrations applies River's schema and the application schema. Idempotent — safe to call on every startup.
   180→func RunMigrations(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool) error {
   181→	migrator, err := rivermigrate.New(riverpgxv5.New(pool), nil)
   182→	if err != nil {
   183→		return fmt.Errorf("create river migrator: %w", err)
   184→	}
   185→	if _, err := migrator.Migrate(ctx, rivermigrate.DirectionUp, nil); err != nil {
   186→		return fmt.Errorf("river migrations: %w", err)
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, schema); err != nil {

depFileNames = map[string]bool{\n\t\"go.mod\":            true,\n\t\"go.sum\":            false, // skip, too large and low value\n\t\"package.json\":      true,\n\t\"package-lock.json\": baseURL == \"\" {\n\t\tbaseURL = \"https://api.github.com\"\n\t}\n\treturn &Scanner{client: client, baseURL: baseURL, token: token}\n}\n\n// ParseRepoURL extracts owner and repo from various GitHub URL formats.\nfunc ParseRepoURL(raw string) (owner, repo string, err error) {\n\traw = strings.TrimSpace(raw)\n\tif raw == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"empty repo URL\")\n\t}\n\t// \".git\")\n\traw = strings.TrimSuffix(raw, \"/\")\n\n\tparts := strings.SplitN(raw, \"/\", 3)\n\tif len(parts) < 2 || parts[0] == \"\" || parts[1] == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", returns their contents.\nfunc (s *Scanner) FetchDependencyFiles(ctx context.Context, owner, repo string) ([]DependencyFile, error) {\n\tbranch, err := s.getDefaultBranch(ctx, owner, repo)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"get default branch: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttree, err := s.getTree(ctx, owner, repo, branch)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"get tree: %w\", owner, repo, branch string) ([]treeEntry, error) {\n\turl := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/repos/%s/%s/git/trees/%s?recursive=1\", s.baseURL, owner, repo, branch)\n\tvar result struct {\n\t\tTree []treeEntry `json:\"tree\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := s.doGet(ctx, url, &result); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn result.Tree, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *Scanner) getFileContent(ctx context.Context, owner, repo, !strings.Contains(prompt, \"go.mod\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"prompt should contain file path\")\n\t}\n\tif !strings.Contains(prompt, \"module example.com\") {\n\t\tt.Error(\"prompt should contain file content\")\n\t}\n}\n```\n\n### Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail\n\nRun: `go test ./internal/onboard/... -v -run TestParseDependencies`\nExpected: FAIL\n\n### Step 3: Implement gemini.go\n\nCreate `internal/onboard/gemini.go`:\n\n```go\npackage onboard\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n\t\"google.golang.org/genai\"\n)\n\nconst extractionSystemPrompt = `You are a dependency extraction agent. Given the contents of dependency/manifest\nfiles from a software project, extract all dependencies.\n\nFor each dependency, return:\n- name: the package/library name\n- version: the version constraint or pinned version\n- ecosystem: one of \"go\", \"npm\", \"pypi\", \"cargo\", \"rubygems\", \"maven\", \"gradle\", \"docker\", \"other\"\n- upstream_repo: your best guess at the canonical GitHub repository URL (e.g., \"github.com/gorilla/mux\")\n- provider: the Changelogue provider to use for release tracking. Use \"github\" for repos\n  with GitHub releases, \"dockerhub\" for Docker images, \"gitlab\" for GitLab repos,\n  \"ecr_public\" for AWS ECR images. When unsure, default to \"github\".\n\nReturn ONLY a JSON array. No explanations.`\n\n// DependencyExtractor uses Gemini to extract dependencies from file contents.\ntype DependencyExtractor struct {\n\tclient *genai.Client\n\tmodel  string\n}\n\n// NewDependencyExtractor creates a DependencyExtractor.\nfunc NewDependencyExtractor(ctx context.Context, apiKey, model string) (*DependencyExtractor, error) {\n\tclient, err := genai.NewClient(ctx, &genai.ClientConfig{\n\t\tAPIKey:  apiKey,\n\t\tBackend: genai.BackendGeminiAPI,\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create genai client: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif model == \"\" {\n\t\tmodel = \"gemini-2.0-flash\"\n\t}\n\treturn &DependencyExtractor{client: client, model: model}, nil\n}\n\n// Extract sends file contents to Gemini and returns parsed dependencies.\nfunc (e *DependencyExtractor) Extract(ctx context.Context, files []DependencyFile) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {\n\tprompt := BuildExtractionPrompt(files)\n\n\tresp, err := e.client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, e.model, genai.NewContentFromText(prompt), &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\t\tSystemInstruction: genai.NewContentFromText(extractionSystemPrompt),\n\t\tTemperature:       genai.Ptr(float32(0.1)),\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"generate content: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(resp.Candidates) == 0 || len(resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"empty response from Gemini\")\n\t}\n\n\ttext := resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts[0].Text\n\treturn ParseDependencies([]byte(text))\n}\n\n// BuildExtractionPrompt constructs the user prompt from dependency files.\nfunc BuildExtractionPrompt(files []DependencyFile) string {\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.WriteString(\"Extract all dependencies from these files:\\n\\n\")\n\tfor _, f := range files {\n\t\tb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"--- %s ---\\n%s\\n\\n\", f.Path, f.Content))\n\t}\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// ParseDependencies parses the LLM response into ScannedDependency structs.\n// Handles raw JSON arrays and markdown-fenced JSON.\nfunc ParseDependencies(raw []byte) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {\n\ttext := strings.TrimSpace(string(raw))\n\n\t// Strip markdown code fences if present\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(text, \"```\") {\n\t\tlines := strings.Split(text, \"\\n\")\n\t\tif len(lines) > 2 {\n\t\t\t// Remove first and last lines (``` markers)\n\t\t\tlines = lines[1 : len(lines)-1]\n\t\t\t// If last line is also ```, it was already removed\n\t\t\tif len(lines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]) == \"```\" {\n\t\t\t\tlines = lines[:len(lines)-1]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttext = strings.Join(lines, \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar deps []models.ScannedDependency\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(text), &deps); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"parse dependencies JSON: %w\\nraw: %s\", err, text)\n\t}\n\treturn deps, nil\n}\n```\n\n### Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass\n\nRun: `go test ./internal/onboard/... -v`\nExpected: PASS\n\n### Step 5: Commit\n\n```bash\ngit add internal/onboard/gemini.go internal/onboard/gemini_test.go\ngit commit -m \"feat(onboard): implement Gemini dependency extractor with tests\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\"\n```\n\n## Important Notes\n- Use exact code provided — do not modify it\n- The `DependencyFile` type is already defined in `scanner.go` (from a parallel task being committed separately). If `scanner.go` doesn't exist yet, that's fine — the test file only uses `DependencyFile` in `TestBuildPrompt` and the `ParseDependencies` tests don't need it. If `scanner.go` isn't present yet, you may need to define `DependencyFile` locally or wait — but it SHOULD be there. Check first.\n- Run tests to verify compilation and correctness\n- If tests fail, debug and fix\n",

}

## Implementation Summary

### Files Created

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/onboard/scanner_test.go`** - Test file written first (TDD red phase), covering:

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/onboard/scanner.go`** - Implementation providing:

- `DependencyFile` struct - holds path and decoded content of a manifest file
- `depFileNames` map - allowlist of known dependency/manifest filenames (`go.mod`, `package.json`, `requirements.txt`, `Cargo.toml`, `Dockerfile`, etc.), with `false` values used to explicitly exclude noisy files like `go.sum`, `yarn.lock`, and `package-lock.json`
- `Scanner` struct - wraps an `*http.Client`, a configurable base URL (defaults to `https://api.github.com`), and a GitHub token (falls back to `GITHUB_TOKEN` env var)
- `NewScanner(client, baseURL, token)` - constructor
- `ParseRepoURL(raw string)` - parses all common GitHub repo URL formats into `(owner, repo, error)`
- `FetchDependencyFiles(ctx, owner, repo)` - orchestrates three GitHub API calls: get default branch, get recursive file tree, fetch content only for matching dependency files (decoding base64 content)

## Summary

### Files Created

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/onboard/gemini_test.go`** — TDD test file created first (as required). Contains 4 tests:
- `TestParseDependencies_ValidJSON` — verifies parsing of a clean JSON array with 2 dependencies

- `TestParseDependencies_EmptyArray` — verifies handling of an empty JSON array

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/onboard/gemini.go`** — Implementation file with:
- `DependencyExtractor` struct wrapping `*genai.Client`
- `NewDependencyExtractor(ctx, apiKey, model)` — creates a client using `genai.BackendGeminiAPI`, defaults model to `gemini-2.0-flash`
- `Extract(ctx, files)` — calls `e.client.Models.GenerateContent` with the prompt wrapped as `[]*genai.Content{genai.NewContentFromText(prompt, "user")}` (the correct v1.40.0 API signature)
- `BuildExtractionPrompt(files)` — formats dependency files into a user prompt with `--- path ---` separators
- `ParseDependencies(raw)` — strips markdown code fences, then JSON-unmarshals into `[]models.ScannedDependency`

### Key Fix Applied
The provided task code used incorrect genai API calls. The actual `google.golang.org/genai@v1.40.0` API requires:
- `genai.NewContentFromText(text, role)` — requires a `role` argument (e.g. `"user"`)
- `GenerateContent(..., []*genai.Content{...}, ...)` — takes a slice, not a single `*genai.Content`

  1442→func (s *PgStore) ReopenTodo(ctx context.Context, id string) error {
  1443→	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx,
  1444→		`UPDATE release_todos SET status = 'pending', acknowledged_at = NULL, resolved_at = NULL WHERE id = $1`, id)
  1445→	if err != nil {
  1446→		return fmt.Errorf("reopen todo: %w", err)
  1447→	}
  1448→	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
  1449→		return fmt.Errorf("todo not found")
  1450→	}
  1451→	return nil
  1452→}
  1453→
  1454→// CreateReleaseTodo inserts a TODO for a source release. Returns the todo ID.
  1455→// Uses ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE for idempotency.
  1456→func (s *PgStore) CreateReleaseTodo(ctx context.Context, releaseID string) (string, error) {
  1457→	var id string
  1458→	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,
  1459→		`INSERT INTO release_todos (release_id) VALUES ($1)
  1460→		 ON CONFLICT (release_id) WHERE release_id IS NOT NULL DO UPDATE SET release_id = EXCLUDED.release_id
  1461→		 RETURNING id`, releaseID).Scan(&id)
  1462→	if err != nil {
  1463→		return "", fmt.Errorf("create release todo: %w", err)
  1464→	}
  1465→	return id, nil
  1466→}

    53→		SystemInstruction: genai.NewContentFromText(extractionSystemPrompt, "user"),
    54→		Temperature:       genai.Ptr(float32(0.1)),
    55→	})
    56→	if err != nil {
    57→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("generate content: %w", err)
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	if len(resp.Candidates) == 0 || len(resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts) == 0 {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("empty response from Gemini")
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	text := resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts[0].Text
    65→	return ParseDependencies([]byte(text))
    66→}
    67→
    68→// BuildExtractionPrompt constructs the user prompt from dependency files.
    69→func BuildExtractionPrompt(files []DependencyFile) string {
    70→	var b strings.Builder
    71→	b.WriteString("Extract all dependencies from these files:\n\n")
    72→	for _, f := range files {
    73→		b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("--- %s ---\n%s\n\n", f.Path, f.Content))
    74→	}
    75→	return b.String()
    76→}
    77→
    78→// ParseDependencies parses the LLM response into ScannedDependency structs.
    79→// Handles raw JSON arrays and markdown-fenced JSON.
    80→func ParseDependencies(raw []byte) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {
    81→	text := strings.TrimSpace(string(raw))
    82→
    83→	// Strip markdown code fences if present
    84→	if strings.HasPrefix(text, "```") {
    85→		lines := strings.Split(text, "\n")
    86→		if len(lines) > 2 {
    87→			// Remove first and last lines (``` markers)
    88→			lines = lines[1 : len(lines)-1]
    89→			// If last line is also ```, it was already removed
    90→			if len(lines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]) == "```" {
    91→				lines = lines[:len(lines)-1]
    92→			}
    93→			text = strings.Join(lines, "\n")
    94→		}
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	var deps []models.ScannedDependency
    98→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(text), &deps); err != nil {
    99→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse dependencies JSON: %w\nraw: %s", err, text)
   100→	}
   101→	return deps, nil
   102→}
   103→

Note: `\"errors\"` is needed for `errors.Is`.\n\nThen append these methods at the end of the file (after the `CreateSemanticReleaseTodo` method which ends at line 1480):\n\n```go\n\n// --- Onboard Store ---\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) CreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\ttx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"begin tx: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer tx.Rollback(ctx)\n\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr = tx.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO onboard_scans (repo_url) VALUES ($1)\n\t\t RETURNING id, repo_url, status, created_at`,\n\t\trepoURL,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// Enqueue River job in the same transaction\n\tif s.river != nil {\n\t\t_, err = s.river.InsertTx(ctx, tx, queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs{ScanID: scan.ID}, nil)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"enqueue scan job: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) GetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, repo_url, status, results, COALESCE(error, ''), created_at, started_at, completed_at\n\t\t FROM onboard_scans WHERE id = $1`, id,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.Results, &scan.Error,\n\t\t&scan.CreatedAt, &scan.StartedAt, &scan.CompletedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error {\n\t_, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE onboard_scans SET status = $2, results = $3, error = NULLIF($4, ''),\n\t\t started_at = CASE WHEN $2 = 'processing' AND started_at IS NULL THEN NOW() ELSE started_at END,\n\t\t completed_at = CASE WHEN $2 IN ('completed', 'failed') THEN NOW() ELSE completed_at END\n\t\t WHERE id = $1`, id, status, results, scanErr,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ActiveScanForRepo(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error) {\n\tvar scan models.OnboardScan\n\terr := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT id, repo_url, status, created_at FROM onboard_scans\n\t\t WHERE repo_url = $1 AND status IN ('pending', 'processing')\n\t\t ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`, repoURL,\n\t).Scan(&scan.ID, &scan.RepoURL, &scan.Status, &scan.CreatedAt)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &scan, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *PgStore) ApplyOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, scanID string, selections []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error) {\n\tresult := &OnboardApplyResult{\n\t\tCreatedProjects: []models.Project{},\n\t\tCreatedSources:  []models.Source{},\n\t\tSkipped:         []string{},\n\t}\n\n\ttx, err := s.pool.Begin(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"begin tx: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer tx.Rollback(ctx)\n\n\tfor _, sel := range selections {\n\t\tprojectID := sel.ProjectID\n\n\t\t// Create project if needed\n\t\tif sel.NewProjectName != \"\" {\n\t\t\tvar p models.Project\n\t\t\terr := tx.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO projects (name, agent_rules) VALUES ($1, '{}')\n\t\t\t\t RETURNING id, name, description, agent_prompt, agent_rules, created_at, updated_at`,\n\t\t\t\tsel.NewProjectName,\n\t\t\t).Scan(&p.ID, &p.Name, &p.Description, &p.AgentPrompt, &p.AgentRules, &p.CreatedAt, &p.UpdatedAt)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create project %q: %w\", sel.NewProjectName, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tprojectID = p.ID\n\t\t\tresult.CreatedProjects = append(result.CreatedProjects, p)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Create source — skip if duplicate\n\t\tvar src models.Source\n\t\terr := tx.QueryRow(ctx,\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sources (project_id, provider, repository)\n\t\t\t VALUES ($1, $2, $3)\n\t\t\t ON CONFLICT (provider, repository) DO NOTHING\n\t\t\t RETURNING id, project_id, provider, repository, poll_interval_seconds, enabled, created_at, updated_at`,\n\t\t\tprojectID, sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo,\n\t\t).Scan(&src.ID, &src.ProjectID, &src.Provider, &src.Repository,\n\t\t\t&src.PollIntervalSeconds, &src.Enabled, &src.CreatedAt, &src.UpdatedAt)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {\n\t\t\t\tresult.Skipped = append(result.Skipped, fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%s: source already exists\", sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo))\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create source %s/%s: %w\", sel.Provider, sel.UpstreamRepo, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tresult.CreatedSources = append(result.CreatedSources, src)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n```\n\nRun: `go vet ./internal/api/...`\nExpected: no errors\n\nCommit separately: `git commit -m \"feat(onboard): Both tasks are complete. Here is a summary of what was implemented:

---

## Task 6 — `internal/api/onboard.go`

Created `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/onboard.go` containing:

**Types:**
- `OnboardSelection` — represents a user's mapping of a scanned dependency to a project and upstream source.
- `OnboardApplyResult` — collects the newly created projects, sources, and skipped duplicates from an apply operation.
- `OnboardStore` interface — five methods: `CreateOnboardScan`, `GetOnboardScan`, `UpdateOnboardScanStatus`, `ActiveScanForRepo`, `ApplyOnboardScan`.

**Handlers (`OnboardHandler`):**
- `Scan` — parses and normalizes the incoming repo URL via `onboard.ParseRepoURL`, checks for a pre-existing active scan (returning 409 Conflict if found), then creates the scan and returns 201.
- `GetScan` — fetches a scan by `{id}` path value, returns 404 when `pgx.ErrNoRows`.
- `Apply` — validates that each selection has exactly one of `project_id` or `new_project_name` set, then delegates to `store.ApplyOnboardScan`.

Commit: `c026519` — `feat(onboard): add OnboardStore interface and API handlers`

---

## Task 7 — OnboardStore methods in `internal/api/pgstore.go`

Updated `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`:

**Import additions:** `"encoding/json"` and `"errors"` added alphabetically to the existing import block.

**New `PgStore` methods appended after `CreateSemanticReleaseTodo`:**

| Method | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `CreateOnboardScan` | Inserts the scan row, then enqueues a `ScanDependenciesJobArgs` River job in the **same transaction** so the job is only visible after the row commits. |
| `GetOnboardScan` | Full `SELECT` with all nullable timestamp columns and `COALESCE(error, '')`. |
| `UpdateOnboardScanStatus` | Single `UPDATE` using `CASE` expressions to set `started_at` on first transition to `processing` and `completed_at` on `completed`/`failed`. |
| `ActiveScanForRepo` | Returns `nil, nil` (not an error) when no active scan exists — caller in `Scan` handler distinguishes the two cases cleanly. |
| `ApplyOnboardScan` | Runs inside a transaction; creates projects on demand, inserts sources with `ON CONFLICT … DO NOTHING`, collects skips via `pgx.ErrNoRows` on the `RETURNING` clause. |

Commit: `03f273d` — `feat(onboard): implement OnboardStore methods in PgStore`

     1→package main
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"log/slog"
     6→	"net/http"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"os/signal"
     9→	"time"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	agentpkg "github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/agent"
    13→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/api"
    14→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/db"
    15→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/ingestion"
    16→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    17→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/routing"
    18→)
    19→
    20→func main() {
    21→	// Configure log level from LOG_LEVEL env (debug, info, warn, error).
    22→	logLevel := new(slog.LevelVar) // defaults to Info
    23→	switch os.Getenv("LOG_LEVEL") {
    24→	case "debug":
    25→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelDebug)
    26→	case "warn":
    27→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelWarn)
    28→	case "error":
    29→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelError)
    30→	}
    31→	slog.SetDefault(slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(os.Stderr, &slog.HandlerOptions{Level: logLevel})))
    32→
    33→	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt)
    34→	defer stop()
    35→
    36→	dbURL := envOr("DATABASE_URL", "postgres://localhost:5432/changelogue?sslmode=disable")
    37→	addr := envOr("LISTEN_ADDR", ":8080")
    38→	noAuth := os.Getenv("NO_AUTH") == "true"
    39→
    40→	// Database
    41→	pool, err := db.NewPool(ctx, dbURL)
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		slog.Error("database connection failed", "err", err)
    44→		os.Exit(1)
    45→	}
    46→	defer pool.Close()
    47→
    48→	if err := db.RunMigrations(ctx, pool); err != nil {
    49→		slog.Error("migrations failed", "err", err)
    50→		os.Exit(1)
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	// API & routing store (created before River so we can register workers)
    54→	// The river client is set to nil initially; TriggerAgentRun (which needs it)
    55→	// won't be called until the server is up and the client is set below.
    56→	pgStore := api.NewPgStore(pool, nil)
    57→
    58→	// Register River workers
    59→	workers := river.NewWorkers()
    60→	notifyWorker := routing.NewNotifyWorker(pgStore, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
    61→	river.AddWorker(workers, notifyWorker)
    62→
    63→	// Agent worker: requires an LLM API key. If unavailable, agent jobs
    64→	// will remain in the queue until the key is configured and the server
    65→	// is restarted.
    66→	llmProvider := envOr("LLM_PROVIDER", "gemini")
    67→	llmModelDefault := "gemini-2.5-flash"
    68→	if llmProvider == "openai" {
    69→		llmModelDefault = "gpt-5.2"
    70→	}
    71→	llmConfig := agentpkg.LLMConfig{
    72→		Provider:      llmProvider,
    73→		Model:         envOr("LLM_MODEL", llmModelDefault),
    74→		GoogleAPIKey:  os.Getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
    75→		OpenAIAPIKey:  os.Getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
    76→		OpenAIBaseURL: os.Getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL"),
    77→	}
    78→	agentOrchestrator, err := agentpkg.NewOrchestrator(pgStore, llmConfig, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
    79→	if err != nil {
    80→		slog.Warn("agent orchestrator not available — agent jobs will not be processed", "err", err)
    81→	} else {
    82→		agentWorker := agentpkg.NewAgentWorker(agentOrchestrator, pgStore)
    83→		river.AddWorker(workers, agentWorker)
    84→		slog.Info("agent worker registered")
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	riverClient, err := queue.NewRiverClient(pool, workers)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		slog.Error("river client failed", "err", err)
    90→		os.Exit(1)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// Now that the river client exists, set it on the existing pgStore so that
    94→	// all holders (including NotifyWorker) can enqueue agent jobs.
    95→	pgStore.SetRiverClient(riverClient)
    96→
    97→	if err := riverClient.Start(ctx); err != nil {
    98→		slog.Error("river start failed", "err", err)
    99→		os.Exit(1)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Ingestion layer
   103→	ingestionStore := ingestion.NewPgStore(pool, riverClient)
   104→	svc := ingestion.NewService(ingestionStore)
   105→
   106→	loader := ingestion.NewSourceLoader(pool, http.DefaultClient)
   107→	orch := ingestion.NewOrchestrator(svc, loader, pool, 5*time.Minute)
   108→
   109→	broadcaster := api.NewBroadcaster()
   110→
   111→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
   112→
   113→	// Serve frontend static export if available
   114→	if info, err := os.Stat("web/out"); err == nil && info.IsDir() {
   115→		api.RegisterFrontend(mux, "web/out")
   116→		slog.Info("serving frontend from web/out")
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Register all API v1 routes
   120→	api.RegisterRoutes(mux, api.Dependencies{
   121→		DB:                    pool,
   122→		ProjectsStore:         pgStore,
   123→		ReleasesStore:         pgStore,
   124→		SubscriptionsStore:    pgStore,
   125→		SourcesStore:          pgStore,
   126→		ChannelsStore:         pgStore,
   127→		ContextSourcesStore:   pgStore,
   128→		SemanticReleasesStore: pgStore,
   129→		AgentStore:            pgStore,
   130→		TodosStore:            pgStore,
   131→		PublicURL:             os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"),
   132→		KeyStore:              pgStore,
   133→		HealthChecker:         pgStore,
   134→		Broadcaster:           broadcaster,
   135→		NoAuth:                noAuth,
   136→		IngestionService:      svc,
   137→		HTTPClient:            http.DefaultClient,
   138→	})
   139→
   140→	srv := &http.Server{Addr: addr, Handler: api.CORS(mux)}
   141→
   142→	// Start polling in background
   143→	go orch.Run(ctx)
   144→
   145→	// Start PostgreSQL LISTEN/NOTIFY → SSE bridge
   146→	go api.ListenForNotifications(ctx, pool, broadcaster)
   147→
   148→	// Start HTTP server
   149→	go func() {
   150→		slog.Info("server starting", "addr", srv.Addr)
   151→		if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != http.ErrServerClosed {
   152→			slog.Error("server error", "err", err)
   153→		}
   154→	}()
   155→
   156→	<-ctx.Done()
   157→	slog.Info("shutting down")
   158→
   159→	shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   160→	defer cancel()
   161→	riverClient.Stop(shutdownCtx)
   162→	srv.Shutdown(shutdownCtx)
   163→}
   164→
   165→func envOr(key, fallback string) string {
   166→	if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
   167→		return v
   168→	}
   169→	return fallback
   170→}

Go module: `github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue`.\n\n## Task 8: Create the scan worker\n\nCreate `internal/onboard/worker.go`:\n\n```go\npackage onboard\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log/slog\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool\"\n\t\"github.com/riverqueue/river\"\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue\"\n)\n\n// ScanStore is the data access interface for the scan worker.\ntype ScanStore interface {\n\tGetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)\n\tUpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error\n}\n\n// ScanWorker processes scan_dependencies River jobs.\ntype ScanWorker struct {\n\triver.WorkerDefaults[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]\n\tstore     ScanStore\n\tscanner   *Scanner\n\textractor *DependencyExtractor\n\tpool      *pgxpool.Pool\n}\n\n// NewScanWorker creates a ScanWorker. extractor may be nil if GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set.\nfunc NewScanWorker(store ScanStore, scanner *Scanner, extractor *DependencyExtractor, pool *pgxpool.Pool) *ScanWorker {\n\treturn &ScanWorker{\n\t\tstore:     store,\n\t\tscanner:   scanner,\n\t\textractor: extractor,\n\t\tpool:      pool,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (w *ScanWorker) Timeout(_ *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) time.Duration {\n\treturn 3 * time.Minute\n}\n\nfunc (w *ScanWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) error {\n\tscanID := job.Args.ScanID\n\tslog.Info(\"scan worker picked up job\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"attempt\", job.Attempt)\n\n\t// Load scan\n\tscan, err := w.store.GetOnboardScan(ctx, scanID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"load scan %s: %w\", scanID, err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Mark processing\n\tif err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"processing\", nil, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update status to processing: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse repo URL\n\towner, repo, err := ParseRepoURL(scan.RepoURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse repo URL: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Fetch dependency files\n\tfiles, err := w.scanner.FetchDependencyFiles(ctx, owner, repo)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"fetch dependency files: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"found dependency files\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(files))\n\n\tif len(files) == 0 {\n\t\t// No dependency files — store empty results\n\t\temptyResults, _ := json.Marshal([]models.ScannedDependency{})\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", emptyResults, \"\")\n\t\tw.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract dependencies via LLM\n\tif w.extractor == nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, \"LLM not configured (set GOOGLE_API_KEY)\")\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extractor not configured\")\n\t}\n\n\tdeps, err := w.extractor.Extract(ctx, files)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"failed\", nil, err.Error())\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"extract dependencies: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tslog.Info(\"extracted dependencies\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"count\", len(deps))\n\n\tresults, err := json.Marshal(deps)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"marshal results: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, \"completed\", results, \"\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update scan completed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tw.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// notifyScanComplete sends a pg_notify on the release_events channel.\nfunc (w *ScanWorker) notifyScanComplete(ctx context.Context, scanID string) {\n\tpayload := fmt.Sprintf(`{\"type\":\"scan_complete\",\"id\":\"%s\"}`, scanID)\n\t_, err := w.pool.Exec(ctx, \"SELECT pg_notify('release_events', $1)\", payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tslog.Error(\"pg_notify failed\", \"scan_id\", scanID, \"err\", err)\n\t}\n}\n```\n\nRun: `go vet ./internal/onboard/...`\nCommit: `git commit -m \"feat(onboard): implement River scan worker\"`\n\n## Task 9: Register worker in main.go and add routes in server.go\n\n### Step 1: Add OnboardStore to Dependencies struct in server.go\n\nIn `internal/api/server.go`, add to the `Dependencies` struct. The struct currently has these fields (in order): DB, ProjectsStore, ReleasesStore, SubscriptionsStore, SourcesStore, ChannelsStore, ContextSourcesStore, SemanticReleasesStore, AgentStore, TodosStore, PublicURL, KeyStore, HealthChecker, Broadcaster, NoAuth, IngestionService, HTTPClient.\n\nAdd after `TodosStore`:\n```go\nOnboardStore          OnboardStore\n```\n\n### Step 2: Register onboard routes in server.go\n\nIn `internal/api/server.go`, inside `RegisterRoutes`, add after the Todos section (after line 117, the `GET /api/v1/todos/{id}/resolve` line):\n\n```go\n\t// Onboard (repo dependency scanning)\n\tob := NewOnboardHandler(deps.OnboardStore)\n\tmux.Handle(\"POST /api/v1/onboard/scan\", chain(http.HandlerFunc(ob.Scan)))\n\tmux.Handle(\"GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}\", chain(http.HandlerFunc(ob.GetScan)))\n\tmux.Handle(\"POST /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}/apply\", chain(http.HandlerFunc(ob.Apply)))\n```\n\n### Step 3: Register scan worker and add OnboardStore in main.go\n\nIn `cmd/server/main.go`:\n\n1. Add the onboard import to the import block:\n```go\n\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/onboard\"\n```\n\n2. Both tasks are complete and verified. Here is a summary of what was done:

---

## Summary

### Task 8: Create the scan worker

**File created:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/onboard/worker.go`

The `ScanWorker` struct implements `river.Worker[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]` and orchestrates the full scan pipeline:

1. Loads the `OnboardScan` record by ID from the store
2. Marks the scan as `processing`
3. Parses the repo URL into `owner/repo` using `ParseRepoURL`
4. Fetches dependency files via `Scanner.FetchDependencyFiles`
5. If no files: stores empty results and marks `completed`
6. If files present: calls `DependencyExtractor.Extract` (Gemini LLM) and stores the parsed `[]models.ScannedDependency` as JSON
7. Sends a `pg_notify` on `release_events` channel with `{"type":"scan_complete","id":"..."}` to trigger SSE fanout

The worker has a 3-minute timeout and handles extractor-not-configured gracefully (emits `failed` status).

**Commit:** `feat(onboard): implement River scan worker`

---

### Task 9: Register worker in main.go and add routes in server.go

**File modified:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/server.go`

- Added `OnboardStore OnboardStore` field to `Dependencies` struct (after `TodosStore`)
- Added 3 onboard routes after the Todos section:
  - `POST /api/v1/onboard/scan`
  - `GET /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}`
  - `POST /api/v1/onboard/scans/{id}/apply`

**File modified:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/cmd/server/main.go`

- Added `"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/onboard"` import
- Registered `ScanWorker` into the River workers pool (after the agent worker block), reusing the existing `llmConfig.GoogleAPIKey` and `llmConfig.Model` for the `DependencyExtractor`
- Added `OnboardStore: pgStore` to the `api.Dependencies{}` block

`go vet ./...` passes with zero errors on the full project.

**Commit:** `feat(onboard): register scan worker and API routes`

    57→            </button>
    58→          </>
    59→        ) : (
    60→          <button
    61→            onClick={() => setExpanded(true)}
    62→            className="mx-auto text-[#9ca3af] transition-colors hover:text-white"
    63→            title="Expand sidebar"

    66→          </button>

    68→      </div>

skipped: string[] } | null>(null);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Fetch existing projects for the dropdown\n  const { data: projectsResp } = useSWR(\"projects-all\", () => projectsApi.list(1, 200));\n  const existingProjects = projectsResp?.data ?? [];\n\n  // Poll scan status\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (step !== \"scanning\" || !scanId) return;\n    const interval = setInterval(async () => {\n      try {\n        const resp = await onboardApi.getScan(scanId);\n        setScan(resp.data);\n        if (resp.data.status === \"completed\") {\n          setStep(\"results\");\n          // Select all by default\n          const deps = resp.data.results ?? [];\n          const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};\n          const assign: Record<number, { mode: \"new\" | \"existing\"; newName?: string }> = {};\n          deps.forEach((d, i) => {\n            sel[i] = true;\n            assign[i] = { mode: \"new\", newName: d.name.replace(/\\//g, \"-\") };\n          });\n          setSelections(sel);\n          setProjectAssignments(assign);\n          clearInterval(interval);\n        } else if (resp.data.status === \"failed\") {\n          setError(resp.data.error || \"Scan failed\");\n          setStep(\"input\");\n          clearInterval(interval);\n        }\n      } catch {\n        // Keep polling\n      }\n    }, 2000);\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, [step, scanId]);\n\n  const startScan = async () => {\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repoUrl);\n      setScanId(resp.data.id);\n      setScan(resp.data);\n      setStep(\"scanning\");\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      setError(e.message || \"Failed to start scan\");\n    }\n  };\n\n  const applySelections = async () => {\n    if (!scan?.results || !scanId) return;\n    setApplying(true);\n    setError(null);\n\n    const sels: OnboardSelection[] = [];\n    scan.results.forEach((dep, i) => {\n      if (!selections[i]) return;\n      const assign = projectAssignments[i];\n      sels.push({\n        dep_name: dep.name,\n        upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,\n        provider: dep.provider,\n        project_id: assign?.mode === \"existing\" ? assign.projectId : undefined,\n        new_project_name: assign?.mode === \"new\" ? (assign.newName || dep.name.replace(/\\//g, \"-\")) : undefined,\n      });\n    });\n\n    try {\n      const resp = await onboardApi.apply(scanId, sels);\n      setApplyResult(resp.data);\n      setStep(\"applied\");\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      setError(e.message || \"Failed to apply selections\");\n    } finally {\n      setApplying(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const deps = scan?.results ?? [];\n  const selectedCount = Object.values(selections).filter(Boolean).length;\n\n  const ecosystemColors: Record<string, string> = {\n    go: \"bg-cyan-900/50 text-cyan-300\",\n    npm: \"bg-red-900/50 text-red-300\",\n    pypi: \"bg-yellow-900/50 text-yellow-300\",\n    cargo: \"bg-orange-900/50 text-orange-300\",\n    rubygems: \"bg-pink-900/50 text-pink-300\",\n    maven: \"bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300\",\n    docker: \"bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300\",\n    other: \"bg-gray-700/50 text-gray-300\",\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"flex flex-1 flex-col p-6\">\n      <div className=\"mb-6\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-xl font-semibold text-white\">Quick Onboard</h1>\n        <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-[#9ca3af]\">\n          Scan a GitHub repository to detect dependencies and start tracking their releases.\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      {error && (\n        <div className=\"mb-4 rounded-md bg-red-900/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300 border border-red-800/50\">\n          {error}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Step 1: Input */}\n      {step === \"input\" && (\n        <div className=\"max-w-xl\">\n          <label className=\"block text-sm font-medium text-[#d1d5db] mb-2\">\n            GitHub Repository URL\n          </label>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              value={repoUrl}\n              onChange={(e) => setRepoUrl(e.target.value)}\n              placeholder=\"owner/repo or https://github.com/owner/repo\"\n              className=\"flex-1 rounded-md border border-[#374151] bg-[#1f2937] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white placeholder-[#6b7280] focus:border-[#e8601a] focus:outline-none\"\n              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === \"Enter\" && repoUrl.trim() && startScan()}\n            />\n            <button\n              onClick={startScan}\n              disabled={!repoUrl.trim()}\n              className=\"flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#e8601a] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#d4560f] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed\"\n            >\n              <Search className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n              Scan\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Step 2: Scanning */}\n      {step === \"scanning\" && (\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20\">\n          <Loader2 className=\"h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-[#e8601a]\" />\n          <p className=\"mt-4 text-sm text-[#9ca3af]\">\n            Scanning repository for dependencies...\n          </p>\n          <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-[#6b7280]\">\n            This may take a moment while we analyze dependency files.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Step 3: Results */}\n      {step === \"results\" && deps.length === 0 && (\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center\">\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-[#9ca3af]\">No dependencies detected in this repository.</p>\n          <button\n            onClick={() => { setStep(\"input\"); setRepoUrl(\"\"); }}\n            className=\"mt-4 text-sm text-[#e8601a] hover:underline\"\n          >\n            Try another repository\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {step === \"results\" && deps.length > 0 && (\n        <div>\n          <div className=\"mb-4 flex items-center justify-between\">\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-[#9ca3af]\">\n              Found <span className=\"text-white font-medium\">{deps.length}</span> dependencies.\n              Selected: <span className=\"text-white font-medium\">{selectedCount}</span>\n            </p>\n            <button\n              onClick={applySelections}\n              disabled={selectedCount === 0 || applying}\n              className=\"flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#e8601a] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#d4560f] disabled:opacity-50\"\n            >\n              {applying ? <Loader2 className=\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin\" /> : <Rocket className=\"h-4 w-4\" />}\n              Track Selected ({selectedCount})\n            </button>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[#374151]\">\n            <table className=\"w-full text-sm\">\n              <thead>\n                <tr className=\"border-b border-[#374151] bg-[#111827]\">\n                  <th className=\"w-10 px-3 py-2\">\n                    <input\n                      type=\"checkbox\"\n                      checked={selectedCount === deps.length}\n                      onChange={(e) => {\n                        const val = e.target.checked;\n                        const s: Record<number, boolean> = {};\n                        deps.forEach((_, i) => { s[i] = val; });\n                        setSelections(s);\n                      }}\n                      className=\"rounded border-[#374151]\"\n                    />\n                  </th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]\">Dependency</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]\">Version</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]\">Ecosystem</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]\">Source</th>\n                  <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]\">Project</th>\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody>\n                {deps.map((dep, i) => (\n                  <tr key={i} className=\"border-b border-[#374151] hover:bg-[#1f2937]/50\">\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2\">\n                      <input\n                        type=\"checkbox\"\n                        checked={!!selections[i]}\n                        onChange={(e) => setSelections({ ...selections, [i]: e.target.checked })}\n                        className=\"rounded border-[#374151]\"\n                      />\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-white font-mono text-xs\">{dep.name}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-[#9ca3af] font-mono text-xs\">{dep.version}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2\">\n                      <span className={`inline-block rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${ecosystemColors[dep.ecosystem] || ecosystemColors.other}`}>\n                        {dep.ecosystem}\n                      </span>\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2 text-[#9ca3af] text-xs\">{dep.upstream_repo}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-2\">\n                      <select\n                        value={projectAssignments[i]?.mode === \"existing\" ? projectAssignments[i]?.projectId : \"__new__\"}\n                        onChange={(e) => {\n                          const val = e.target.value;\n                          if (val === \"__new__\") {\n                            setProjectAssignments({\n                              ...projectAssignments,\n                              [i]: { mode: \"new\", newName: dep.name.replace(/\\//g, \"-\") },\n                            });\n                          } else {\n                            setProjectAssignments({\n                              ...projectAssignments,\n                              [i]: { mode: \"existing\", projectId: val },\n                            });\n                          }\n                        }}\n                        className=\"rounded border border-[#374151] bg-[#1f2937] px-2 py-1 text-xs text-white\"\n                      >\n                        <option value=\"__new__\">Create new project</option>\n                        {existingProjects.map((p) => (\n                          <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>\n                        ))}\n                      </select>\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Step 4: Applied */}\n      {step === \"applied\" && applyResult && (\n        <div>\n          <div className=\"mb-6 rounded-md bg-green-900/30 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-300 border border-green-800/50\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              <Check className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n              Successfully created {applyResult.created_sources.length} key={i}>{s}</li>)}\n              </ul>\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n            <button\n              onClick={() => router.push(\"/projects\")}\n <ExternalLink className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n            </button>\n            <button\n              onClick={() => { setStep(\"input\"); setRepoUrl(\"\"); setScanId(null); setScan(null); ## Summary

### Task 10 — TypeScript Types & API Client

**File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`**

Appended four new interfaces after the `SSEEvent` type:
- `OnboardScan` — represents a scan job with status, results, and timestamps
- `ScannedDependency` — a single dependency detected in a repo (name, version, ecosystem, upstream_repo, provider)
- `OnboardSelection` — a user's selection to track, with optional project assignment
- `OnboardApplyResult` — the result of applying selections (created projects, sources, and skipped items)

**File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`**

- Added `OnboardScan`, `OnboardSelection`, `OnboardApplyResult` to the type import block
- Appended the `onboard` API module with three methods: `scan()`, `getScan()`, and `apply()`

### Task 11 — Sidebar Entry

**File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx`**

- Added `Rocket` to the lucide-react import list
- Added `{ href: "/onboard", label: "Quick Onboard", icon: Rocket }` as the second item in `navItems`, after Dashboard and before Projects

### Task 12 — Onboarding Page

**File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/onboard/page.tsx`** (new file, 315 lines)

A 4-step client-side page:
1. **Input** — Text field to enter a GitHub repo URL, with a Scan button
2. **Scanning** — Animated spinner while polling the scan status every 2 seconds via `onboardApi.getScan()`
3. **Results** — Table of detected dependencies with checkboxes, ecosystem badges, and a per-row project assignment dropdown (create new vs. assign to existing project fetched via SWR)
4. **Applied** — Success/warning banners showing created sources/projects and any skipped items, with navigation to Projects or scan-another flow

Each task was committed separately with the `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>` trailer.
</output>

## GitHub Repo Onboarding — Implementation Complete

**14 commits** on `feat/github-repo-onboarding` branch, all tests passing, server builds cleanly.

### What was built

**Backend (Go):**
- `internal/models/onboard.go` — `OnboardScan` and `ScannedDependency` models
- `internal/db/migrations.go` — `onboard_scans` table migration
- `internal/queue/jobs.go` — `ScanDependenciesJobArgs` for River
- `internal/onboard/scanner.go` — GitHub API scanner (repo tree + file contents)
- `internal/onboard/gemini.go` — Gemini LLM dependency extractor
- `internal/onboard/worker.go` — River job worker (scan → extract → store → pg_notify)
- `internal/api/onboard.go` — API handlers (scan, get scan, apply) + `OnboardStore` interface
- `internal/api/pgstore.go` — OnboardStore implementation (transactional scan+job creation, apply with rollback)
- `internal/api/server.go` — 3 new routes registered
- `cmd/server/main.go` — Scan worker wired up with LLM config

**Frontend (Next.js):**
- `web/lib/api/types.ts` — 4 new TypeScript interfaces
- `web/lib/api/client.ts` — `onboard` API module (scan, getScan, apply)
- `web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx` — "Quick Onboard" with Rocket icon
- `web/app/onboard/page.tsx` — 4-step onboarding flow (input → scanning → results → applied)

**Tests:**
- `internal/onboard/scanner_test.go` — URL parsing + httptest mock GitHub API
- `internal/onboard/gemini_test.go` — JSON parsing + markdown fence stripping
- `internal/onboard/worker_test.go` — Mock store integration
- `internal/api/onboard_test.go` — Handler tests (201, 422, 409, 400 validation)

> AGENT

The issue is on line 1531-1535. PostgreSQL can't infer the type for `$2` when it's used both in `SET status = $2` (text context) and in `CASE WHEN $2 = 'processing'` / `CASE WHEN $2 IN ('completed', 'failed')` (comparison context). The fix is to cast `$2` explicitly.

[feat/github-repo-onboarding f7e95ba] fix(onboard): cast $2 to text in UpdateOnboardScanStatus to fix PG type inference
 1 file changed, 3 insertions(+), 3 deletions(-)
```

> AGENT

Fixed. The issue was that PostgreSQL's parameter type inference failed when `$2` was used in multiple contexts — `SET status = $2` (assignment) and `CASE WHEN $2 = 'processing'` / `$2 IN ('completed', 'failed')` (comparison). Adding explicit `$2::text` casts resolves the ambiguity.

The scan worker should now process jobs successfully on the next retry attempt.

Perfect! Now I have all the information you need. Here's a comprehensive report on how OpenAI is integrated in the codebase:

## OpenAI Integration Report

### 1. LLMConfig Structure (`internal/agent/model.go`)

```go
type LLMConfig struct {
	Provider string // "gemini" or "openai"
	Model    string // e.g. "gemini-2.5-flash", "gpt-5.2"

	// Gemini
	GoogleAPIKey string

	// OpenAI
	OpenAIAPIKey  string
	OpenAIBaseURL string // defaults to https://api.openai.com/v1
}
```

The `LLMConfig` struct holds all provider-agnostic configuration with provider-specific API key and base URL fields.

### 2. How OpenAI Client is Created (`internal/agent/model.go`)

The `NewLLMModel()` function handles instantiation:

```go
case "openai":
	return oaimodel.NewModel(ctx, cfg.Model, oaimodel.Config{
		APIKey:  cfg.OpenAIAPIKey,
		BaseURL: cfg.OpenAIBaseURL,
	}, wsConfig)
```

This delegates to the OpenAI package which creates an HTTP-based model implementation.

### 3. OpenAI SDK/Package Used (`internal/agent/openai/openai.go`)

**The codebase does NOT use an external OpenAI SDK.** Instead:
- Uses the standard library **`net/http`** for API calls
- Uses **Google ADK types** (`google.golang.org/adk/model`) for interface compatibility
- Uses **Google genai types** (`google.golang.org/genai`) for LLMResponse conversion
- Implements OpenAI's **Responses API** format directly via custom JSON marshaling

The OpenAI model is a custom implementation (`openaiModel`) that:
- Converts ADK request types to OpenAI Responses API format
- Makes raw HTTP POST requests to the configured base URL
- Converts responses back to ADK LLMResponse format

### 4. Base URL and API Key Configuration

**Environment variables** (`cmd/server/main.go`, lines 67-78):
```go
llmProvider := envOr("LLM_PROVIDER", "gemini")
llmConfig := agentpkg.LLMConfig{
	Provider:      llmProvider,
	Model:         envOr("LLM_MODEL", llmModelDefault),
	GoogleAPIKey:  os.Getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
	OpenAIAPIKey:  os.Getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
	OpenAIBaseURL: os.Getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL"),  // defaults to https://api.openai.com/v1
}
```

**Key details:**
- `OPENAI_API_KEY` - Required for OpenAI provider
- `OPENAI_BASE_URL` - Optional; defaults to `https://api.openai.com/v1` if not set (line 41-42 in openai.go)
- Bearer token header: `Authorization: Bearer {API_KEY}` (line 188 in openai.go)

### 5. HTTP Request Implementation (`internal/agent/openai/openai.go`)

The client makes POST requests to `{baseURL}/responses` endpoint:

```go
httpReq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, 
	m.baseURL+"/responses", bytes.NewReader(body))
httpReq.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
httpReq.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.apiKey)
```

### Key Takeaway for Your Task

For `internal/onboard/gemini.go` dependency extractor, you'll need to:
1. **Accept OpenAI config** through the LLM layer (similar to how the agent uses `NewLLMModel()`)
2. **Replace the Gemini-specific code** that directly calls `genai.Client.Models.GenerateContent()`
3. **Use the abstracted LLM interface** (`model.LLM`) instead of directly calling Gemini, OR
4. **Create an OpenAI-specific DependencyExtractor** that uses the same HTTP-based approach as `internal/agent/openai/openai.go`

The OpenAI integration uses no external SDK — pure HTTP with custom JSON marshaling, so you have full control over the request/response format.

     1→package onboard
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"strings"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    10→	"google.golang.org/genai"
    11→)
    12→
    13→const extractionSystemPrompt = `You are a dependency extraction agent. Given the contents of dependency/manifest
    14→files from a software project, extract all dependencies.
    15→
    16→For each dependency, return:
    17→- name: the package/library name
    18→- version: the version constraint or pinned version
    19→- ecosystem: one of "go", "npm", "pypi", "cargo", "rubygems", "maven", "gradle", "docker", "other"
    20→- upstream_repo: your best guess at the canonical GitHub repository URL (e.g., "github.com/gorilla/mux")
    21→- provider: the Changelogue provider to use for release tracking. Use "github" for repos
    22→  with GitHub releases, "dockerhub" for Docker images, "gitlab" for GitLab repos,
    23→  "ecr_public" for AWS ECR images. When unsure, default to "github".
    24→
    25→Return ONLY a JSON array. No explanations.`
    26→
    27→// DependencyExtractor uses Gemini to extract dependencies from file contents.
    28→type DependencyExtractor struct {
    29→	client *genai.Client
    30→	model  string
    31→}
    32→
    33→// NewDependencyExtractor creates a DependencyExtractor.
    34→func NewDependencyExtractor(ctx context.Context, apiKey, model string) (*DependencyExtractor, error) {
    35→	client, err := genai.NewClient(ctx, &genai.ClientConfig{
    36→		APIKey:  apiKey,
    37→		Backend: genai.BackendGeminiAPI,
    38→	})
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create genai client: %w", err)
    41→	}
    42→	if model == "" {
    43→		model = "gemini-2.0-flash"
    44→	}
    45→	return &DependencyExtractor{client: client, model: model}, nil
    46→}
    47→
    48→// Extract sends file contents to Gemini and returns parsed dependencies.
    49→func (e *DependencyExtractor) Extract(ctx context.Context, files []DependencyFile) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {
    50→	prompt := BuildExtractionPrompt(files)
    51→
    52→	resp, err := e.client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, e.model, []*genai.Content{genai.NewContentFromText(prompt, "user")}, &genai.GenerateContentConfig{
    53→		SystemInstruction: genai.NewContentFromText(extractionSystemPrompt, "user"),
    54→		Temperature:       genai.Ptr(float32(0.1)),
    55→	})
    56→	if err != nil {
    57→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("generate content: %w", err)
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	if len(resp.Candidates) == 0 || len(resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts) == 0 {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("empty response from Gemini")
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	text := resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts[0].Text
    65→	return ParseDependencies([]byte(text))
    66→}
    67→
    68→// BuildExtractionPrompt constructs the user prompt from dependency files.
    69→func BuildExtractionPrompt(files []DependencyFile) string {
    70→	var b strings.Builder
    71→	b.WriteString("Extract all dependencies from these files:\n\n")
    72→	for _, f := range files {
    73→		b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("--- %s ---\n%s\n\n", f.Path, f.Content))
    74→	}
    75→	return b.String()
    76→}
    77→
    78→// ParseDependencies parses the LLM response into ScannedDependency structs.
    79→// Handles raw JSON arrays and markdown-fenced JSON.
    80→func ParseDependencies(raw []byte) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {
    81→	text := strings.TrimSpace(string(raw))
    82→
    83→	// Strip markdown code fences if present
    84→	if strings.HasPrefix(text, "```") {
    85→		lines := strings.Split(text, "\n")
    86→		if len(lines) > 2 {
    87→			// Remove first and last lines (``` markers)
    88→			lines = lines[1 : len(lines)-1]
    89→			// If last line is also ```, it was already removed
    90→			if len(lines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]) == "```" {
    91→				lines = lines[:len(lines)-1]
    92→			}
    93→			text = strings.Join(lines, "\n")
    94→		}
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	var deps []models.ScannedDependency
    98→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(text), &deps); err != nil {
    99→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse dependencies JSON: %w\nraw: %s", err, text)
   100→	}
   101→	return deps, nil
   102→}
   103→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→
     7→	"google.golang.org/adk/model"
     8→	"google.golang.org/adk/model/gemini"
     9→	"google.golang.org/genai"
    10→
    11→	oaimodel "github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/agent/openai"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// LLMConfig holds the configuration for creating an LLM model.
    15→type LLMConfig struct {
    16→	Provider string // "gemini" or "openai"
    17→	Model    string // e.g. "gemini-2.5-flash", "gpt-5.2"
    18→
    19→	// Gemini
    20→	GoogleAPIKey string
    21→
    22→	// OpenAI
    23→	OpenAIAPIKey  string
    24→	OpenAIBaseURL string // defaults to https://api.openai.com/v1
    25→}
    26→
    27→// NewLLMModel creates a model.LLM based on the configured provider.
    28→// wsConfig is optional and only applies to the OpenAI provider; it enables
    29→// the Responses API built-in web_search tool on the created model.
    30→func NewLLMModel(ctx context.Context, cfg LLMConfig, wsConfig *oaimodel.WebSearch) (model.LLM, error) {
    31→	switch cfg.Provider {
    32→	case "gemini", "":
    33→		if cfg.GoogleAPIKey == "" {
    34→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("GOOGLE_API_KEY is required for gemini provider")
    35→		}
    36→		return gemini.NewModel(ctx, cfg.Model, &genai.ClientConfig{
    37→			APIKey: cfg.GoogleAPIKey,
    38→		})
    39→
    40→	case "openai":
    41→		return oaimodel.NewModel(ctx, cfg.Model, oaimodel.Config{
    42→			APIKey:  cfg.OpenAIAPIKey,
    43→			BaseURL: cfg.OpenAIBaseURL,
    44→		}, wsConfig)
    45→
    46→	default:
    47→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown LLM provider: %q (supported: gemini, openai)", cfg.Provider)
    48→	}
    49→}
    50→

     1→package main
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"log/slog"
     6→	"net/http"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"os/signal"
     9→	"time"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	agentpkg "github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/agent"
    13→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/api"
    14→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/db"
    15→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/ingestion"
    16→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/onboard"
    17→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    18→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/routing"
    19→)
    20→
    21→func main() {
    22→	// Configure log level from LOG_LEVEL env (debug, info, warn, error).
    23→	logLevel := new(slog.LevelVar) // defaults to Info
    24→	switch os.Getenv("LOG_LEVEL") {
    25→	case "debug":
    26→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelDebug)
    27→	case "warn":
    28→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelWarn)
    29→	case "error":
    30→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelError)
    31→	}
    32→	slog.SetDefault(slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(os.Stderr, &slog.HandlerOptions{Level: logLevel})))
    33→
    34→	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt)
    35→	defer stop()
    36→
    37→	dbURL := envOr("DATABASE_URL", "postgres://localhost:5432/changelogue?sslmode=disable")
    38→	addr := envOr("LISTEN_ADDR", ":8080")
    39→	noAuth := os.Getenv("NO_AUTH") == "true"
    40→
    41→	// Database
    42→	pool, err := db.NewPool(ctx, dbURL)
    43→	if err != nil {
    44→		slog.Error("database connection failed", "err", err)
    45→		os.Exit(1)
    46→	}
    47→	defer pool.Close()
    48→
    49→	if err := db.RunMigrations(ctx, pool); err != nil {
    50→		slog.Error("migrations failed", "err", err)
    51→		os.Exit(1)
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	// API & routing store (created before River so we can register workers)
    55→	// The river client is set to nil initially; TriggerAgentRun (which needs it)
    56→	// won't be called until the server is up and the client is set below.
    57→	pgStore := api.NewPgStore(pool, nil)
    58→
    59→	// Register River workers
    60→	workers := river.NewWorkers()
    61→	notifyWorker := routing.NewNotifyWorker(pgStore, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
    62→	river.AddWorker(workers, notifyWorker)
    63→
    64→	// Agent worker: requires an LLM API key. If unavailable, agent jobs
    65→	// will remain in the queue until the key is configured and the server
    66→	// is restarted.
    67→	llmProvider := envOr("LLM_PROVIDER", "gemini")
    68→	llmModelDefault := "gemini-2.5-flash"
    69→	if llmProvider == "openai" {
    70→		llmModelDefault = "gpt-5.2"
    71→	}
    72→	llmConfig := agentpkg.LLMConfig{
    73→		Provider:      llmProvider,
    74→		Model:         envOr("LLM_MODEL", llmModelDefault),
    75→		GoogleAPIKey:  os.Getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
    76→		OpenAIAPIKey:  os.Getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
    77→		OpenAIBaseURL: os.Getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL"),
    78→	}
    79→	agentOrchestrator, err := agentpkg.NewOrchestrator(pgStore, llmConfig, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		slog.Warn("agent orchestrator not available — agent jobs will not be processed", "err", err)
    82→	} else {
    83→		agentWorker := agentpkg.NewAgentWorker(agentOrchestrator, pgStore)
    84→		river.AddWorker(workers, agentWorker)
    85→		slog.Info("agent worker registered")
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	// Scan worker: requires LLM for dependency extraction
    89→	scanScanner := onboard.NewScanner(http.DefaultClient, "", "")
    90→	var scanExtractor *onboard.DependencyExtractor
    91→	if llmConfig.GoogleAPIKey != "" {
    92→		ext, err := onboard.NewDependencyExtractor(ctx, llmConfig.GoogleAPIKey, llmConfig.Model)
    93→		if err != nil {
    94→			slog.Warn("dependency extractor not available", "err", err)
    95→		} else {
    96→			scanExtractor = ext
    97→		}
    98→	}
    99→	scanWorker := onboard.NewScanWorker(pgStore, scanScanner, scanExtractor, pool)
   100→	river.AddWorker(workers, scanWorker)
   101→	slog.Info("scan worker registered")
   102→
   103→	riverClient, err := queue.NewRiverClient(pool, workers)
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		slog.Error("river client failed", "err", err)
   106→		os.Exit(1)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	// Now that the river client exists, set it on the existing pgStore so that
   110→	// all holders (including NotifyWorker) can enqueue agent jobs.
   111→	pgStore.SetRiverClient(riverClient)
   112→
   113→	if err := riverClient.Start(ctx); err != nil {
   114→		slog.Error("river start failed", "err", err)
   115→		os.Exit(1)
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	// Ingestion layer
   119→	ingestionStore := ingestion.NewPgStore(pool, riverClient)
   120→	svc := ingestion.NewService(ingestionStore)
   121→
   122→	loader := ingestion.NewSourceLoader(pool, http.DefaultClient)
   123→	orch := ingestion.NewOrchestrator(svc, loader, pool, 5*time.Minute)
   124→
   125→	broadcaster := api.NewBroadcaster()
   126→
   127→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
   128→
   129→	// Serve frontend static export if available
   130→	if info, err := os.Stat("web/out"); err == nil && info.IsDir() {
   131→		api.RegisterFrontend(mux, "web/out")
   132→		slog.Info("serving frontend from web/out")
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	// Register all API v1 routes
   136→	api.RegisterRoutes(mux, api.Dependencies{
   137→		DB:                    pool,
   138→		ProjectsStore:         pgStore,
   139→		ReleasesStore:         pgStore,
   140→		SubscriptionsStore:    pgStore,
   141→		SourcesStore:          pgStore,
   142→		ChannelsStore:         pgStore,
   143→		ContextSourcesStore:   pgStore,
   144→		SemanticReleasesStore: pgStore,
   145→		AgentStore:            pgStore,
   146→		TodosStore:            pgStore,
   147→		OnboardStore:          pgStore,
   148→		PublicURL:             os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"),
   149→		KeyStore:              pgStore,
   150→		HealthChecker:         pgStore,
   151→		Broadcaster:           broadcaster,
   152→		NoAuth:                noAuth,
   153→		IngestionService:      svc,
   154→		HTTPClient:            http.DefaultClient,
   155→	})
   156→
   157→	srv := &http.Server{Addr: addr, Handler: api.CORS(mux)}
   158→
   159→	// Start polling in background
   160→	go orch.Run(ctx)
   161→
   162→	// Start PostgreSQL LISTEN/NOTIFY → SSE bridge
   163→	go api.ListenForNotifications(ctx, pool, broadcaster)
   164→
   165→	// Start HTTP server
   166→	go func() {
   167→		slog.Info("server starting", "addr", srv.Addr)
   168→		if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != http.ErrServerClosed {
   169→			slog.Error("server error", "err", err)
   170→		}
   171→	}()
   172→
   173→	<-ctx.Done()
   174→	slog.Info("shutting down")
   175→
   176→	shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   177→	defer cancel()
   178→	riverClient.Stop(shutdownCtx)
   179→	srv.Shutdown(shutdownCtx)
   180→}
   181→
   182→func envOr(key, fallback string) string {
   183→	if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
   184→		return v
   185→	}
   186→	return fallback
   187→}
   188→
