    11→	"strings"
    12→
    13→	"google.golang.org/adk/model"
    14→	"google.golang.org/genai"
    15→)
    16→
    17→const defaultBaseURL = "https://api.openai.com/v1"
    18→
    19→// Config holds connection parameters for an OpenAI-compatible API.
    20→type Config struct {
    21→	APIKey  string
    22→	BaseURL string // defaults to https://api.openai.com/v1
    23→}
    24→
    25→// openaiModel implements model.LLM for OpenAI-compatible Responses APIs.
    26→type openaiModel struct {
    27→	name     string
    28→	apiKey   string
    29→	baseURL  string
    30→	client   *http.Client
    31→	wsConfig *WebSearch
    32→}
    33→
    34→// NewModel creates an OpenAI-backed model.LLM. wsConfig may be nil if web
    35→// search is not needed.
    36→func NewModel(_ context.Context, modelName string, cfg Config, wsConfig *WebSearch) (model.LLM, error) {
    37→	if cfg.APIKey == "" {
    38→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: API key is required")
    39→	}
    40→	baseURL := cfg.BaseURL
    41→	if baseURL == "" {
    42→		baseURL = defaultBaseURL
    43→	}
    44→	return &openaiModel{
    45→		name:     modelName,
    46→		apiKey:   cfg.APIKey,
    47→		baseURL:  baseURL,
    48→		client:   &http.Client{},
    49→		wsConfig: wsConfig,
    50→	}, nil
    51→}
    52→
    53→func (m *openaiModel) Name() string { return m.name }
    54→
    55→// GenerateContent converts the ADK request to an OpenAI Responses API request,
    56→// calls the API, and converts the response back to an LLMResponse.
    57→// Streaming is not implemented; the iterator always yields a single response.
    58→func (m *openaiModel) GenerateContent(ctx context.Context, req *model.LLMRequest, _ bool) iter.Seq2[*model.LLMResponse, error] {
    59→	return func(yield func(*model.LLMResponse, error) bool) {
    60→		resp, err := m.doRequest(ctx, req)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			yield(nil, err)
    63→			return
    64→		}
    65→		yield(resp, nil)
    66→	}
    67→}
    68→
    69→// Close is a no-op; the HTTP client has no persistent state.
    70→func (m *openaiModel) Close() {}
    71→
    72→// --- Responses API types ---
    73→
    74→type responsesRequest struct {
    75→	Model        string         `json:"model"`
    76→	Input        []inputItem    `json:"input"`
    77→	Instructions string         `json:"instructions,omitempty"`
    78→	Tools        []responseTool `json:"tools,omitempty"`
    79→	Temperature  *float32       `json:"temperature,omitempty"`
    80→	TopP         *float32       `json:"top_p,omitempty"`
    81→	MaxTokens    int32          `json:"max_output_tokens,omitempty"`
    82→	Store        bool           `json:"store"`
    83→}
    84→
    85→type inputItem struct {
    86→	Type      string `json:"type"`               // "message", "function_call", "function_call_output"
    87→	Role      string `json:"role,omitempty"`
    88→	Content   string `json:"content,omitempty"`
    89→	ID        string `json:"id,omitempty"`
    90→	CallID    string `json:"call_id,omitempty"`
    91→	Name      string `json:"name,omitempty"`
    92→	Arguments string `json:"arguments,omitempty"`
    93→	Output    string `json:"output,omitempty"`
    94→}
    95→
    96→type responseTool struct {
    97→	Type              string         `json:"type"` // "function" or "web_search"
    98→	Name              string         `json:"name,omitempty"`
    99→	Description       string         `json:"description,omitempty"`
   100→	Parameters        any            `json:"parameters,omitempty"`
   101→	SearchContextSize string         `json:"search_context_size,omitempty"`
   102→	UserLocation      any            `json:"user_location,omitempty"`
   103→	Filters           *searchFilters `json:"filters,omitempty"`
   104→}
   105→
   106→type searchFilters struct {
   107→	AllowedDomains []string `json:"allowed_domains,omitempty"`
   108→}
   109→
   110→type responsesResponse struct {
   111→	ID         string       `json:"id"`
   112→	Status     string       `json:"status"`
   113→	Output     []outputItem `json:"output"`
   114→	OutputText string       `json:"output_text"`
   115→	Usage      *respUsage   `json:"usage,omitempty"`
   116→	Error      *apiError    `json:"error,omitempty"`
   117→}
   118→
   119→type outputItem struct {
   120→	Type      string          `json:"type"` // "message", "function_call", "web_search_call"
   121→	ID        string          `json:"id,omitempty"`
   122→	Status    string          `json:"status,omitempty"`
   123→	CallID    string          `json:"call_id,omitempty"`
   124→	Name      string          `json:"name,omitempty"`
   125→	Arguments string          `json:"arguments,omitempty"`
   126→	Role      string          `json:"role,omitempty"`
   127→	Content   []outputContent `json:"content,omitempty"`
   128→}
   129→
   130→type outputContent struct {
   131→	Type        string       `json:"type"` // "output_text"
   132→	Text        string       `json:"text"`
   133→	Annotations []annotation `json:"annotations,omitempty"`
   134→}
   135→
   136→type annotation struct {
   137→	Type       string `json:"type"` // "url_citation"
   138→	URL        string `json:"url"`
   139→	Title      string `json:"title"`
   140→	StartIndex int    `json:"start_index"`
   141→	EndIndex   int    `json:"end_index"`
   142→}
   143→
   144→type respUsage struct {
   145→	InputTokens  int32 `json:"input_tokens"`
   146→	OutputTokens int32 `json:"output_tokens"`
   147→	TotalTokens  int32 `json:"total_tokens"`
   148→}
   149→
   150→type apiError struct {
   151→	Message string `json:"message"`
   152→	Type    string `json:"type"`
   153→	Code    string `json:"code"`
   154→}
   155→
   156→// --- request/response conversion ---
   157→
   158→func (m *openaiModel) doRequest(ctx context.Context, req *model.LLMRequest) (*model.LLMResponse, error) {
   159→	items, instructions := convertInputs(req)
   160→	tools := convertResponseTools(req, m.wsConfig)
   161→
   162→	respReq := responsesRequest{
   163→		Model:        m.name,
   164→		Input:        items,
   165→		Instructions: instructions,
   166→		Tools:        tools,
   167→		Store:        false,
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	if req.Config != nil {
   171→		respReq.Temperature = req.Config.Temperature
   172→		respReq.TopP = req.Config.TopP
   173→		if req.Config.MaxOutputTokens > 0 {
   174→			respReq.MaxTokens = req.Config.MaxOutputTokens
   175→		}
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	body, err := json.Marshal(respReq)
   179→	if err != nil {
   180→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: marshal request: %w", err)
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	httpReq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, m.baseURL+"/responses", bytes.NewReader(body))
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: create http request: %w", err)
   186→	}
   187→	httpReq.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   188→	httpReq.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+m.apiKey)
   189→
   190→	httpResp, err := m.client.Do(httpReq)
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: http request: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→	defer httpResp.Body.Close()
   195→
   196→	respBody, err := io.ReadAll(httpResp.Body)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: read response: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	if httpResp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   202→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: API returned status %d: %s", httpResp.StatusCode, string(respBody))
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	var respOut responsesResponse
   206→	if err := json.Unmarshal(respBody, &respOut); err != nil {
   207→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: unmarshal response: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	if respOut.Error != nil {
   211→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("openai: API error: %s (type=%s, code=%s)",
   212→			respOut.Error.Message, respOut.Error.Type, respOut.Error.Code)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	return convertResponseOutput(&respOut), nil
   216→}
   217→
   218→// convertInputs transforms ADK Contents into Responses API input items and
   219→// extracts the system instruction as a separate instructions string.
   220→func convertInputs(req *model.LLMRequest) ([]inputItem, string) {
   221→	var items []inputItem
   222→	var instructions string
   223→
   224→	// System instruction.
   225→	if req.Config != nil && req.Config.SystemInstruction != nil {
   226→		var text string
   227→		for _, p := range req.Config.SystemInstruction.Parts {
   228→			if p.Text != "" {
   229→				text += p.Text
   230→			}
   231→		}
   232→		instructions = text
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	// Conversation contents.
   236→	for _, c := range req.Contents {
   237→		for _, p := range c.Parts {
   238→			switch {
   239→			case p.FunctionCall != nil:
   240→				argsJSON, _ := json.Marshal(p.FunctionCall.Args)
   241→				items = append(items, inputItem{
   242→					Type:      "function_call",
   243→					CallID:    p.FunctionCall.ID,
   244→					Name:      p.FunctionCall.Name,
   245→					Arguments: string(argsJSON),
   246→				})
   247→			case p.FunctionResponse != nil:
   248→				respJSON, _ := json.Marshal(p.FunctionResponse.Response)
   249→				items = append(items, inputItem{
   250→					Type:   "function_call_output",
   251→					CallID: p.FunctionResponse.ID,
   252→					Output: string(respJSON),
   253→				})
   254→			case p.Text != "":
   255→				items = append(items, inputItem{
   256→					Type:    "message",
   257→					Role:    mapRole(c.Role),
   258→					Content: p.Text,
   259→				})
   260→			}
   261→		}
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	return items, instructions
   265→}
   266→
   267→// convertResponseTools transforms ADK tools to Responses API flat format.
   268→// Function tools become {type: "function", name, description, parameters}.
   269→// If a GoogleSearch marker is present, a {type: "web_search"} tool is added
   270→// using wsConfig for search_context_size and allowed_domains.
   271→func convertResponseTools(req *model.LLMRequest, wsConfig *WebSearch) []responseTool {
   272→	if req.Config == nil || len(req.Config.Tools) == 0 {
   273→		return nil
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	var tools []responseTool
   277→	hasSearch := false
   278→
   279→	for _, t := range req.Config.Tools {
   280→		if t.GoogleSearch != nil {
   281→			hasSearch = true
   282→			continue
   283→		}
   284→		for _, fd := range t.FunctionDeclarations {
   285→			rt := responseTool{
   286→				Type:        "function",
   287→				Name:        fd.Name,
   288→				Description: fd.Description,
   289→			}
   290→			switch {
   291→			case fd.ParametersJsonSchema != nil:
   292→				rt.Parameters = fd.ParametersJsonSchema
   293→			case fd.Parameters != nil:
   294→				rt.Parameters = schemaToMap(fd.Parameters)
   295→			}
   296→			tools = append(tools, rt)
   297→		}
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	if hasSearch {
   301→		wsTool := responseTool{Type: "web_search"}
   302→		if wsConfig != nil {
   303→			wsTool.SearchContextSize = wsConfig.SearchContextSize
   304→			if len(wsConfig.AllowedDomains) > 0 {
   305→				wsTool.Filters = &searchFilters{
   306→					AllowedDomains: wsConfig.AllowedDomains,
   307→				}
   308→			}
   309→		}
   310→		tools = append(tools, wsTool)
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	return tools
   314→}
   315→
   316→// schemaToMap converts a genai.Schema to a JSON-serialisable map that matches
   317→// the OpenAI function parameters format (JSON Schema).
   318→func schemaToMap(s *genai.Schema) map[string]any {
   319→	m := map[string]any{}
   320→	if s.Type != "" {
   321→		m["type"] = strings.ToLower(string(s.Type))
   322→	}
   323→	if s.Description != "" {
   324→		m["description"] = s.Description
   325→	}
   326→	if len(s.Enum) > 0 {
   327→		m["enum"] = s.Enum
   328→	}
   329→	if s.Items != nil {
   330→		m["items"] = schemaToMap(s.Items)
   331→	}
   332→	if len(s.Properties) > 0 {
   333→		props := map[string]any{}
   334→		for k, v := range s.Properties {
   335→			props[k] = schemaToMap(v)
   336→		}
   337→		m["properties"] = props
   338→	}
   339→	if len(s.Required) > 0 {
   340→		m["required"] = s.Required
   341→	}
   342→	return m
   343→}
   344→
   345→// convertResponseOutput transforms a Responses API response into an ADK LLMResponse.
   346→func convertResponseOutput(resp *responsesResponse) *model.LLMResponse {
   347→	llmResp := &model.LLMResponse{
   348→		TurnComplete: true,
   349→	}
   350→
   351→	content := &genai.Content{Role: "model"}
   352→
   353→	for _, item := range resp.Output {
   354→		switch item.Type {
   355→		case "function_call":
   356→			var args map[string]any
   357→			if item.Arguments != "" {
   358→				_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(item.Arguments), &args)
   359→			}
   360→			content.Parts = append(content.Parts, &genai.Part{
   361→				FunctionCall: &genai.FunctionCall{
   362→					ID:   item.CallID,
   363→					Name: item.Name,
   364→					Args: args,
   365→				},
   366→			})
   367→		case "message":
   368→			for _, c := range item.Content {
   369→				if c.Text != "" {
   370→					content.Parts = append(content.Parts, &genai.Part{Text: c.Text})
   371→				}
   372→			}
   373→		case "web_search_call":
   374→			// Skipped — handled internally by the API.
   375→		}
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	if len(content.Parts) == 0 {
   379→		content.Parts = append(content.Parts, &genai.Part{Text: ""})
   380→	}
   381→
   382→	llmResp.Content = content
   383→
   384→	if resp.Usage != nil {
   385→		llmResp.UsageMetadata = &genai.GenerateContentResponseUsageMetadata{
   386→			PromptTokenCount:     resp.Usage.InputTokens,
   387→			CandidatesTokenCount: resp.Usage.OutputTokens,
   388→			TotalTokenCount:      resp.Usage.TotalTokens,
   389→		}
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	return llmResp
   393→}
   394→
   395→func mapRole(role string) string {
   396→	switch role {
   397→	case "model":
   398→		return "assistant"
   399→	default:
   400→		return role
   401→	}
   402→}
   403→

Given the contents of dependency/manifest\nfiles from a software project, extract all dependencies.\n\nFor each dependency, return:\n- name: the package/library name\n- version: the version constraint or pinned version\n- ecosystem: one of \"go\", \"npm\", \"pypi\", \"cargo\", \"rubygems\", \"maven\", \"gradle\", \"docker\", \"other\"\n- upstream_repo: your best guess at the canonical GitHub repository URL (e.g., \"github.com/gorilla/mux\")\n- provider: the Changelogue provider to use for release tracking. // \"gemini\" or \"openai\"\n\tmodel    string\n\n\t// Gemini\n\tgeminiClient *genai.Client\n\n\t// OpenAI\n\topenaiAPIKey  string\n\topenaiBaseURL string\n\thttpClient    *http.Client\n}\n\n// ExtractorConfig holds the configuration for creating a DependencyExtractor.\ntype ExtractorConfig struct {\n\tProvider      string // \"gemini\" or \"openai\"\n\tModel         string\n\tGoogleAPIKey \"openai\",\n\t\t\tmodel:         model,\n\t\t\topenaiAPIKey:  cfg.OpenAIAPIKey,\n\t\t\topenaiBaseURL: baseURL,\n\t\t\thttpClient:    &http.Client{},\n\t\t}, nil\n\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unknown LLM provider: %q (supported: gemini, openai)\", cfg.Provider)\n\t}\n}\n\n// Extract sends file contents to the configured LLM and returns parsed dependencies.\nfunc (e *DependencyExtractor) Extract(ctx context.Context, files []DependencyFile) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {\n\tprompt := BuildExtractionPrompt(files)\n\n\tvar text string\n\tvar err error\n\n\tswitch e.provider {\n\tcase \"gemini\":\n\t\ttext, err = e.extractGemini(ctx, prompt)\n\tcase \"openai\":\n\t\ttext, err = e.extractOpenAI(ctx, prompt)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unknown provider: %s\", e.provider)\n\t}\n\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\treturn ParseDependencies([]byte(text))\n}\n\nfunc (e *DependencyExtractor) extractGemini(ctx context.Context, prompt string) (string, error) {\n\tresp, err := e.geminiClient.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, e.model, []*genai.Content{genai.NewContentFromText(prompt, \"user\")}, &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\t\tSystemInstruction: genai.NewContentFromText(extractionSystemPrompt, \"user\"),\n\t\tTemperature:       genai.Ptr(float32(0.1)),\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"generate content: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(resp.Candidates) == 0 || len(resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"empty response from Gemini\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn resp.Candidates[0].Content.Parts[0].Text, nil\n}\n\n// OpenAI Responses API types (minimal, for dependency extraction only)\ntype openaiResponsesRequest struct {\n\tModel        string           `json:\"model\"`\n\tInput        []openaiInput    `json:\"input\"`\n\tInstructions string           `json:\"instructions,omitempty\"`\n\tTemperature  *float32         `json:\"temperature,omitempty\"`\n\tStore        bool             `json:\"store\"`\n}\n\ntype openaiInput struct {\n\tType    string `json:\"type\"`\n\tRole    string `json:\"role,omitempty\"`\n\tContent string `json:\"content,omitempty\"`\n}\n\ntype openaiResponsesResponse struct {\n\tOutput []openaiOutputItem `json:\"output\"`\n\tError  *openaiError       `json:\"error,omitempty\"`\n}\n\ntype openaiOutputItem struct {\n\tType    string                `json:\"type\"`\n\tContent []openaiOutputContent `json:\"content,omitempty\"`\n}\n\ntype openaiOutputContent struct {\n\tType string `json:\"type\"`\n\tText string `json:\"text\"`\n}\n\ntype openaiError struct {\n\tMessage string `json:\"message\"`\n\tType    string `json:\"type\"`\n}\n\nfunc (e *DependencyExtractor) extractOpenAI(ctx context.Context, prompt string) (string, error) {\n\ttemp := float32(0.1)\n\treqBody := openaiResponsesRequest{\n\t\tModel: e.model,\n\t\tInput: []openaiInput{\n\t\t\t{Type: \"message\", Role: \"user\", Content: prompt},\n\t\t},\n\t\tInstructions: extractionSystemPrompt,\n\t\tTemperature:  &temp,\n\t\tStore:        false,\n\t}\n\n\tbody, err := json.Marshal(reqBody)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"marshal request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\thttpReq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, e.openaiBaseURL+\"/responses\", bytes.NewReader(body))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"create http request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\thttpReq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\thttpReq.Header.Set(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer \"+e.openaiAPIKey)\n\n\thttpResp, err := e.httpClient.Do(httpReq)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"http request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer httpResp.Body.Close()\n\n\trespBody, err := io.ReadAll(httpResp.Body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"read response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif httpResp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"OpenAI API returned status %d: %s\", httpResp.StatusCode, string(respBody))\n\t}\n\n\tvar resp openaiResponsesResponse\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(respBody, &resp); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"unmarshal response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif resp.Error != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"OpenAI API error: %s (type=%s)\", resp.Error.Message, resp.Error.Type)\n\t}\n\n\t// Extract text from message output\n\tfor _, item := range resp.Output {\n\t\tif item.Type == \"message\" {\n\t\t\tfor _, c := range item.Content {\n\t\t\t\tif c.Type == \"output_text\" && c.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn c.Text, nil\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"empty response from OpenAI\")\n}\n\n// BuildExtractionPrompt constructs the user prompt from dependency files.\nfunc BuildExtractionPrompt(files []DependencyFile) string {\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\tb.WriteString(\"Extract all dependencies from these files:\\n\\n\")\n\tfor _, f := range files {\n\t\tb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"--- %s ---\\n%s\\n\\n\", f.Path, f.Content))\n\t}\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\n// ParseDependencies parses the LLM response into ScannedDependency structs.\n// Handles raw JSON arrays and markdown-fenced JSON.\nfunc ParseDependencies(raw []byte) ([]models.ScannedDependency, error) {\n\ttext := strings.TrimSpace(string(raw))\n\n\t// Strip markdown code fences if present\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(text, \"```\") {\n\t\tlines := strings.Split(text, \"\\n\")\n\t\tif len(lines) > 2 {\n\t\t\t// Remove first and last lines (``` markers)\n\t\t\tlines = lines[1 : len(lines)-1]\n\t\t\t// If last line is also ```, it was already removed\n\t\t\tif len(lines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]) == \"```\" {\n\t\t\t\tlines = lines[:len(lines)-1]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttext = strings.Join(lines, \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar deps []models.ScannedDependency\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(text), &deps); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"parse dependencies JSON: %w\\nraw: %s\", err, text)\n\t}\n\treturn deps, nil\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. The dependency extractor now supports both Gemini and OpenAI. Here's what changed:

- **`internal/onboard/gemini.go`** → **`internal/onboard/extractor.go`**: Refactored `DependencyExtractor` to accept an `ExtractorConfig` with `Provider` field. When provider is `"openai"`, it uses the OpenAI Responses API (same pattern as `internal/agent/openai/`). Gemini path is unchanged.

- **`cmd/server/main.go`**: Now passes the full `ExtractorConfig` (provider, model, both API keys) instead of only creating the extractor when `GoogleAPIKey` is set. It will create the extractor for whichever provider is configured via `LLM_PROVIDER`.

- **`internal/onboard/worker.go`**: Updated error message to mention both `GOOGLE_API_KEY` and `OPENAI_API_KEY`.

Since you have `LLM_PROVIDER=openai` and `OPENAI_API_KEY` set, the scan worker should now create the extractor and process dependency scan jobs successfully. Try scanning a repo again.

> AGENT

`Description` and `AgentPrompt` are plain `string` — they can't scan `NULL`. The fix is to use `COALESCE` in the `RETURNING` clause to convert `NULL` to empty string, which is the standard pattern.

> AGENT

Fixed. The issue was that the `INSERT INTO projects ... RETURNING` clause returned `NULL` for `description` and `agent_prompt` (which aren't set during onboard), but the Go `Project` struct uses plain `string` fields that can't scan `NULL`. Added `COALESCE(description, '')` and `COALESCE(agent_prompt, '')` to convert them to empty strings.

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     6→import { onboard as onboardApi, projects as projectsApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
     7→import type { OnboardScan, OnboardSelection, Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
     8→import { Rocket, Loader2, Check, Search, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
     9→
    10→type Step = "input" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied";
    11→
    12→export default function OnboardPage() {
    13→  const router = useRouter();
    14→  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("input");
    15→  const [repoUrl, setRepoUrl] = useState("");
    16→  const [scanId, setScanId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    17→  const [scan, setScan] = useState<OnboardScan | null>(null);
    18→  const [selections, setSelections] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
    19→  const [projectAssignments, setProjectAssignments] = useState<Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; projectId?: string; newName?: string }>>({});
    20→  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);
    21→  const [applyResult, setApplyResult] = useState<{ created_projects: Project[]; created_sources: any[]; skipped: string[] } | null>(null);
    22→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    23→
    24→  // Fetch existing projects for the dropdown
    25→  const { data: projectsResp } = useSWR("projects-all", () => projectsApi.list(1, 200));
    26→  const existingProjects = projectsResp?.data ?? [];
    27→
    28→  // Poll scan status
    29→  useEffect(() => {
    30→    if (step !== "scanning" || !scanId) return;
    31→    const interval = setInterval(async () => {
    32→      try {
    33→        const resp = await onboardApi.getScan(scanId);
    34→        setScan(resp.data);
    35→        if (resp.data.status === "completed") {
    36→          setStep("results");
    37→          // Select all by default
    38→          const deps = resp.data.results ?? [];
    39→          const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};
    40→          const assign: Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; newName?: string }> = {};
    41→          deps.forEach((d, i) => {
    42→            sel[i] = true;
    43→            assign[i] = { mode: "new", newName: d.name.replace(/\//g, "-") };
    44→          });
    45→          setSelections(sel);
    46→          setProjectAssignments(assign);
    47→          clearInterval(interval);
    48→        } else if (resp.data.status === "failed") {
    49→          setError(resp.data.error || "Scan failed");
    50→          setStep("input");
    51→          clearInterval(interval);
    52→        }
    53→      } catch {
    54→        // Keep polling
    55→      }
    56→    }, 2000);
    57→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
    58→  }, [step, scanId]);
    59→
    60→  const startScan = async () => {
    61→    setError(null);
    62→    try {
    63→      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repoUrl);
    64→      setScanId(resp.data.id);
    65→      setScan(resp.data);
    66→      setStep("scanning");
    67→    } catch (e: any) {
    68→      setError(e.message || "Failed to start scan");
    69→    }
    70→  };
    71→
    72→  const applySelections = async () => {
    73→    if (!scan?.results || !scanId) return;
    74→    setApplying(true);
    75→    setError(null);
    76→
    77→    const sels: OnboardSelection[] = [];
    78→    scan.results.forEach((dep, i) => {
    79→      if (!selections[i]) return;
    80→      const assign = projectAssignments[i];
    81→      sels.push({
    82→        dep_name: dep.name,
    83→        upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,
    84→        provider: dep.provider,
    85→        project_id: assign?.mode === "existing" ? assign.projectId : undefined,
    86→        new_project_name: assign?.mode === "new" ? (assign.newName || dep.name.replace(/\//g, "-")) : undefined,
    87→      });
    88→    });
    89→
    90→    try {
    91→      const resp = await onboardApi.apply(scanId, sels);
    92→      setApplyResult(resp.data);
    93→      setStep("applied");
    94→    } catch (e: any) {
    95→      setError(e.message || "Failed to apply selections");
    96→    } finally {
    97→      setApplying(false);
    98→    }
    99→  };
   100→
   101→  const deps = scan?.results ?? [];
   102→  const selectedCount = Object.values(selections).filter(Boolean).length;
   103→

   105→    go: "bg-cyan-900/50 text-cyan-300",
   106→    npm: "bg-red-900/50 text-red-300",
   107→    pypi: "bg-yellow-900/50 text-yellow-300",
   108→    cargo: "bg-orange-900/50 text-orange-300",
   109→    rubygems: "bg-pink-900/50 text-pink-300",
   110→    maven: "bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300",
   111→    docker: "bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300",
   112→    other: "bg-gray-700/50 text-gray-300",
   113→  };
   114→
   115→  return (
   116→    <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-6">
   117→      <div className="mb-6">
   118→        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">Quick Onboard</h1>
   119→        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">

   122→      </div>

   126→          {error}
   127→        </div>
   128→      )}
   129→
   130→      {/* Step 1: Input */}
   131→      {step === "input" && (
   132→        <div className="max-w-xl">

   135→          </label>
   136→          <div className="flex gap-2">
   137→            <input
   138→              type="text"
   139→              value={repoUrl}
   140→              onChange={(e) => setRepoUrl(e.target.value)}
   141→              placeholder="owner/repo or https://github.com/owner/repo"
   142→              className="flex-1 rounded-md border border-[#374151] bg-[#1f2937] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-white placeholder-[#6b7280] focus:border-[#e8601a] focus:outline-none"
   143→              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && repoUrl.trim() && startScan()}
   144→            />
   145→            <button
   146→              onClick={startScan}
   147→              disabled={!repoUrl.trim()}
   148→              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#e8601a] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#d4560f] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
   149→            >
   150→              <Search className="h-4 w-4" />
   151→              Scan
   152→            </button>
   153→          </div>
   154→        </div>
   155→      )}
   156→
   157→      {/* Step 2: Scanning */}
   158→      {step === "scanning" && (
   159→        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20">
   160→          <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-[#e8601a]" />
   161→          <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">
   162→            Scanning repository for dependencies...
   163→          </p>
   164→          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#6b7280]">
   165→            This may take a moment while we analyze dependency files.
   166→          </p>
   167→        </div>
   168→      )}
   169→
   170→      {/* Step 3: Results */}
   171→      {step === "results" && deps.length === 0 && (
   172→        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-center">
   173→          <p className="text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">No dependencies detected in this repository.</p>
   174→          <button
   175→            onClick={() => { setStep("input"); setRepoUrl(""); }}
   176→            className="mt-4 text-sm text-[#e8601a] hover:underline"
   177→          >
   178→            Try another repository
   179→          </button>
   180→        </div>
   181→      )}
   182→
   183→      {step === "results" && deps.length > 0 && (
   184→        <div>
   185→          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   186→            <p className="text-sm text-[#9ca3af]">
   187→              Found <span className="text-white font-medium">{deps.length}</span> dependencies.
   188→              Selected: <span className="text-white font-medium">{selectedCount}</span>
   189→            </p>
   190→            <button
   191→              onClick={applySelections}
   192→              disabled={selectedCount === 0 || applying}
   193→              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-[#e8601a] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-[#d4560f] disabled:opacity-50"
   194→            >
   195→              {applying ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Rocket className="h-4 w-4" />}
   196→              Track Selected ({selectedCount})
   197→            </button>
   198→          </div>
   199→
   200→          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[#374151]">
   201→            <table className="w-full text-sm">

   213→                      }}
   214→                      className="rounded border-[#374151]"
   215→                    />
   216→                  </th>
   217→                  <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium text-[#9ca3af]">Dependency</th>

   222→                </tr>
   223→              </thead>
   224→              <tbody>

    22→
    23→function getProviderUrl(
    24→  provider: string,
    25→  repository: string,
    26→  version: string
    27→): string | null {
    28→  switch (provider) {
    29→    case "github":
    30→      return `https://github.com/${repository}/releases/tag/${version}`;
    31→    case "dockerhub":
    32→      return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;
    33→    case "ecr-public":
    34→      return `https://gallery.ecr.aws/${repository}`;
    35→    case "gitlab":
    36→      return `https://gitlab.com/${repository}/-/releases/${version}`;
    37→    default:
    38→      return null;
    39→  }
    40→}
    41→
    42→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    43→/*  Page                                                               */
    44→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    45→
    46→export default function ReleasesPage() {
    47→  return (
    48→    <Suspense>
    49→      <ReleasesPageInner />
    50→    </Suspense>
    51→  );
    52→}
    53→
    54→function ReleasesPageInner() {
    55→  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
    56→  const initialProject = searchParams.get("project") ?? "all";
    57→  const initialShowExcluded = searchParams.get("show_excluded") !== "false";
    58→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    59→  const [projectFilter, setProjectFilter] = useState<string>(initialProject);
    60→  const [showExcluded, setShowExcluded] = useState(initialShowExcluded);
    61→  const [triggeringVersion, setTriggeringVersion] = useState<string | null>(null);
    62→
    63→  /* Fetch projects for the filter dropdown */
    64→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR("projects-for-filter", async () => {
    65→    const firstPage = await projectsApi.list(1, 100);
    66→    return firstPage;
    67→  });
    68→
    69→  /* Fetch releases — scoped by project or all */
    70→  const { data: scopedData, isLoading: scopedLoading } = useSWR(
    71→    projectFilter !== "all" ? ["releases", page, projectFilter, showExcluded] : null,
    72→    () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectFilter, page, PER_PAGE, showExcluded)
    73→  );
    74→
    75→  const { data: allReleasesData, isLoading: allLoading } = useSWR(
    76→    projectFilter === "all" ? ["all-releases", page, showExcluded] : null,
    77→    () => releasesApi.list(page, PER_PAGE, showExcluded)
    78→  );
    79→
    80→  const isLoading = projectFilter !== "all" ? scopedLoading : allLoading;

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import React, { useState, useMemo, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
     4→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     5→import Link from "next/link";
     6→import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
     7→import {
     8→  projects as projectsApi,
     9→  releases as releasesApi,
    10→  sources as sourcesApi,
    11→  semanticReleases as srApi,
    12→} from "@/lib/api/client";
    13→import { getProviderIcon } from "@/components/ui/provider-badge";
    14→import { ProjectLogo } from "@/components/ui/project-logo";
    15→import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/format";
    16→import { Plus, ArrowRight, LayoutGrid, List, Search, Pencil, ArrowUpDown, Loader2, Info } from "lucide-react";
    17→import { SourceForm } from "@/components/sources/source-form";
    18→import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
    19→import { ProjectForm } from "@/components/projects/project-form";
    20→import type { Project, Source } from "@/lib/api/types";
    21→import { URGENCY_STYLES, URGENCY_COLORS } from "@/components/ui/urgency-pill";
    22→
    23→/* ---------- Project Card Logo ---------- */
    24→
    25→function ProjectCardLogo({ projectId, name }: { projectId: string; name: string }) {
    26→  const { data } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}-card-sources`, () =>
    27→    sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)
    28→  );
    29→  return <ProjectLogo name={name} sources={data?.data} size={40} />;
    30→}
    31→
    32→/* ---------- Overflow Flow ---------- */
    33→
    34→const LINE_HEIGHT = 28; // matches leading-7 (1.75rem = 28px)
    35→const MAX_LINES = 2;
    36→const MAX_HEIGHT = LINE_HEIGHT * MAX_LINES;
    37→
    38→function FlowSection({
    39→  label,
    40→  moreHref,
    41→  children,
    42→}: {
    43→  label: string;
    44→  moreHref: string;
    45→  children: React.ReactNode;
    46→}) {
    47→  const measureRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    48→  const moreRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
    49→  const [visibleCount, setVisibleCount] = useState<number | null>(null);
    50→
    51→  const items = React.Children.toArray(children);
    52→
    53→  const measure = useCallback(() => {
    54→    const el = measureRef.current;
    55→    if (!el) return;
    56→    const nodes = el.children;
    57→    const itemNodeCount = nodes.length - 2; // exclude label (first) and hidden "more…" span (last)
    58→    // Check if everything fits without overflow
    59→    if (el.scrollHeight <= MAX_HEIGHT + 4) {
    60→      setVisibleCount(items.length);
    61→      return;
    62→    }
    63→    // Measure actual "more…" link width
    64→    const moreWidth = moreRef.current ? moreRef.current.offsetWidth + 8 : 52;
    65→    const containerWidth = el.offsetWidth;
    66→    // First pass: find all items that fit within MAX_HEIGHT (2 lines)
    67→    let lastFitting = 0;
    68→    for (let i = 1; i <= itemNodeCount; i++) {
    69→      const node = nodes[i] as HTMLElement;
    70→      if (node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight > MAX_HEIGHT) break;
    71→      lastFitting = i;
    72→    }
    73→    // Second pass: walk backward from lastFitting until "more…" fits after the item
    74→    let count = lastFitting;
    75→    while (count > 1) {
    76→      const node = nodes[count] as HTMLElement;
    77→      const nodeBottom = node.offsetTop + node.offsetHeight;
    78→      // If this item is on line 1, "more…" can always wrap to line 2 — we're fine
    79→      if (nodeBottom <= LINE_HEIGHT) break;
    80→      // Item is on line 2 — check if "more…" fits after it on the same line
    81→      const nodeRight = node.offsetLeft + node.offsetWidth;
    82→      if (nodeRight + moreWidth <= containerWidth) break;
    83→      count--;
    84→    }
    85→    setVisibleCount(Math.max(1, count));
    86→  }, [items.length]);
    87→
    88→  useEffect(() => {
    89→    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(measure);
    90→    const obs = new ResizeObserver(measure);
    91→    if (measureRef.current) obs.observe(measureRef.current);
    92→    return () => {
    93→      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    94→      obs.disconnect();
    95→    };
    96→  }, [measure, items.length]);
    97→
    98→  const hasOverflow = visibleCount !== null && visibleCount < items.length;
    99→  const displayed = visibleCount !== null ? items.slice(0, visibleCount) : items;
   100→
   101→  return (
   102→    <div className="relative">
   103→      {/* Hidden measurer — renders all items to find cutoff */}
   104→      <div
   105→        ref={measureRef}
   106→        className="text-[13px] leading-7"
   107→        style={{ position: "absolute", visibility: "hidden", top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, pointerEvents: "none" }}
   108→        aria-hidden="true"
   109→      >
   110→        <span className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5">
   111→          {label}:
   112→        </span>
   113→        {items}
   114→        {/* Hidden "more…" to measure its actual width */}
   115→        <span ref={moreRef} className="inline-flex items-baseline text-[12px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
   116→          more…
   117→        </span>
   118→      </div>
   119→      {/* Visible section */}
   120→      <div className="text-[13px] leading-7">
   121→        <span
   122→          className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] mr-1.5"
   123→          style={{ color: "#9ca3af", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   124→        >
   125→          {label}:
   126→        </span>
   127→        {displayed}
   128→        {hasOverflow && (
   129→          <Link
   130→            href={moreHref}
   131→            className="inline-flex items-baseline text-[12px] font-medium hover:underline whitespace-nowrap"
   132→            style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   133→          >
   134→            more…
   135→          </Link>
   136→        )}
   137→      </div>
   138→    </div>
   139→  );
   140→}
   141→

   144→function UrgencyLegend() {
   145→  const entries = [
   146→    { key: "critical", label: "Critical", desc: "Breaking changes, security patches" },
   147→    { key: "high", label: "High", desc: "Significant API changes, deprecations" },
   148→    { key: "medium", label: "Medium", desc: "Notable changes worth reviewing" },
   149→    { key: "low", label: "Low", desc: "Routine updates, dependency bumps" },

   152→  return (

   162→        {entries.map(({ key, label, desc }) => {

   170→              >

   174→                <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold leading-tight" style={{ color: s.text }}>{label}</span>

   256→              backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   257→              border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   258→            }}
   259→          >
   260→            <span
   261→              className="inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
   262→              style={{ backgroundColor: source.enabled ? "#16a34a" : "#d1d5db" }}
   263→            />
   264→            {(() => { const Icon = getProviderIcon(source.provider); return Icon ? <Icon size={12} className="shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} /> : null; })()}
   265→            <span style={{ fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace", color: "#374151" }}>
   266→              {source.repository}
   267→            </span>
   268→            {source.last_polled_at && (
   269→              <span style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   270→                {timeAgo(source.last_polled_at).replace(" ago", "")}
   271→              </span>
   272→            )}
   273→            <Pencil className="h-2.5 w-2.5 hidden group-hover/chip:inline shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af", opacity: 0.5 }} />
   274→          </button>
   275→        ))}
   276→        <button
   277→          onClick={() => setSourceCreateOpen(true)}
   278→          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium rounded border transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   279→          style={{
   280→            borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   281→            color: "#9ca3af",
   282→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   283→          }}
   284→        >
   285→          <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
   286→          Add Source
   287→        </button>
   288→      </div>
   289→
   290→      {/* Releases flow */}
   291→      {releases.length > 0 && (
   292→        <FlowSection label="Releases" moreHref={`/releases?project=${project.id}`}>
   293→          {releases.map((r) => {
   294→            const src = sourceMap.get(r.source_id);
   295→            const matchingSr = srItems.find((sr) => sr.version === r.version);
   296→            return (
   297→              <span key={r.id} className="inline-flex items-baseline mr-2.5">
   298→                <Link
   299→                  href={`/releases/${r.id}`}
   300→                  className={r.excluded ? "" : "text-[#2563eb] hover:underline"}
   301→                  style={{
   302→                    fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   303→                    fontSize: "12px",
   304→                    ...(r.excluded ? { color: "#c4c4c0" } : {}),
   305→                  }}
   306→                >
   307→                  {r.version}
   308→                </Link>
   309→                {!r.excluded && matchingSr?.status === "completed" && (() => {
   310→                  const pill = matchingSr.report?.urgency
   311→                    ? URGENCY_STYLES[matchingSr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   312→                    : undefined;
   313→                  return pill ? (
   314→                    <Link
   315→                      href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${matchingSr.id}`}
   316→                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full ml-1 transition-colors"
   317→                      style={{ backgroundColor: pill.bg, border: `1px solid ${pill.border}`, color: pill.text, width: 18, height: 18 }}
   318→                      title={`${matchingSr.report!.urgency} — View report`}
   319→                    >
   320→                      <pill.icon size={10} />
   321→                    </Link>
   322→                  ) : (
   323→                    <Link
   324→                      href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${matchingSr.id}`}
   325→                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 ml-1 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-colors"
   326→                      style={{ backgroundColor: "rgba(107,114,128,0.08)", border: "1px solid rgba(107,114,128,0.18)", color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   327→                      title="View report"
   328→                    >
   329→                      Report
   330→                    </Link>
   331→                  );
   332→                })()}
   333→                {!r.excluded && (matchingSr?.status === "pending" || matchingSr?.status === "processing") && (
   334→                  <span
   335→                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded px-1 py-0.5 ml-1 text-[10px]"
   336→                    style={{ backgroundColor: "#fef9c3", color: "#92400e", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   337→                  >
   338→                    <Loader2 size={10} className="animate-spin" />
   339→                  </span>
   340→                )}
   341→                {src && (
   342→                  <span
   343→                    className="text-[11px] ml-1 hidden sm:inline"
   344→                    style={{ color: r.excluded ? "#dcdcda" : "#9ca3af" }}
   345→                  >
   346→                    ({src.repository.split("/").pop()})
   347→                  </span>
   348→                )}
   349→                <span
   350→                  className="text-[11px] ml-1"
   351→                  style={{ color: r.excluded ? "#dcdcda" : "#c4c4c0" }}
   352→                >
   353→                  {timeAgo(r.released_at || r.created_at).replace(" ago", "")}
   354→                </span>
   355→              </span>
   356→            );
   357→          })}
   358→        </FlowSection>
   359→      )}
   360→
   361→      {/* Add Source dialog */}
   362→      <Dialog open={sourceCreateOpen} onOpenChange={setSourceCreateOpen}>
   363→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   364→          <DialogHeader>
   365→            <DialogTitle>Add Source</DialogTitle>
   366→          </DialogHeader>
   367→          <SourceForm
   368→            title="Add Source"
   369→            projectId={project.id}
   370→            onSubmit={async (input) => {
   371→              const res = await sourcesApi.create(project.id, input);
   372→              if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   373→            }}
   374→            onSuccess={() => {
   375→              setSourceCreateOpen(false);
   376→              mutateSources();
   377→            }}
   378→            onCancel={() => setSourceCreateOpen(false)}
   379→          />
   380→        </DialogContent>
   381→      </Dialog>
   382→
   383→      {/* Edit Source dialog */}
   384→      <Dialog
   385→        open={!!editingSource}
   386→        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setEditingSource(null); }}
   387→      >
   388→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   389→          <DialogHeader>
   390→            <DialogTitle>Edit Source</DialogTitle>
   391→          </DialogHeader>
   392→          {editingSource && (
   393→            <SourceForm
   394→              key={editingSource.id}
   395→              title="Edit Source"
   396→              initial={editingSource}
   397→              onSubmit={async (input) => {
   398→                await sourcesApi.update(editingSource.id, input);
   399→              }}
   400→              onSuccess={() => {
   401→                setEditingSource(null);
   402→                mutateSources();
   403→              }}
   404→              onCancel={() => setEditingSource(null)}
   405→            />
   406→          )}
   407→        </DialogContent>
   408→      </Dialog>
   409→    </div>
   410→  );
   411→}
   412→
   413→/* ---------- Compact Row ---------- */
   414→
   415→function ProjectCompactRow({ project }: { project: Project }) {
   416→  const router = useRouter();
   417→  const { data: relData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-releases`, () =>
   418→    releasesApi.listByProject(project.id, 1, 5, true)
   419→  );
   420→  const { data: srcData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sources`, () =>
   421→    sourcesApi.listByProject(project.id)
   422→  );
   423→  const { data: srData } = useSWR(`project-${project.id}-card-sr`, () =>
   424→    srApi.list(project.id, 1, 3)
   425→  );
   426→
   427→  const releases = relData?.data ?? [];
   428→  const sources = srcData?.data ?? [];
   429→  const srItems = srData?.data ?? [];
   430→  const latest = releases[0];
   431→  const latestSr = srItems[0];
   432→
   433→  const sourceMap = useMemo(() => {
   434→    const m = new Map<string, Source>();
   435→    for (const s of sources) m.set(s.id, s);
   436→    return m;
   437→  }, [sources]);
   438→
   439→  const latestSrc = latest ? sourceMap.get(latest.source_id) : undefined;
   440→  const latestIcon = latestSrc ? getProviderIcon(latestSrc.provider) : undefined;
   441→
   442→  const urgencyStyle = latestSr?.report?.urgency
   443→    ? URGENCY_COLORS[latestSr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   444→    : undefined;
   445→
   446→  return (
   447→    <div
   448→      onClick={() => router.push(`/projects/${project.id}`)}
   449→      className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-md px-4 py-2.5 transition-colors hover:bg-[#f9f9f7] cursor-pointer"
   450→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   451→    >
   452→      {/* Project name */}
   453→      <span
   454→        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[14px] font-bold shrink-0"
   455→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)", color: "#e8601a", minWidth: "160px" }}
   456→      >
   457→        <ProjectLogo name={project.name} sources={sources} size={24} />
   458→        {project.name}
   459→      </span>
   460→
   461→      {/* Latest release */}
   462→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0" style={{ minWidth: "140px" }}>
   463→        {latest ? (
   464→          <>
   465→            <Link
   466→              href={`/releases/${latest.id}`}
   467→              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   468→              className="inline-flex items-center rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] leading-none hover:ring-1 hover:ring-gray-300 transition-shadow"
   469→              style={{
   470→                backgroundColor: "#f3f3f1",
   471→                fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   472→                color: "#374151",
   473→              }}
   474→            >
   475→              {latest.version}
   476→            </Link>
   477→            {latestIcon && latestIcon({ size: 12, className: "shrink-0", style: { color: "#9ca3af" } })}
   478→            {latestSr?.status === "completed" && (() => {
   479→              const pill = latestSr.report?.urgency
   480→                ? URGENCY_STYLES[latestSr.report.urgency.toLowerCase()]
   481→                : undefined;
   482→              return pill ? (
   483→                <Link
   484→                  href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${latestSr.id}`}
   485→                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   486→                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full transition-colors"
   487→                  style={{ backgroundColor: pill.bg, border: `1px solid ${pill.border}`, color: pill.text, width: 18, height: 18 }}
   488→                  title={`${latestSr.report!.urgency} — View report`}
   489→                >
   490→                  <pill.icon size={10} />
   491→                </Link>
   492→              ) : (
   493→                <Link
   494→                  href={`/projects/${project.id}/semantic-releases/${latestSr.id}`}
   495→                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   496→                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-colors"
   497→                  style={{ backgroundColor: "rgba(107,114,128,0.08)", border: "1px solid rgba(107,114,128,0.18)", color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   498→                  title="View report"
   499→                >
   500→                  Report
   501→                </Link>
   502→              );
   503→            })()}
   504→            {(latestSr?.status === "pending" || latestSr?.status === "processing") && (
   505→              <span
   506→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px]"
   507→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#fef9c3", color: "#92400e", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   508→              >
   509→                <Loader2 size={10} className="animate-spin" />
   510→              </span>
   511→            )}
   512→          </>
   513→        ) : (
   514→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   515→            No releases
   516→          </span>
   517→        )}
   518→      </span>
   519→
   520→      {/* Summary */}
   521→      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
   522→        {latestSr?.status === "completed" ? (
   523→          <>
   524→            {urgencyStyle && (
   525→              <span
   526→                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-none shrink-0"
   527→                style={{ backgroundColor: urgencyStyle.bg, color: urgencyStyle.text }}
   528→              >
   529→                {latestSr.report?.urgency}
   530→              </span>
   531→            )}
   532→            {latestSr.report?.summary && (
   533→              <span className="text-[11px] truncate" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   534→                {latestSr.report.summary.length > 60
   535→                  ? latestSr.report.summary.slice(0, 60) + "\u2026"
   536→                  : latestSr.report.summary}
   537→              </span>
   538→            )}
   539→          </>
   540→        ) : (
   541→          <span className="text-[12px] italic" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }}>
   542→            {latestSr ? latestSr.status : "No analysis"}
   543→          </span>
   544→        )}
   545→      </span>
   546→
   547→      {/* Arrow */}
   548→      <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: "#c4c4c0" }} />
   549→    </div>
   550→  );
   551→}
   552→
   553→/* ---------- Page ---------- */
   554→
   555→export default function ProjectsPage() {
   556→  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
   557→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"cards" | "compact">("cards");
   558→  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   559→  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<"updated" | "added" | "name">("updated");
   560→  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
   561→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR("projects", () => projectsApi.list(1, 100));
   562→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   563→
   564→  const PAGE_SIZE = 12;
   565→  const filtered = search
   566→    ? items.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
   567→    : items;
   568→  const sorted = useMemo(() => {
   569→    const arr = [...filtered];
   570→    switch (sortBy) {
   571→      case "updated":
   572→        return arr.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.updated_at).getTime() - new Date(a.updated_at).getTime());
   573→      case "added":
   574→        return arr.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime());
   575→      case "name":
   576→        return arr.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
   577→    }
   578→  }, [filtered, sortBy]);
   579→  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sorted.length / PAGE_SIZE));
   580→  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
   581→  const paged = sorted.slice((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, currentPage * PAGE_SIZE);
   582→
   583→  return (
   584→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   585→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   586→        <div>
   587→          <h1
   588→            style={{
   589→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   590→              fontSize: "24px",
   591→              fontWeight: 700,
   592→              color: "#111113",
   593→            }}
   594→          >
   595→            Projects
   596→          </h1>
   597→          <p
   598→            className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280] inline-flex items-center gap-1"
   599→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   600→          >
   601→            Tracked projects, releases, and urgency at a glance.
   602→            <UrgencyLegend />
   603→          </p>
   604→        </div>
   605→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   606→          <div className="relative">
   607→            <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />
   608→            <input
   609→              type="text"
   610→              value={search}
   611→              onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
   612→              placeholder="Search projects..."
   613→              className="rounded-md border py-1.5 pl-8 pr-3 text-[13px] w-48"
   614→              style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}
   615→            />
   616→          </div>
   617→          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[13px]" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}>
   618→            <ArrowUpDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }} />
   619→            <select
   620→              value={sortBy}
   621→              onChange={(e) => { setSortBy(e.target.value as "updated" | "added" | "name"); setPage(1); }}
   622→              className="bg-transparent outline-none cursor-pointer text-[13px]"
   623→              style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)", color: "#374151" }}
   624→            >
   625→              <option value="updated">Recently updated</option>
   626→              <option value="added">Recently added</option>
   627→              <option value="name">Name</option>
   628→            </select>
   629→          </div>
   630→          <div className="flex items-center rounded-md border" style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5" }}>
   631→            <button
   632→              onClick={() => setViewMode("cards")}
   633→              className="p-1.5 rounded-l-md transition-colors"
   634→              style={{
   635→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "cards" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   636→                color: viewMode === "cards" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   637→              }}
   638→              title="Card view"
   639→            >
   640→              <LayoutGrid className="h-4 w-4" />
   641→            </button>
   642→            <button
   643→              onClick={() => setViewMode("compact")}
   644→              className="p-1.5 rounded-r-md transition-colors"
   645→              style={{
   646→                backgroundColor: viewMode === "compact" ? "#f3f3f1" : "transparent",
   647→                color: viewMode === "compact" ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   648→              }}
   649→              title="Compact view"
   650→            >
   651→              <List className="h-4 w-4" />
   652→            </button>
   653→          </div>
   654→          <button
   655→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   656→            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   657→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   658→          >
   659→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   660→            New Project
   661→          </button>
   662→        </div>
   663→      </div>
   664→
   665→      {isLoading ? (
   666→        <p
   667→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   668→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   669→        >
   670→          Loading...
   671→        </p>
   672→      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
   673→        <div
   674→          className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-md border py-12"
   675→          style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   676→        >
   677→          <p
   678→            className="text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   679→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   680→          >
   681→            No projects yet — create one to start tracking releases
   682→          </p>
   683→          <button
   684→            onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}
   685→            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   686→            style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   687→          >
   688→            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   689→            New Project
   690→          </button>
   691→        </div>
   692→      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
   693→        <p
   694→          className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[14px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
   695→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
   696→        >
   697→          No projects matching &ldquo;{search}&rdquo;
   698→        </p>
   699→      ) : (
   700→        <>
   701→          <div className={viewMode === "cards" ? "space-y-5" : "space-y-1.5"}>
   702→            {paged.map((project) =>
   703→              viewMode === "cards" ? (
   704→                <ProjectFlowCard key={project.id} project={project} />
   705→              ) : (
   706→                <ProjectCompactRow key={project.id} project={project} />
   707→              )
   708→            )}
   709→          </div>
   710→          {totalPages > 1 && (
   711→            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
   712→              <button
   713→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
   714→                disabled={currentPage <= 1}
   715→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   716→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   717→              >
   718→                Previous
   719→              </button>
   720→              <span
   721→                className="text-[13px]"
   722→                style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   723→              >
   724→                Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
   725→              </span>
   726→              <button
   727→                onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}
   728→                disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
   729→                className="rounded-md border px-3 py-1 text-[13px] transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
   730→                style={{ borderColor: "#e8e8e5", color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   731→              >
   732→                Next
   733→              </button>
   734→            </div>
   735→          )}
   736→        </>
   737→      )}
   738→
   739→      <Dialog open={createOpen} onOpenChange={setCreateOpen}>
   740→        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
   741→          <DialogHeader>
   742→            <DialogTitle>Create Project</DialogTitle>
   743→          </DialogHeader>
   744→          <ProjectForm
   745→            title="Create Project"
   746→            onSubmit={async (result) => {
   747→              const created = await projectsApi.create(result.project);
   748→              if (result.source && created.data?.id) {
   749→                const res = await sourcesApi.create(created.data.id, result.source);
   750→                if (res.data?.id) sourcesApi.poll(res.data.id).catch(() => {});
   751→              }
   752→            }}
   753→            onSuccess={() => { setCreateOpen(false); mutate("projects"); }}
   754→            onCancel={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
   755→          />
   756→        </DialogContent>
   757→      </Dialog>
   758→    </div>
   759→  );
   760→}
   761→

<system-reminder>
Whenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.

**Problem:** The onboard page used dark-theme colors (`bg-[#1f2937]`, `text-white`, `border-[#374151]`, `bg-[#111827]`) inherited from the subagent's defaults, while the app uses a light theme.

**Fix — matched the app's design system:**

- **Typography:** `var(--font-fraunces)` for the heading, `var(--font-dm-sans)` for body text, `JetBrains Mono` for dependency names and versions

- **Ecosystem badges:** Light-colored pills with subtle borders (e.g. Go = cyan tint, npm = red tint) instead of dark semi-transparent backgrounds
- **Version chips:** Light `#f3f3f1` background matching the rest of the app
- **Buttons:** `#e8601a` accent color, consistent with the global `--beacon-accent`
- **Error/success alerts:** Light red/green backgrounds matching standard patterns instead of dark tinted ones
- **Table rows:** Unselected rows dimmed with opacity, hover uses `#fafaf9`, header uses uppercase tracking labels like the projects page

### 1. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/page.tsx` - Default/Home Page

This is the Dashboard page (the home page). It's a client component that:
- Displays a "Dashboard" heading using the Fraunces font
- Shows a `DiscoverySection` component
- Renders `StatsCards`, `ReleaseTrendChart`, and `UnifiedFeed` components when projects exist
- Has a loading state and empty state fallback
- Connects to an SSE (Server-Sent Events) endpoint at `/api/v1/events` to listen for real-time release updates
- Uses SWR for data fetching and revalidates on new events

### 2. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/layout/sidebar.tsx` - Navigation Structure

The Sidebar is a collapsible navigation component with these menu items:
- Dashboard (/)
- Quick Onboard (/onboard)
- Projects (/projects)
- Todo (/todo)
- Releases (/releases)
- Channels (/channels)
- Subscriptions (/subscriptions)

Features:
- Toggleable expand/collapse (52px when collapsed, 200px when expanded)
- active state styling with orange left border (#e8601a)
- Shows icons from Lucide React when collapsed, adds text labels when expanded
- Dark theme (#16181c background)
- Uses `usePathname()` to determine active route

### 3. `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/layout.tsx` - Root Layout

The root layout provides:
- Global metadata (title: "Changelogue", description: "Agent-driven release intelligence platform")
- Two Google Fonts: Fraunces and DM_Sans (set as CSS variables)
- Two-column layout: Sidebar + main content area
- Main content area is scrollable with padding and fade-in animation
- Full-screen flex layout (100vh height)

     1→// web/app/page.tsx
     2→"use client";
     3→
     4→import { useEffect } from "react";
     5→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     6→import { StatsCards } from "@/components/dashboard/stats-cards";
     7→import { ReleaseTrendChart } from "@/components/dashboard/release-trend-chart";
     8→import { UnifiedFeed } from "@/components/dashboard/unified-feed";
     9→import { DashboardEmptyState } from "@/components/dashboard/empty-state";
    10→import { DiscoverySection } from "@/components/dashboard/discovery-section";
    11→import { projects as projectsApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
    12→
    13→const SSE_BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
    14→
    15→export default function DashboardPage() {
    16→  const { data: projectsData, isLoading } = useSWR("projects-for-dashboard", () =>
    17→    projectsApi.list()
    18→  );
    19→
    20→  // Listen to SSE events and revalidate dashboard data on new releases
    21→  useEffect(() => {
    22→    let es: EventSource | null = null;
    23→    let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    24→
    25→    function connect() {
    26→      try {
    27→        es = new EventSource(`${SSE_BASE}/events`);
    28→        es.onmessage = () => {
    29→          // Revalidate all dashboard SWR keys on any event
    30→          mutate((key) => typeof key === "string" && (
    31→            key === "stats" ||
    32→            key.startsWith("trend-") ||
    33→            key === "unified-feed"
    34→          ), undefined, { revalidate: true });
    35→        };
    36→        es.onerror = () => {
    37→          es?.close();
    38→          retryTimer = setTimeout(connect, 5000);
    39→        };
    40→      } catch {
    41→        retryTimer = setTimeout(connect, 5000);
    42→      }
    43→    }
    44→
    45→    connect();
    46→    return () => {
    47→      es?.close();
    48→      if (retryTimer) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
    49→    };
    50→  }, []);
    51→
    52→  const hasProjects = !isLoading && projectsData?.data && projectsData.data.length > 0;
    53→
    54→  return (
    55→    <div className="space-y-6">
    56→      <h1
    57→        style={{
    58→          fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
    59→          fontSize: "24px",
    60→          fontWeight: 700,
    61→          color: "#111113",
    62→        }}
    63→      >
    64→        Dashboard
    65→      </h1>
    66→
    67→      <DiscoverySection />
    68→
    69→      {hasProjects ? (
    70→        <>
    71→          <StatsCards />
    72→          <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
    73→            <ReleaseTrendChart />
    74→            <UnifiedFeed />
    75→          </div>
    76→        </>
    77→      ) : isLoading ? (
    78→        <div
    79→          className="py-16 text-center"
    80→          style={{
    81→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    82→            fontSize: "13px",
    83→            color: "#6b7280",
    84→          }}
    85→        >
    86→          Loading...
    87→        </div>
    88→      ) : (
    89→        <DashboardEmptyState />
    90→      )}
    91→    </div>
    92→  );
    93→}
    94→

    74→        {navItems.map((item) => {

     1→// web/components/dashboard/discovery-section.tsx
     2→"use client";
     3→
     4→import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
     5→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
