     7→import { FaGithub, FaDocker } from "react-icons/fa";
     8→import { discover, projects, sources } from "@/lib/api/client";
     9→import type { DiscoverItem } from "@/lib/api/types";
    10→
    11→function formatStars(count: number): string {
    12→  if (count >= 1000) {
    13→    const k = count / 1000;
    14→    return k >= 10 ? `${Math.round(k)}k` : `${Math.round(k * 10) / 10}k`;
    15→  }
    16→  return String(count);
    17→}
    18→
    19→export function DiscoverySection() {
    20→  const [trackingIds, setTrackingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    21→  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false);
    22→
    23→  const { data: githubData, isLoading } = useSWR(
    24→    "discover-github",
    25→    () => discover.github(),
    26→    { revalidateOnFocus: false },
    27→  );
    28→
    29→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(
    30→    "projects-list",
    31→    () => projects.list(1, 200),
    32→    { revalidateOnFocus: false },
    33→  );
    34→
    35→  const trackedNames = useMemo(
    36→    () =>
    37→      new Set(
    38→        (projectsData?.data ?? []).flatMap((p) => [
    39→          p.name,
    40→          p.name.split("/").pop()!,
    41→        ]),
    42→      ),
    43→    [projectsData],
    44→  );
    45→
    46→  const allItems = useMemo(() => githubData?.data ?? [], [githubData]);
    47→
    48→  const isTracked = useCallback(
    49→    (item: DiscoverItem) =>
    50→      trackedNames.has(item.full_name) || trackedNames.has(item.name),
    51→    [trackedNames],
    52→  );
    53→
    54→  const handleTrack = useCallback(
    55→    async (item: DiscoverItem) => {
    56→      const key = item.full_name;
    57→      setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));
    58→      try {
    59→        const projRes = await projects.create({
    60→          name: item.full_name,
    61→          description: item.description,
    62→        });
    63→        const project = projRes.data;
    64→
    65→        const provider = item.provider === "github" ? "github" : "dockerhub";
    66→        const sourceRes = await sources.create(project.id, {
    67→          provider,
    68→          repository: item.full_name,
    69→          poll_interval_seconds: 86400,
    70→          enabled: true,
    71→        });
    72→
    73→        await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);
    74→        await mutate("projects-list");
    75→        await mutate("projects-for-dashboard");
    76→      } catch (err: unknown) {
    77→        const msg =
    78→          err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to track project";
    79→        alert(msg);
    80→      } finally {
    81→        setTrackingIds((prev) => {
    82→          const next = new Set(prev);
    83→          next.delete(key);
    84→          return next;
    85→        });
    86→      }
    87→    },
    88→    [],
    89→  );
    90→
    91→  if (isLoading) {
    92→    return (
    93→      <div
    94→        className="rounded-lg bg-white px-5 py-4"
    95→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
    96→      >
    97→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    98→          <span
    99→            style={{
   100→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   101→              fontSize: "14px",
   102→              fontWeight: 600,
   103→              color: "#111113",
   104→            }}
   105→          >
   106→            Trending
   107→          </span>
   108→          <div className="flex gap-3 overflow-hidden">
   109→            {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
   110→              <div
   111→                key={i}
   112→                className="h-8 w-48 flex-shrink-0 animate-pulse rounded-full"
   113→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#f0f0ed" }}
   114→              />
   115→            ))}
   116→          </div>
   117→        </div>
   118→      </div>
   119→    );
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  if (allItems.length === 0) return null;
   123→
   124→  return (
   125→    <div
   126→      className="rounded-lg bg-white"
   127→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", overflow: "hidden" }}
   128→    >
   129→      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 px-5 py-3">
   130→        {/* Label */}
   131→        <span
   132→          className="flex-shrink-0"
   133→          style={{
   134→            fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   135→            fontSize: "14px",
   136→            fontWeight: 600,
   137→            color: "#111113",
   138→          }}
   139→        >
   140→          Trending
   141→        </span>
   142→
   143→        {/* Marquee container */}
   144→        <div
   145→          className="relative flex-1 overflow-hidden"
   146→          onMouseEnter={() => setPaused(true)}
   147→          onMouseLeave={() => setPaused(false)}
   148→        >
   149→          {/* Fade edges */}
   150→          <div
   151→            className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 top-0 z-10 h-full w-8"
   152→            style={{
   153→              background:
   154→                "linear-gradient(to right, white, transparent)",
   155→            }}
   156→          />
   157→          <div
   158→            className="pointer-events-none absolute right-0 top-0 z-10 h-full w-8"
   159→            style={{
   160→              background:
   161→                "linear-gradient(to left, white, transparent)",
   162→            }}
   163→          />
   164→
   165→          {/* Scrolling track — duplicated for seamless loop */}
   166→          <div
   167→            className="flex gap-2.5"
   168→            style={{
   169→              animationName: "marquee",
   170→              animationDuration: `${Math.max(allItems.length * 6, 60)}s`,
   171→              animationTimingFunction: "linear",
   172→              animationIterationCount: "infinite",
   173→              animationPlayState: paused ? "paused" : "running",
   174→              width: "max-content",
   175→            }}
   176→          >
   177→            {[...allItems, ...allItems].map((item, idx) => {
   178→              const tracked = isTracked(item);
   179→              const tracking = trackingIds.has(item.full_name);
   180→
   181→              return (
   182→                <MarqueeChip
   183→                  key={`${item.full_name}-${idx}`}
   184→                  item={item}
   185→                  tracked={tracked}
   186→                  tracking={tracking}
   187→                  onTrack={handleTrack}
   188→                />
   189→              );
   190→            })}
   191→          </div>
   192→        </div>
   193→      </div>
   194→
   195→      {/* Keyframes injected via style tag */}
   196→      <style>{`
   197→        @keyframes marquee {
   198→          0% { transform: translateX(0); }
   199→          100% { transform: translateX(-50%); }
   200→        }
   201→      `}</style>
   202→    </div>
   203→  );
   204→}
   205→
   206→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   207→
   208→function MarqueeChip({
   209→  item,
   210→  tracked,
   211→  tracking,
   212→  onTrack,
   213→}: {
   214→  item: DiscoverItem;
   215→  tracked: boolean;
   216→  tracking: boolean;
   217→  onTrack: (item: DiscoverItem) => void;
   218→}) {
   219→  const ProviderIcon =
   220→    item.provider === "github" ? FaGithub : FaDocker;
   221→
   222→  if (tracked) {
   223→    return (
   224→      <span
   225→        className="inline-flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5"
   226→        style={{
   227→          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   228→          fontSize: "12px",
   229→          color: "#16a34a",
   230→          backgroundColor: "#f0fdf4",
   231→          border: "1px solid #bbf7d0",
   232→          whiteSpace: "nowrap",
   233→        }}
   234→      >
   235→        <ProviderIcon className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />
   236→        {item.full_name}
   237→        <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
   238→      </span>
   239→    );
   240→  }
   241→
   242→  return (
   243→    <button
   244→      onClick={() => onTrack(item)}
   245→      disabled={tracking}
   246→      className="inline-flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 transition-all hover:shadow-sm disabled:opacity-70"
   247→      style={{
   248→        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   249→        fontSize: "12px",
   250→        color: "#111113",
   251→        backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   252→        border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   253→        whiteSpace: "nowrap",
   254→        cursor: tracking ? "wait" : "pointer",
   255→      }}
   256→    >
   257→      <ProviderIcon className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />
   258→      <span>{item.full_name}</span>
   259→      <Star className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   260→      <span style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontSize: "11px" }}>
   261→        {formatStars(item.stars)}
   262→      </span>
   263→      {tracking ? (
   264→        <Loader2
   265→          className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin"
   266→          style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   267→        />
   268→      ) : (
   269→        <Plus className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   270→      )}
   271→    </button>
   272→  );
   273→}
   274→

Done. Here's what changed:

1. **Removed** "Quick Onboard" from the sidebar navigation — no more dedicated tab
2. **Added** an inline "Quick Onboard" card on the Dashboard page, right below the Trending section — it matches the same card style (white bg, `#e8e8e5` border, Fraunces label) with a repo URL input and Scan button
3. **Dashboard flow**: User types a repo URL on the dashboard → clicks Scan (or hits Enter) → navigates to `/onboard?repo=...`
4. **Auto-start**: The `/onboard` page now reads the `?repo=` query param and automatically triggers the scan on load, skipping the input step

```
     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"errors"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
    12→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    13→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/onboard"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// OnboardSelection represents a user's choice for one dependency.
    17→type OnboardSelection struct {
    18→	DepName        string `json:"dep_name"`
    19→	UpstreamRepo   string `json:"upstream_repo"`
    20→	Provider       string `json:"provider"`
    21→	ProjectID      string `json:"project_id,omitempty"`
    22→	NewProjectName string `json:"new_project_name,omitempty"`
    23→}
    24→
    25→// OnboardApplyResult holds the outcome of applying scan selections.
    26→type OnboardApplyResult struct {
    27→	CreatedProjects []models.Project `json:"created_projects"`
    28→	CreatedSources  []models.Source  `json:"created_sources"`
    29→	Skipped         []string         `json:"skipped"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// OnboardStore defines the data access interface for onboarding operations.
    33→type OnboardStore interface {
    34→	CreateOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)
    35→	GetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)
    36→	UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error
    37→	ActiveScanForRepo(ctx context.Context, repoURL string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)
    38→	ApplyOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, scanID string, selections []OnboardSelection) (*OnboardApplyResult, error)
    39→}
    40→
    41→// OnboardHandler handles HTTP requests for repo onboarding.
    42→type OnboardHandler struct {
    43→	store OnboardStore
    44→}
    45→
    46→// NewOnboardHandler creates a new OnboardHandler.
    47→func NewOnboardHandler(store OnboardStore) *OnboardHandler {
    48→	return &OnboardHandler{store: store}
    49→}
    50→
    51→// Scan creates a new dependency scan for a GitHub repo.
    52→func (h *OnboardHandler) Scan(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    53→	var input struct {
    54→		RepoURL string `json:"repo_url"`
    55→	}
    56→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &input); err != nil {
    57→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", err.Error())
    58→		return
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Normalize the repo URL
    62→	owner, repo, err := onboard.ParseRepoURL(input.RepoURL)
    63→	if err != nil || strings.TrimSpace(input.RepoURL) == "" {
    64→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "validation_error", "Invalid or empty repo URL")
    65→		return
    66→	}
    67→	normalizedURL := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", owner, repo)
    68→
    69→	// Check for active scan
    70→	active, err := h.store.ActiveScanForRepo(r.Context(), normalizedURL)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", err.Error())
    73→		return
    74→	}
    75→	if active != nil {
    76→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusConflict, "conflict", fmt.Sprintf("Active scan already exists: %s", active.ID))
    77→		return
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	scan, err := h.store.CreateOnboardScan(r.Context(), normalizedURL)
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", err.Error())
    83→		return
    84→	}
    85→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusCreated, scan)
    86→}
    87→
    88→// GetScan returns a single scan by ID.
    89→func (h *OnboardHandler) GetScan(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    90→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    91→	scan, err := h.store.GetOnboardScan(r.Context(), id)
    92→	if err != nil {
    93→		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
    94→			RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Scan not found")
    95→			return
    96→		}
    97→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", err.Error())
    98→		return
    99→	}
   100→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, scan)
   101→}
   102→
   103→// Apply creates projects and sources from the user's selections.
   104→func (h *OnboardHandler) Apply(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   105→	id := r.PathValue("id")
   106→	var input struct {
   107→		Selections []OnboardSelection `json:"selections"`
   108→	}
   109→	if err := DecodeJSON(r, &input); err != nil {
   110→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", err.Error())
   111→		return
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	// Validate selections
   115→	for i, sel := range input.Selections {
   116→		hasProjectID := sel.ProjectID != ""
   117→		hasNewName := sel.NewProjectName != ""
   118→		if hasProjectID == hasNewName {
   119→			RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "validation_error",
   120→				fmt.Sprintf("selection[%d]: exactly one of project_id or new_project_name must be set", i))
   121→			return
   122→		}
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	result, err := h.store.ApplyOnboardScan(r.Context(), id, input.Selections)
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", err.Error())
   128→		return
   129→	}
   130→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, result)
   131→}
   132→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import React, { useState, useEffect, Suspense } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
     6→import { onboard as onboardApi, projects as projectsApi } from "@/lib/api/client";
     7→import type { OnboardScan, OnboardSelection, Project } from "@/lib/api/types";
     8→import { Loader2, Check, Search, ExternalLink, PackageSearch, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
     9→
    10→type Step = "input" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied";
    11→
    12→const ecosystemColors: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; border: string }> = {
    13→  go: { bg: "#ecfeff", text: "#0e7490", border: "#a5f3fc" },
    14→  npm: { bg: "#fef2f2", text: "#b91c1c", border: "#fecaca" },
    15→  pypi: { bg: "#fefce8", text: "#a16207", border: "#fde68a" },
    16→  cargo: { bg: "#fff7ed", text: "#c2410c", border: "#fed7aa" },
    17→  rubygems: { bg: "#fdf2f8", text: "#be185d", border: "#fbcfe8" },
    18→  maven: { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
    19→  gradle: { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
    20→  docker: { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
    21→  other: { bg: "#f3f3f1", text: "#6b7280", border: "#e8e8e5" },
    22→};
    23→
    24→export default function OnboardPage() {
    25→  return (
    26→    <Suspense>
    27→      <OnboardPageInner />
    28→    </Suspense>
    29→  );
    30→}
    31→
    32→function OnboardPageInner() {
    33→  const router = useRouter();
    34→  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
    35→  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("input");
    36→  const [repoUrl, setRepoUrl] = useState("");
    37→  const [scanId, setScanId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    38→  const [scan, setScan] = useState<OnboardScan | null>(null);
    39→  const [selections, setSelections] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
    40→  const [projectAssignments, setProjectAssignments] = useState<Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; projectId?: string; newName?: string }>>({});
    41→  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);
    42→  const [applyResult, setApplyResult] = useState<{ created_projects: Project[]; created_sources: any[]; skipped: string[] } | null>(null);
    43→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    44→  const [autoStarted, setAutoStarted] = useState(false);
    45→
    46→  // Fetch existing projects for the dropdown
    47→  const { data: projectsResp } = useSWR("projects-all", () => projectsApi.list(1, 200));
    48→  const existingProjects = projectsResp?.data ?? [];
    49→
    50→  // Poll scan status
    51→  useEffect(() => {
    52→    if (step !== "scanning" || !scanId) return;
    53→    const interval = setInterval(async () => {
    54→      try {
    55→        const resp = await onboardApi.getScan(scanId);
    56→        setScan(resp.data);
    57→        if (resp.data.status === "completed") {
    58→          setStep("results");
    59→          const deps = resp.data.results ?? [];
    60→          const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};
    61→          const assign: Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; newName?: string }> = {};
    62→          deps.forEach((d, i) => {
    63→            sel[i] = true;
    64→            assign[i] = { mode: "new", newName: d.name.replace(/\//g, "-") };
    65→          });
    66→          setSelections(sel);
    67→          setProjectAssignments(assign);
    68→          clearInterval(interval);
    69→        } else if (resp.data.status === "failed") {
    70→          setError(resp.data.error || "Scan failed");
    71→          setStep("input");
    72→          clearInterval(interval);
    73→        }
    74→      } catch {
    75→        // Keep polling
    76→      }
    77→    }, 2000);
    78→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
    79→  }, [step, scanId]);
    80→
    81→  // Auto-start scan from ?repo= query param
    82→  useEffect(() => {
    83→    const repo = searchParams.get("repo");
    84→    if (repo && !autoStarted) {
    85→      setAutoStarted(true);
    86→      setRepoUrl(repo);
    87→      (async () => {
    88→        setError(null);
    89→        try {
    90→          const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repo);
    91→          setScanId(resp.data.id);
    92→          setScan(resp.data);
    93→          setStep("scanning");
    94→        } catch (e: any) {
    95→          setError(e.message || "Failed to start scan");
    96→        }
    97→      })();
    98→    }
    99→  }, [searchParams, autoStarted]);
   100→
   101→  const startScan = async () => {
   102→    setError(null);
   103→    try {
   104→      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repoUrl);
   105→      setScanId(resp.data.id);
   106→      setScan(resp.data);
   107→      setStep("scanning");
   108→    } catch (e: any) {
   109→      setError(e.message || "Failed to start scan");
   110→    }
   111→  };
   112→
   113→  const applySelections = async () => {
   114→    if (!scan?.results || !scanId) return;
   115→    setApplying(true);
   116→    setError(null);
   117→
   118→    const sels: OnboardSelection[] = [];
   119→    scan.results.forEach((dep, i) => {
   120→      if (!selections[i]) return;
   121→      const assign = projectAssignments[i];
   122→      sels.push({
   123→        dep_name: dep.name,
   124→        upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,
   125→        provider: dep.provider,
   126→        project_id: assign?.mode === "existing" ? assign.projectId : undefined,
   127→        new_project_name: assign?.mode === "new" ? (assign.newName || dep.name.replace(/\//g, "-")) : undefined,
   128→      });
   129→    });
   130→
   131→    try {
   132→      const resp = await onboardApi.apply(scanId, sels);
   133→      setApplyResult(resp.data);
   134→      setStep("applied");
   135→    } catch (e: any) {
   136→      setError(e.message || "Failed to apply selections");
   137→    } finally {
   138→      setApplying(false);
   139→    }
   140→  };
   141→
   142→  const deps = scan?.results ?? [];
   143→  const selectedCount = Object.values(selections).filter(Boolean).length;
   144→
   145→  return (
   146→    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 fade-in">
   147→      {/* Header */}
   148→      <div>
   149→        <h1
   150→          style={{
   151→            fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   152→            fontSize: "24px",
   153→            fontWeight: 700,
   154→            color: "#111113",
   155→          }}
   156→        >
   157→          Quick Onboard
   158→        </h1>
   159→        <p
   160→          className="mt-1 text-[13px]"
   161→          style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   162→        >
   163→          Scan a GitHub repository to detect dependencies and start tracking their releases.
   164→        </p>
   165→      </div>
   166→
   167→      {error && (
   168→        <div
   169→          className="rounded-md px-4 py-3 text-[13px]"
   170→          style={{
   171→            backgroundColor: "#fef2f2",
   172→            border: "1px solid #fecaca",
   173→            color: "#b91c1c",
   174→            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   175→          }}
   176→        >
   177→          {error}
   178→        </div>
   179→      )}
   180→
   181→      {/* Step 1: Input */}
   182→      {step === "input" && (
   183→        <div
   184→          className="rounded-md px-6 py-8"
   185→          style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", backgroundColor: "#ffffff" }}
   186→        >
   187→          <div className="max-w-xl">
   188→            <label
   189→              className="block text-[13px] font-medium mb-2"
   190→              style={{ color: "#374151", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   191→            >
   192→              GitHub Repository URL
   193→            </label>
   194→            <div className="flex gap-2">
   195→              <input
   196→                type="text"
   197→                value={repoUrl}
   198→                onChange={(e) => setRepoUrl(e.target.value)}
   199→                placeholder="owner/repo or https://github.com/owner/repo"
   200→                className="flex-1 rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2"
   201→                style={{
   202→                  borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   203→                  color: "#111113",
   204→                  fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace",
   205→                  backgroundColor: "#ffffff",
   206→                }}
   207→                onFocus={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "#e8601a"; }}
   208→                onBlur={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "#e8e8e5"; }}
   209→                onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && repoUrl.trim() && startScan()}
   210→              />
   211→              <button
   212→                onClick={startScan}
   213→                disabled={!repoUrl.trim()}
   214→                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
   215→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   216→              >
   217→                <Search className="h-4 w-4" />
   218→                Scan
   219→              </button>
   220→            </div>
   221→            <p className="mt-2 text-[12px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   222→              We&apos;ll scan for go.mod, package.json, requirements.txt, Cargo.toml, and other dependency files.
   223→            </p>
   224→          </div>
   225→        </div>
   226→      )}
   227→
   228→      {/* Step 2: Scanning */}
   229→      {step === "scanning" && (
   230→        <div

   233→        >
   234→          <div

   236→            style={{ width: 48, height: 48, backgroundColor: "#fff7ed" }}
   237→          >
   238→            <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   239→          </div>
   240→          <p

   242→            style={{ color: "#111113", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   243→          >
   244→            Scanning repository for dependencies...
   245→          </p>
   246→          <p className="mt-1 text-[12px]" style={{ color: "#9ca3af" }}>
   247→            This may take a moment while we analyze dependency files.
   248→          </p>
   249→        </div>
   250→      )}
   251→
   252→      {/* Step 3: Results — empty */}
   253→      {step === "results" && deps.length === 0 && (

   257→        >

   259→          <p
   260→            className="text-[14px]"

   262→          >

   264→          </p>
   265→          <button
   266→            onClick={() => { setStep("input"); setRepoUrl(""); }}

   269→          >

   272→        </div>

   274→
   275→      {/* Step 3: Results — with deps */}
   276→      {step === "results" && deps.length > 0 && (

   282→            </p>
   283→            <button
   284→              onClick={applySelections}
   285→              disabled={selectedCount === 0 || applying}

   287→              style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   288→            >

   290→              Track Selected ({selectedCount})

   292→          </div>
   293→
   294→          <div
   295→            className="overflow-hidden rounded-md"

   297→          >

   299→              <thead>

   305→                      onChange={(e) => {

   313→                    />

   375→                          className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium"

   381→                        >

   383→                        </span>
   384→                      </td>
   385→                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
   386→                        <span className="text-[12px]" style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontFamily: "'JetBrains Mono', monospace" }}>
   387→                          {dep.upstream_repo}
   388→                        </span>
   389→                      </td>
   390→                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
   391→                        <select
   392→                          value={projectAssignments[i]?.mode === "existing" ? projectAssignments[i]?.projectId : "__new__"}
   393→                          onChange={(e) => {
   394→                            const val = e.target.value;
   395→                            if (val === "__new__") {
   396→                              setProjectAssignments({
   397→                                ...projectAssignments,
   398→                                [i]: { mode: "new", newName: dep.name.replace(/\//g, "-") },
   399→                              });
   400→                            } else {
   401→                              setProjectAssignments({

   403→                                [i]: { mode: "existing", projectId: val },
   404→                              });
   405→                            }
   406→                          }}
   407→                          className="rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[12px] bg-white"
   408→                          style={{
   409→                            borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   410→                            color: "#374151",
   411→                            fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",

   413→                        >

   428→
   429→      {/* Step 4: Applied */}
   430→      {step === "applied" && applyResult && (

   448→          {applyResult.skipped.length > 0 && (

   461→              </ul>
   462→            </div>
   463→          )}
   464→
   465→          <div className="flex gap-3">
   466→            <button
   467→              onClick={() => router.push("/projects")}
   468→              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
   469→              style={{ backgroundColor: "#e8601a", fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   470→            >
   471→              View Projects
   472→              <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
   473→            </button>
   474→            <button
   475→              onClick={() => { setStep("input"); setRepoUrl(""); setScanId(null); setScan(null); setApplyResult(null); }}
   476→              className="rounded-md border px-4 py-2 text-[13px] transition-colors hover:bg-[#f3f3f1]"
   477→              style={{
   478→                borderColor: "#e8e8e5",
   479→                color: "#374151",

     1→package onboard
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"log/slog"
     8→	"time"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
    11→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    12→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    13→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// ScanStore is the data access interface for the scan worker.
    17→type ScanStore interface {
    18→	GetOnboardScan(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.OnboardScan, error)
    19→	UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx context.Context, id, status string, results json.RawMessage, scanErr string) error
    20→}
    21→
    22→// ScanWorker processes scan_dependencies River jobs.
    23→type ScanWorker struct {
    24→	river.WorkerDefaults[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]
    25→	store     ScanStore
    26→	scanner   *Scanner
    27→	extractor *DependencyExtractor
    28→	pool      *pgxpool.Pool
    29→}
    30→
    31→// NewScanWorker creates a ScanWorker. extractor may be nil if GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set.
    32→func NewScanWorker(store ScanStore, scanner *Scanner, extractor *DependencyExtractor, pool *pgxpool.Pool) *ScanWorker {
    33→	return &ScanWorker{
    34→		store:     store,
    35→		scanner:   scanner,
    36→		extractor: extractor,
    37→		pool:      pool,
    38→	}
    39→}
    40→
    41→func (w *ScanWorker) Timeout(_ *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) time.Duration {
    42→	return 3 * time.Minute
    43→}
    44→
    45→func (w *ScanWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.ScanDependenciesJobArgs]) error {
    46→	scanID := job.Args.ScanID
    47→	slog.Info("scan worker picked up job", "scan_id", scanID, "attempt", job.Attempt)
    48→
    49→	// Load scan
    50→	scan, err := w.store.GetOnboardScan(ctx, scanID)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return fmt.Errorf("load scan %s: %w", scanID, err)
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	// Mark processing
    56→	if err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "processing", nil, ""); err != nil {
    57→		return fmt.Errorf("update status to processing: %w", err)
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Parse repo URL
    61→	owner, repo, err := ParseRepoURL(scan.RepoURL)
    62→	if err != nil {
    63→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, err.Error())
    64→		return fmt.Errorf("parse repo URL: %w", err)
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Fetch dependency files
    68→	files, err := w.scanner.FetchDependencyFiles(ctx, owner, repo)
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, err.Error())
    71→		return fmt.Errorf("fetch dependency files: %w", err)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	slog.Info("found dependency files", "scan_id", scanID, "count", len(files))
    75→
    76→	if len(files) == 0 {
    77→		// No dependency files — store empty results
    78→		emptyResults, _ := json.Marshal([]models.ScannedDependency{})
    79→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "completed", emptyResults, "")
    80→		w.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)
    81→		return nil
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Extract dependencies via LLM
    85→	if w.extractor == nil {
    86→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, "LLM not configured (set GOOGLE_API_KEY or OPENAI_API_KEY)")
    87→		return river.JobCancel(fmt.Errorf("extractor not configured"))
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	deps, err := w.extractor.Extract(ctx, files)
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "failed", nil, err.Error())
    93→		return fmt.Errorf("extract dependencies: %w", err)
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	slog.Info("extracted dependencies", "scan_id", scanID, "count", len(deps))
    97→
    98→	results, err := json.Marshal(deps)
    99→	if err != nil {
   100→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal results: %w", err)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	if err := w.store.UpdateOnboardScanStatus(ctx, scanID, "completed", results, ""); err != nil {
   104→		return fmt.Errorf("update scan completed: %w", err)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	w.notifyScanComplete(ctx, scanID)
   108→	return nil
   109→}
   110→
   111→// notifyScanComplete sends a pg_notify on the release_events channel.
   112→func (w *ScanWorker) notifyScanComplete(ctx context.Context, scanID string) {
   113→	payload := fmt.Sprintf(`{"type":"scan_complete","id":"%s"}`, scanID)
   114→	_, err := w.pool.Exec(ctx, "SELECT pg_notify('release_events', $1)", payload)
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		slog.Error("pg_notify failed", "scan_id", scanID, "err", err)
   117→	}
   118→}
   119→

  "old_string": "  // Auto-start scan from ?repo= query param\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const repo = searchParams.get(\"repo\");\n    if (repo && !autoStarted) {\n      setAutoStarted(true);\n      setRepoUrl(repo);\n      (async () => {\n        setError(null);\n        try {\n          const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repo);\n          setScanId(resp.data.id);\n          setScan(resp.data);\n          setStep(\"scanning\");\n        } catch (e: any) {\n          setError(e.message || \"Failed to start scan\");\n        }\n      })();\n    }\n  }, [searchParams, autoStarted]);\n\n  const startScan = async () => {\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(repoUrl);\n      setScanId(resp.data.id);\n      setScan(resp.data);\n      setStep(\"scanning\");\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      setError(e.message || \"Failed to start scan\");\n    }\n  };",
  "new_string": "  // Auto-start scan from ?repo= query param\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const repo = searchParams.get(\"repo\");\n    if (repo && !autoStarted) {\n      setAutoStarted(true);\n      setRepoUrl(repo);\n      beginScan(repo);\n    }\n  }, [searchParams, autoStarted]);\n\n  const beginScan = async (url: string) => {\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(url);\n      const scanData = resp.data;\n      setScanId(scanData.id);\n      setScan(scanData);\n      // If scan is already completed (resuming a finished scan), go straight to results\n      if (scanData.status === \"completed\") {\n        const deps = scanData.results ?? [];\n        const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};\n        const assign: Record<number, { mode: \"new\" | \"existing\"; newName?: string }> = {};\n        deps.forEach((d, i) => {\n          sel[i] = true;\n          assign[i] = { mode: \"new\", newName: d.name.replace(/\\//g, \"-\") };\n        });\n        setSelections(sel);\n        setProjectAssignments(assign);\n        setStep(\"results\");\n      } else if (scanData.status === \"failed\") {\n        setError(scanData.error || \"Scan failed\");\n        setStep(\"input\");\n      } else {\n        setStep(\"scanning\");\n      }\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      setError(e.message || \"Failed to start scan\");\n    }\n  };\n\n  const startScan = () => {\n    if (repoUrl.trim()) beginScan(repoUrl);\n  };"
}

(\n          <Check className=\"h-3 w-3\" style={{ color: \"#16a34a\" }} />\n        ) : active ? (\n          <Loader2 className=\"h-3 w-3 animate-spin\" style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }} />\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full\" style={{ backgroundColor: \"#d1d5db\" }} />\n        )}\n      </div>\n      <span\n        className=\"text-[12px]\"\n        style={{\n          color: done ? \"#16a34a\" : active ? \"#111113\" : \"#9ca3af\",\n          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n          fontWeight: active ? 500 : 400,\n        }}\n      >\n        {label}\n      </span>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction StepConnector({ done }: { done: boolean }) {\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"h-px\"\n      style={{\n        width: 24,\n        backgroundColor: done ? \"#16a34a\" : \"#e8e8e5\",\n      }}\n    />\n  );\n}"

> AGENT

Done. Here's what changed:

**Backend** (`internal/api/onboard.go`): On a 409 conflict (active scan already exists for the same repo), the endpoint now returns the existing scan object as JSON data instead of just an error message. This lets the frontend pick up where it left off.

**Frontend API client** (`web/lib/api/client.ts`): The `onboard.scan()` method now handles the 409 response specially — instead of throwing, it returns the existing scan data so the caller can resume tracking it.

**Frontend onboard page** (`web/app/onboard/page.tsx`):
- The `beginScan()` function checks the returned scan's status: if it's already `completed`, it jumps straight to results; if `failed`, it shows the error; otherwise it starts polling
- The scanning step now shows a **3-stage progress indicator**: `Queued → Fetching files → Extracting deps` with check/spinner/dot icons based on the current stage
- An **elapsed time counter** ticks every second during scanning
- The **repo URL** is shown during scanning for context
